# USBGuard Approval Manager - Configuration Example
# SRS v4.5 - All keys validated by config-reader.sh (no source/eval)

# TTL for temporary rules (seconds, 60-86400)
TEMP_TTL_SECONDS=3600

# Minimum reasonable epoch (2020-01-01)
MIN_REASONABLE_EPOCH=1577836800

# Maximum allowed clock jump (seconds)
MAX_CLOCK_JUMP_SECONDS=3600

# Number of backups to keep
BACKUP_KEEP=5

# IPC timeout for usbguard queries (seconds, 1-60)
IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=5

# Log level: DEBUG, INFO, WARNING, ERROR
LOG_LEVEL=INFO

# State directory
STATE_DIR=/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager

# Backup directory
BACKUP_DIR=/var/backups/usbguard-approval-manager

# Lock files (split-locking)
STATE_LOCK_FILE=/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager-state.lock
POLICY_LOCK_FILE=/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager-policy.lock

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared, testable helpers for transactional deployment.
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# עזרים משותפים להתקנה טרנזקציונית.
# ספרייה של פונקציות עזר הניתנות לבדיקה, לתהליכי התקנה ומחיקה טרנזקציוניים.
# מבטיחה שפעולות deployment הן אטומיות וניתנות להפיכה, ושמצב המערכת
# נשאר תקין לאורך כל תהליך ההתקנה או המחיקה.

DEPLOY_ROOT="/"
DEPLOY_STAGE=0
DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR=""
declare -ga DEPLOY_TRACKED_PATHS=()
declare -ga DEPLOY_TRACKED_KINDS=()
declare -ga DEPLOY_CREATED_DIRS=()

deploy_error() {
  printf 'deploy: ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
}

deploy_set_root() {
  local requested="${1:-/}"
  if [[ "$requested" == "/" ]]; then
    DEPLOY_ROOT="/"
    DEPLOY_STAGE=0
    return 0
  fi
  if [[ "$requested" != /* || "$requested" == *"/../"* ||
    "$requested" == */.. || ! -d "$requested" || -L "$requested" ]]; then
    deploy_error "staging root must be an existing absolute, non-symlink directory"
    return 1
  fi
  DEPLOY_ROOT="$(cd -- "$requested" && pwd -P)" || {
    deploy_error "unable to resolve staging root"
    return 1
  }
  if [[ "$DEPLOY_ROOT" == "/" ]]; then
    deploy_error "staging root cannot resolve to /"
    return 1
  fi
  DEPLOY_STAGE=1
}

deploy_path() {
  local path="$1"
  if [[ "$path" != /* || "$path" == *"/../"* || "$path" == */.. ]]; then
    deploy_error "managed path is not a normalized absolute path: $path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$DEPLOY_ROOT" == "/" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "$path"
  else
    printf '%s%s\n' "${DEPLOY_ROOT%/}" "$path"
  fi
}

deploy_expected_owner() {
  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    printf '%s:%s\n' "$(id -u)" "$(id -g)"
  else
    printf '0:%s\n' "$1"
  fi
}

deploy_group_id() {
  local group="$1"
  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    id -g
  else
    getent group "$group" | awk -F: 'NR == 1 { print $3 }'
  fi
}

deploy_begin_transaction() {
  local temp_base="${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"
  if [[ ! -d "$temp_base" || -L "$temp_base" ]]; then
    deploy_error "temporary directory is unavailable or unsafe"
    return 1
  fi
  DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR="$(mktemp -d "$temp_base/usbam-deploy.XXXXXX")" || {
    deploy_error "unable to create deployment rollback area"
    return 1
  }
  chmod 700 "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR"
  DEPLOY_TRACKED_PATHS=()
  DEPLOY_TRACKED_KINDS=()
  DEPLOY_CREATED_DIRS=()
}

deploy_track_path() {
  local path="$1"
  local destination index existing
  local i
  for i in "${!DEPLOY_TRACKED_PATHS[@]}"; do
    [[ "${DEPLOY_TRACKED_PATHS[$i]}" == "$path" ]] && return 0
  done
  destination="$(deploy_path "$path")" || return 1
  index="${#DEPLOY_TRACKED_PATHS[@]}"
  if [[ -L "$destination" ]]; then
    deploy_error "refusing to replace symbolic link: $path"
    return 1
  elif [[ -e "$destination" ]]; then
    existing="present"
    if ! cp -a -- "$destination" "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR/$index"; then
      deploy_error "unable to save rollback copy of $path"
      return 1
    fi
  else
    existing="absent"
  fi
  DEPLOY_TRACKED_PATHS+=("$path")
  DEPLOY_TRACKED_KINDS+=("$existing")
}

deploy_ensure_directory() {
  local path="$1" mode="$2" owner="$3" group="$4"
  local destination group_id expected actual
  mode="${mode#0}"
  destination="$(deploy_path "$path")" || return 1
  deploy_ensure_parent "$(dirname -- "$path")" "$owner" "$group" || return 1
  group_id="$(deploy_group_id "$group")" || {
    deploy_error "required group is unavailable: $group"
    return 1
  }
  expected="$(deploy_expected_owner "$group_id"):$mode"

  if [[ -L "$destination" ]]; then
    deploy_error "directory must not be a symbolic link: $path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -e "$destination" ]]; then
    if [[ ! -d "$destination" ]]; then
      deploy_error "path exists but is not a directory: $path"
      return 1
    fi
    actual="$(stat -c '%u:%g:%a' -- "$destination")" || return 1
    if [[ "$actual" != "$expected" ]]; then
      deploy_error "unsafe directory ownership or mode for $path (expected $expected, got $actual)"
      return 1
    fi
    return 0
  fi

  deploy_track_path "$path" || return 1
  if ! install -d -m "$mode" -o "$owner" -g "$group" -- "$destination"; then
    deploy_error "unable to create directory: $path"
    return 1
  fi
  DEPLOY_CREATED_DIRS+=("$path")
}

deploy_ensure_parent() {
  local path="$1" owner="${2:-root}" group="${3:-root}"
  local destination actual actual_owner mode parent
  destination="$(deploy_path "$path")" || return 1
  if [[ -L "$destination" ]]; then
    deploy_error "managed parent is a symbolic link: $path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "$destination" ]]; then
    if (( ! DEPLOY_STAGE )) || [[ "$path" == "/" ]]; then
      deploy_error "managed parent is missing or not a directory: $path"
      return 1
    fi
    parent="$(dirname -- "$path")"
    deploy_ensure_parent "$parent" "$owner" "$group" || return 1
    deploy_track_path "$path" || return 1
    if ! install -d -m 0755 -o "$owner" -g "$group" -- "$destination"; then
      deploy_error "unable to create staging parent: $path"
      return 1
    fi
    DEPLOY_CREATED_DIRS+=("$path")
  fi
  actual="$(stat -c '%u:%a' -- "$destination")" || return 1
  actual_owner="${actual%%:*}"
  mode="${actual##*:}"
  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    [[ "$actual_owner" == "$(id -u)" ]] || {
      deploy_error "staging parent is not owned by the current user: $path"
      return 1
    }
  elif [[ "$actual_owner" != "0" ]]; then
    deploy_error "managed parent must be root-owned and not group/world writable: $path"
    return 1
  elif (( (8#$mode & 0022) != 0 )); then
    if [[ "$path" == "/run/lock" ]] && (( (8#$mode & 01000) != 0 )); then
      return 0
    fi
    deploy_error "managed parent must be root-owned and not group/world writable: $path"
    return 1
  fi
}

deploy_install_file() {
  local source="$1" path="$2" mode="$3" owner="$4" group="$5"
  local destination parent temp group_id
  mode="${mode#0}"
  if [[ -L "$source" || ! -f "$source" || ! -r "$source" ]]; then
    deploy_error "source is missing, unreadable, or not a regular file: $source"
    return 1
  fi
  destination="$(deploy_path "$path")" || return 1
  parent="$(dirname -- "$destination")"
  if [[ ! -d "$parent" || -L "$parent" ]]; then
    deploy_error "destination parent is missing or unsafe: $path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -L "$destination" ]]; then
    deploy_error "refusing to replace symbolic link: $path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -f "$destination" ]] && cmp -s -- "$source" "$destination" &&
    [[ "$(stat -c '%a' -- "$destination")" == "$mode" ]]; then
    group_id="$(deploy_group_id "$group")" || return 1
    if [[ "$(stat -c '%u:%g' -- "$destination")" == "$(deploy_expected_owner "$group_id")" ]]; then
      return 0
    fi
  fi

  deploy_track_path "$path" || return 1
  temp="$(mktemp "$parent/.usbam-install.XXXXXX")" || {
    deploy_error "unable to stage installed file: $path"
    return 1
  }
  group_id="$(deploy_group_id "$group")" || {
    rm -f -- "$temp"
    return 1
  }
  if ! install -m "$mode" -o "$owner" -g "$group" -- "$source" "$temp"; then
    rm -f -- "$temp"
    deploy_error "unable to set installed file permissions: $path"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! mv -f -- "$temp" "$destination"; then
    rm -f -- "$temp"
    deploy_error "unable to atomically install: $path"
    return 1
  fi
}

deploy_rollback() {
  local i path destination
  local failed=0
  for ((i=${#DEPLOY_TRACKED_PATHS[@]}-1; i>=0; i--)); do
    path="${DEPLOY_TRACKED_PATHS[$i]}"
    destination="$(deploy_path "$path")" || { failed=1; continue; }
    if [[ -e "$destination" || -L "$destination" ]]; then
      if ! rm -rf -- "$destination"; then
        deploy_error "rollback could not remove changed path: $path"
        failed=1
        continue
      fi
    fi
    if [[ "${DEPLOY_TRACKED_KINDS[$i]}" == "present" ]]; then
      if ! cp -a -- "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR/$i" "$destination"; then
        deploy_error "rollback could not restore: $path"
        failed=1
      fi
    fi
  done
  for ((i=${#DEPLOY_CREATED_DIRS[@]}-1; i>=0; i--)); do
    path="${DEPLOY_CREATED_DIRS[$i]}"
    destination="$(deploy_path "$path")" || { failed=1; continue; }
    if [[ -d "$destination" ]] && ! rmdir -- "$destination" 2>/dev/null; then
      deploy_error "rollback left non-empty directory in place: $path"
      failed=1
    fi
  done
  if [[ -n "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR" && -d "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR" ]]; then
    if ! rm -rf -- "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR"; then
      deploy_error "unable to remove deployment rollback data"
      failed=1
    fi
  fi
  DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR=""
  return "$failed"
}

deploy_commit() {
  if [[ -n "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR" && -d "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR" ]]; then
    rm -rf -- "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR" || {
      deploy_error "unable to remove committed deployment rollback data"
      return 1
    }
  fi
  DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR=""
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Remove application code and integration files while preserving all user data.
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# הסר קוד יישום וקבצי אינטגרציה תוך שמירה על כל נתוני המשתמש.
# מבצע הסרה נקייה של קוד והתקניות תוך כדי שמירה על נתוני המשתמש,
# ההגדרות והנתונים האישיים. כל הנתונים המשתמשים נשמרים ומגובים.

PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/deploy-lib.sh"

LIBEXEC_REL="/usr/libexec/usbguard-approval-manager"
SERVICE_REL="/etc/systemd/system/usbguard-approval-ttl.service"
TIMER_REL="/etc/systemd/system/usbguard-approval-ttl.timer"
SUDOERS_REL="/etc/sudoers.d/usbguard-approval-manager"
LOGROTATE_REL="/etc/logrotate.d/usbguard-approval-manager"
REAPER_SERVICE_REL="/etc/systemd/system/usbguard-approval-manager-reaper.service"
REAPER_TIMER_REL="/etc/systemd/system/usbguard-approval-manager-reaper.timer"
TIMER_UNIT="usbguard-approval-ttl.timer"
SERVICE_UNIT="usbguard-approval-ttl.service"
REAPER_TIMER_UNIT="usbguard-approval-manager-reaper.timer"
REAPER_SERVICE_UNIT="usbguard-approval-manager-reaper.service"
WAS_ACTIVE=0
WAS_ENABLED=0
WAS_REAPER_ACTIVE=0
WAS_REAPER_ENABLED=0

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: deploy-uninstall.sh [--root STAGING_DIRECTORY] [--help]

Remove installed code, systemd units, sudoers and logrotate configuration.
The application configuration, TTL state, Audit log, backups and lock file
are always preserved.
EOF
}

parse_args() {
  local root="/"
  while (( $# )); do
    case "$1" in
      --root)
        if (( $# < 2 )) || [[ -z "$2" || "$root" != "/" ]]; then
          usage >&2
          return 7
        fi
        root="$2"
        shift 2
        ;;
      --help)
        if (( $# != 1 )); then usage >&2; return 7; fi
        usage
        exit 0
        ;;
      *)
        usage >&2
        return 7
        ;;
    esac
  done
  deploy_set_root "$root"
}

on_exit() {
  local status="$1"
  trap - EXIT
  if (( status != 0 )); then
    if [[ -n "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR" ]]; then
      printf 'uninstall: rolling back removed application files\n' >&2
      if ! deploy_rollback; then status=1; fi
      if (( ! DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
        systemctl daemon-reload || status=1
      fi
    fi
    if (( ! DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
      if (( WAS_ENABLED )); then systemctl enable "$TIMER_UNIT" || status=1; fi
      if (( WAS_ACTIVE )); then systemctl start "$TIMER_UNIT" || status=1; fi
      if (( WAS_REAPER_ENABLED )); then systemctl enable "$REAPER_TIMER_UNIT" || status=1; fi
      if (( WAS_REAPER_ACTIVE )); then systemctl start "$REAPER_TIMER_UNIT" || status=1; fi
    fi
  fi
  exit "$status"
}
trap 'on_exit $?' EXIT

wait_for_service_idle() {
  local attempt
  for ((attempt=0; attempt<60; attempt++)); do
    if ! systemctl is-active --quiet "$SERVICE_UNIT"; then
      return 0
    fi
    sleep 1
  done
  deploy_error "TTL service did not finish within 60 seconds; files were not removed"
  return 1
}

confirm_uninstall() {
  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then return 0; fi
  if (( EUID != 0 )); then
    deploy_error "live uninstallation requires root"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -t 0 ]]; then
    deploy_error "live uninstallation requires an interactive confirmation"
    return 1
  fi
  printf 'Remove application code and service integrations? Config, TTL, Audit and backups are preserved. [y/N] '
  local answer
  IFS= read -r answer || return 1
  case "$answer" in
    y|Y|yes|YES) ;;
    *) printf 'Uninstallation cancelled.\n'; return 130 ;;
  esac
}

stop_timer_safely() {
  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then return 0; fi
  command -v systemctl >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
    deploy_error "systemctl is unavailable"
    return 1
  }
  if systemctl is-enabled --quiet "$TIMER_UNIT"; then WAS_ENABLED=1; fi
  if systemctl is-active --quiet "$TIMER_UNIT"; then
    WAS_ACTIVE=1
    systemctl stop "$TIMER_UNIT" || {
      deploy_error "unable to stop the TTL timer"
      return 1
    }
  fi
  if systemctl is-enabled --quiet "$REAPER_TIMER_UNIT"; then WAS_REAPER_ENABLED=1; fi
  if systemctl is-active --quiet "$REAPER_TIMER_UNIT"; then
    WAS_REAPER_ACTIVE=1
    systemctl stop "$REAPER_TIMER_UNIT" || {
      deploy_error "unable to stop the reaper timer"
      return 1
    }
  fi
  wait_for_service_idle
  if (( WAS_ENABLED )); then
    systemctl disable "$TIMER_UNIT" || {
      deploy_error "unable to disable the TTL timer"
      return 1
    }
  fi
  if (( WAS_REAPER_ENABLED )); then
    systemctl disable "$REAPER_TIMER_UNIT" || {
      deploy_error "unable to disable the reaper timer"
      return 1
    }
  fi
}

remove_managed_path() {
  local relative="$1" path
  path="$(deploy_path "$relative")" || return 1
  [[ -e "$path" || -L "$path" ]] || return 0
  deploy_track_path "$relative" || return 1
  if [[ -d "$path" && ! -L "$path" ]]; then
    rm -rf -- "$path"
  else
    rm -f -- "$path"
  fi
}

main() {
  parse_args "$@" || exit $?
  confirm_uninstall || exit $?
  stop_timer_safely
  deploy_begin_transaction

  remove_managed_path "$LIBEXEC_REL"
  remove_managed_path "$SERVICE_REL"
  remove_managed_path "$TIMER_REL"
  remove_managed_path "$REAPER_SERVICE_REL"
  remove_managed_path "$REAPER_TIMER_REL"
  remove_managed_path "$SUDOERS_REL"
  remove_managed_path "$LOGROTATE_REL"

  if (( ! DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    systemctl daemon-reload || {
      deploy_error "systemd daemon-reload failed"
      return 1
    }
  fi
  deploy_commit
  printf 'Application files removed. Configuration, TTL state, Audit, backups and lock were preserved.\n'
}

main "$@"

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Install the application without changing USBGuard policy or enabling units.
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# התקנת היישום ללא שינוי מדיניות USBGuard או הפעלת units.
# מבצע התקנה נקייה של הקוד והקונפיגורציה תוך כדי שמירה על המדיניות הקיימת.
# הסקריפט אינו משנה את חוקי ה-USBGuard הקיימים ואינו ממפעיל systemd/systemctl units.
#
# תלויים: deploy-lib.sh, config-reader.sh, validators.sh

PROJECT_ROOT="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/deploy-lib.sh"
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/config-reader.sh"
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/validators.sh"
source "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/lib/usbguard-policy.sh"

CONFIG_REL="/etc/usbguard-approval-manager/approval-manager.conf"
STATE_REL="/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager"
BACKUP_REL="/var/backups/usbguard-approval-manager"
LOG_REL="/var/log/usbguard-approval-manager"
LOCK_REL="/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager.lock"
LIBEXEC_REL="/usr/libexec/usbguard-approval-manager"
SERVICE_REL="/etc/systemd/system/usbguard-approval-ttl.service"
TIMER_REL="/etc/systemd/system/usbguard-approval-ttl.timer"
SUDOERS_REL="/etc/sudoers.d/usbguard-approval-manager"
LOGROTATE_REL="/etc/logrotate.d/usbguard-approval-manager"
REAPER_SERVICE_REL="/etc/systemd/system/usbguard-approval-manager-reaper.service"
REAPER_TIMER_REL="/etc/systemd/system/usbguard-approval-manager-reaper.timer"
CONFIG_CONF_REL="/etc/usbguard-approval-manager/config/usbguard-approval-manager.conf.example"
DEPLOY_ABORTING=0

usage() {
  cat <<'EOF'
Usage: deploy.sh [--root STAGING_DIRECTORY] [--help]

Install application files and protected data directories. A staging root is
for packaging/tests only; it must already exist and cannot be /. A live
installation requires root, USBGuard IPC, and an interactive confirmation.
The installer never edits USBGuard rules or enables/starts the TTL timer.
EOF
}

parse_args() {
  local root="/"
  while (( $# )); do
    case "$1" in
      --root)
        if (( $# < 2 )) || [[ -z "$2" || "$root" != "/" ]]; then
          usage >&2
          return 7
        fi
        root="$2"
        shift 2
        ;;
      --help)
        if (( $# != 1 )); then usage >&2; return 7; fi
        usage
        exit 0
        ;;
      *)
        usage >&2
        return 7
        ;;
    esac
  done
  deploy_set_root "$root"
}

on_exit() {
  local status="$1"
  trap - EXIT
  if (( status != 0 )) && [[ -n "$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR" ]] &&
    (( DEPLOY_ABORTING == 0 )); then
    DEPLOY_ABORTING=1
    printf 'deploy: rolling back the incomplete installation\n' >&2
    if ! deploy_rollback; then
      printf 'deploy: ERROR: rollback was incomplete; inspect the reported paths\n' >&2
      status=1
    fi
    if (( ! DEPLOY_STAGE )) && command -v systemctl >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      systemctl daemon-reload || {
        printf 'deploy: ERROR: systemd could not reload the restored units\n' >&2
        status=1
      }
    fi
  fi
  exit "$status"
}
trap 'on_exit $?' EXIT

require_command() {
  command -v "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
    deploy_error "required command is unavailable: $1"
    return 1
  }
}

preflight_live_host() {
  local available_kb needed_kb version config_path os_release distro_id distro_version
  local -a required=(bash python3 jq flock timeout usbguard systemctl systemd-analyze visudo logrotate install stat df du awk diff whiptail)
  local command_name

  if (( EUID != 0 )); then
    deploy_error "live installation requires root"
    return 1
  fi
  for command_name in "${required[@]}"; do
    require_command "$command_name" || return 1
  done
  if ! getent group usbadmins >/dev/null; then
    deploy_error "the required usbadmins group does not exist"
    return 1
  fi
  os_release="$(deploy_path /etc/os-release)"
  if [[ -L "$os_release" || ! -f "$os_release" || ! -r "$os_release" ]]; then
    deploy_error "the target distribution cannot be identified from /etc/os-release"
    return 1
  fi
  distro_id="$(awk -F= '$1 == "ID" { gsub(/^"|"$/, "", $2); print $2; exit }' "$os_release")"
  distro_version="$(awk -F= '$1 == "VERSION_ID" { gsub(/^"|"$/, "", $2); print $2; exit }' "$os_release")"
  if [[ -z "$distro_id" ]]; then
    deploy_error "the target distribution identifier is missing"
    return 1
  fi
  printf 'Target distribution: %s %s\n' "$distro_id" "${distro_version:-version-unknown}"
  if ! usbguard --version >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    deploy_error "USBGuard is unavailable"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! timeout --foreground 5s usbguard list-rules >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    deploy_error "USBGuard IPC is unavailable; refusing deployment"
    return 1
  fi

  config_path="$(deploy_path "$CONFIG_REL")"
  if [[ -L "$config_path" || ( -e "$config_path" && ! -f "$config_path" ) ]]; then
    deploy_error "existing application configuration is not a regular file"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -f "$config_path" ]]; then
    load_config "$config_path" || {
      deploy_error "existing application configuration is invalid; it was not replaced"
      return 1
    }
  else
    load_config "$PROJECT_ROOT/conf/approval-manager.conf.example" || return 1
  fi

  local state_path ttl_path
  state_path="$(deploy_path "$STATE_REL")"
  ttl_path="$state_path/ttl.json"
  if [[ -L "$state_path" || ( -e "$state_path" && ! -d "$state_path" ) ]]; then
    deploy_error "existing state path is not a directory"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -L "$ttl_path" || ( -e "$ttl_path" && ! -f "$ttl_path" ) ]]; then
    deploy_error "existing ttl.json is not a regular file"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -f "$ttl_path" ]] && ! validate_ttl_json "$ttl_path"; then
    deploy_error "existing ttl.json is invalid; state was not replaced"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! get_usbguard_version; then
    deploy_error "unable to determine the USBGuard version"
    return 1
  fi
  version="$USBAM_POLICY_VERSION"
  if ! get_persistent_policy_paths; then
    deploy_error "unable to read the active USBGuard policy configuration"
    return 1
  fi
  printf 'USBGuard version: %s\n' "$version"
  printf 'RuleFile: %s\n' "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]:-not configured}"
  printf 'RuleFolder: %s\n' "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]:-not configured}"
  printf 'USBGuard policy diff: empty; policy files are not installation targets and no rules will be changed.\n'

  available_kb="$(df -Pk "$DEPLOY_ROOT" | awk 'NR == 2 { print $4 }')"
  needed_kb="$(du -sk "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts" "$PROJECT_ROOT/systemd" \
    "$PROJECT_ROOT/sudoers" "$PROJECT_ROOT/logrotate" "$PROJECT_ROOT/config" | awk '{ total += $1 } END { print total + 1024 }')"
  if [[ ! "$available_kb" =~ ^[0-9]+$ || ! "$needed_kb" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] ||
    (( available_kb < needed_kb )); then
    deploy_error "insufficient disk space for installation"
    return 1
  fi

  local policy_path
  for policy_path in "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]:-}" \
    "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]:-}"; do
    [[ -n "$policy_path" ]] || continue
    if [[ -e "$policy_path" ]]; then
      printf 'policy_source_sha256=%s  %s\n' \
        "$(sha256sum -- "$policy_path" 2>/dev/null | awk '{ print $1 }' || printf 'unavailable')" \
        "$policy_path"
    fi
  done
}

confirm_live_install() {
  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    return 0
  fi
  if [[ ! -t 0 ]]; then
    deploy_error "live installation requires an interactive confirmation"
    return 1
  fi
  printf 'Install USBGuard Approval Manager? Existing configuration, TTL, Audit and backups are preserved. [y/N] '
  local answer
  IFS= read -r answer || return 1
  case "$answer" in
    y|Y|yes|YES) ;;
    *) printf 'Installation cancelled.\n'; return 130 ;;
  esac
}

show_application_diff() {
  local source target name result changed=0
  shopt -s nullglob
  for source in "$PROJECT_ROOT"/scripts/*.sh "$PROJECT_ROOT"/scripts/lib/*; do
    name="$(basename -- "$source")"
    if [[ "$source" == "$PROJECT_ROOT"/scripts/lib/* ]]; then
      target="$LIBEXEC_REL/lib/$name"
    else
      target="$LIBEXEC_REL/$name"
    fi
    target="$(deploy_path "$target")"
    if [[ -L "$target" ]]; then
      shopt -u nullglob
      deploy_error "installed application path is a symbolic link: $target"
      return 1
    fi
    if [[ -f "$target" ]] && ! cmp -s -- "$source" "$target"; then
      changed=1
      printf '\n--- installed update: %s ---\n' "$target"
      diff -u -- "$target" "$source" || result=$?
      if [[ "${result:-0}" -gt 1 ]]; then
        shopt -u nullglob
        deploy_error "unable to compare installed file: $target"
        return 1
      fi
      result=0
    fi
  done
  shopt -u nullglob
  if (( changed == 0 )); then
    printf 'Application file diff: empty (new install or already current).\n'
  fi
}

verify_managed_directories() {
  local root_group="root" app_owner="root" admins_group="usbadmins"
  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    app_owner="$(id -un)"
    root_group="$(id -gn)"
    admins_group="$root_group"
  fi

  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    deploy_ensure_directory /usr/libexec 0755 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
    deploy_ensure_directory /etc/systemd/system 0755 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
    deploy_ensure_directory /etc/sudoers.d 0750 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
    deploy_ensure_directory /etc/logrotate.d 0755 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
    deploy_ensure_directory /run/lock 0755 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  else
    deploy_ensure_parent /usr/libexec
    deploy_ensure_parent /etc/systemd/system
    deploy_ensure_parent /etc/sudoers.d
    deploy_ensure_parent /etc/logrotate.d
    deploy_ensure_parent /run/lock
  fi
  deploy_ensure_directory "$LIBEXEC_REL" 0750 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  deploy_ensure_directory "$LIBEXEC_REL/lib" 0750 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  deploy_ensure_directory /etc/usbguard-approval-manager 0750 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  deploy_ensure_directory "$STATE_REL" 0700 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  deploy_ensure_directory "$BACKUP_REL" 0700 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  deploy_ensure_directory "$LOG_REL" 0750 "$app_owner" "$admins_group"
}

install_managed_files() {
  local app_owner="root" root_group="root" admins_group="usbadmins"
  local file name target mode source_path
  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    app_owner="$(id -un)"
    root_group="$(id -gn)"
    admins_group="$root_group"
  fi

  shopt -s nullglob
  for file in "$PROJECT_ROOT"/scripts/*.sh; do
    [[ -f "$file" && ! -L "$file" ]] || {
      deploy_error "unexpected non-regular script source"
      return 1
    }
    name="$(basename -- "$file")"
    target="$LIBEXEC_REL/$name"
    deploy_install_file "$file" "$target" 0750 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  done
  for file in "$PROJECT_ROOT"/scripts/lib/*; do
    [[ -f "$file" && ! -L "$file" ]] || {
      deploy_error "unexpected non-regular library source"
      return 1
    }
    name="$(basename -- "$file")"
    target="$LIBEXEC_REL/lib/$name"
    mode=0640
    [[ "$name" == *.sh ]] && mode=0640
    deploy_install_file "$file" "$target" "$mode" "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  done
  shopt -u nullglob

  local config_path state_path log_path lock_path
  config_path="$(deploy_path "$CONFIG_REL")"
  state_path="$(deploy_path "$STATE_REL")"
  log_path="$(deploy_path "$LOG_REL")"
  lock_path="$(deploy_path "$LOCK_REL")"

  if [[ ! -e "$config_path" ]]; then
    deploy_install_file "$PROJECT_ROOT/conf/approval-manager.conf.example" \
      "$CONFIG_REL" 0600 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  fi
  if [[ ! -e "$state_path/ttl.json" ]]; then
    source_path="$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR/initial-ttl.json"
    printf '%s\n' '{"schema_version":1,"records":[]}' > "$source_path"
    chmod 600 "$source_path"
    deploy_install_file "$source_path" "$STATE_REL/ttl.json" 0600 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  fi
  if [[ ! -e "$log_path/audit.jsonl" ]]; then
    source_path="$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR/initial-audit.jsonl"
    : > "$source_path"
    chmod 600 "$source_path"
    deploy_install_file "$source_path" "$LOG_REL/audit.jsonl" \
      0640 "$app_owner" "$admins_group"
  fi
  if [[ ! -e "$lock_path" ]]; then
    source_path="$DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_DIR/initial-lock"
    : > "$source_path"
    chmod 600 "$source_path"
    deploy_install_file "$source_path" "$LOCK_REL" 0600 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  fi

  deploy_install_file "$PROJECT_ROOT/systemd/usbguard-approval-ttl.service" \
    "$SERVICE_REL" 0644 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  deploy_install_file "$PROJECT_ROOT/systemd/usbguard-approval-ttl.timer" \
    "$TIMER_REL" 0644 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  deploy_install_file "$PROJECT_ROOT/sudoers/usbguard-approval-manager" \
    "$SUDOERS_REL" 0440 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  deploy_install_file "$PROJECT_ROOT/logrotate/usbguard-approval-manager" \
    "$LOGROTATE_REL" 0644 "$app_owner" "$root_group"

  deploy_install_file "$PROJECT_ROOT/systemd/usbguard-approval-manager-reaper.service" \
    "$REAPER_SERVICE_REL" 0644 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  deploy_install_file "$PROJECT_ROOT/systemd/usbguard-approval-manager-reaper.timer" \
    "$REAPER_TIMER_REL" 0644 "$app_owner" "$root_group"

  if [[ ! -e "$(deploy_path "$CONFIG_CONF_REL")" ]]; then
    deploy_install_file "$PROJECT_ROOT/config/usbguard-approval-manager.conf.example" \
      "$CONFIG_CONF_REL" 0600 "$app_owner" "$root_group"
  fi
}

verify_installed_files() {
  local systemd_root="$DEPLOY_ROOT" result
  if command -v visudo >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    local sudoers_path
    sudoers_path="$(deploy_path "$SUDOERS_REL")"
    visudo -c -f "$sudoers_path" || {
      deploy_error "sudoers validation failed"
      return 1
    }
  elif (( ! DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    deploy_error "visudo is required to validate the sudoers policy"
    return 1
  else
    printf 'UNVERIFIED: visudo is unavailable in staging; sudoers syntax check skipped\n'
  fi

  if command -v systemd-analyze >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    local service_path timer_path
    service_path="$(deploy_path "$SERVICE_REL")"
    timer_path="$(deploy_path "$TIMER_REL")"
    if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
      systemd-analyze --root="$systemd_root" verify "$service_path" "$timer_path" || {
        deploy_error "systemd unit validation failed"
        return 1
      }
    else
      systemd-analyze verify "$service_path" "$timer_path" || {
        deploy_error "systemd unit validation failed"
        return 1
      }
    fi
  elif (( ! DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    deploy_error "systemd-analyze is required to validate service units"
    return 1
  else
    printf 'UNVERIFIED: systemd-analyze is unavailable in staging; unit syntax check skipped\n'
  fi

  if command -v logrotate >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    local logrotate_path
    logrotate_path="$(deploy_path "$LOGROTATE_REL")"
    logrotate --debug --state /dev/null "$logrotate_path" >/dev/null || {
      deploy_error "logrotate configuration validation failed"
      return 1
    }
  elif (( ! DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    deploy_error "logrotate is required to validate log rotation"
    return 1
  else
    printf 'UNVERIFIED: logrotate is unavailable in staging; syntax check skipped\n'
  fi

  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    if USBAM_ROOT="$DEPLOY_ROOT" bash "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/validate_all.sh" --static; then
      result=0
    else
      result=$?
    fi
  else
    if bash "$PROJECT_ROOT/scripts/validate_all.sh" --static; then
      result=0
    else
      result=$?
    fi
  fi
  (( result == 0 )) || {
    deploy_error "static installation validation failed"
    return 1
  }
}

main() {
  parse_args "$@" || exit $?
  if (( ! DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    preflight_live_host
    show_application_diff
  fi
  confirm_live_install || exit $?
  deploy_begin_transaction
  verify_managed_directories
  install_managed_files
  verify_installed_files

  if (( ! DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    systemctl daemon-reload || {
      deploy_error "systemd daemon-reload failed"
      return 1
    }
  fi

  deploy_commit
  printf 'Installation completed. Existing config, TTL state, Audit and backups were preserved.\n'
  printf 'The TTL timer activation state was not changed; enable it only after required G-01 and release gates are approved.\n'
  if (( DEPLOY_STAGE )); then
    printf 'Staging root: %s (no host services, devices or policy were changed).\n' "$DEPLOY_ROOT"
  fi
}

main "$@"

# USBGuard Approval Manager

A command-line and text-interface manager for controlled temporary and
permanent USBGuard approvals.

## Requirements baseline

Implementation follows the approved SRS v4.5 baseline in [../USB.md](../USB.md).
The prior v4.4 specification is retained as historical reference.
G-01 and restore/deployment safety evidence remain mandatory gates before
implementing or enabling dependent policy-changing operations.

The application code, configuration, deployment units, and tests are kept in
this directory. The specification, implementation roadmap, and prompt catalog
remain at the repository root.

## Project status

The foundation includes a strict configuration reader, shared result model,
atomic JSON writer, process lock, separate operational/Audit logging, and
calendar clock guards with focused unit tests. The USBGuard adapter includes
read and policy-changing operations, but every mutation is fail-closed behind
a root-owned, mode-`0600` G-01 evidence marker and the shared process lock.
Callers must hold the lock for the entire mutation operation. No marker is
included in this project. The TTL stages and Reaper are implemented and
mock-tested; the Reaper preserves rules found in either configured persistent
policy source (`RuleFile` or `RuleFolder`) and requires recovery when clock
trust or policy-source inspection fails. Live TTL/Reaper behavior remains
unverified. The time guard recognizes NTP synchronization evidence, but the
target's trust approval and `MAX_CLOCK_JUMP_SECONDS` threshold are still
outstanding, so G-01 remains unverified.

## Development rules

- Keep components small, modular, and focused on one responsibility.
- Preserve the approved SRS baseline and stop on unresolved security behavior.
- Do not change USBGuard policy on the development host.
- Run policy-changing integration tests only in an isolated lab VM with
  USB passthrough and a verified recovery route.

## Local checks

From this directory in Linux/WSL, run the syntax checks and all unit tests with:

```bash
make test
```

The unit-test target uses mocked USBGuard commands; it does not change policy
or verify G-01 against a live daemon. The G-01 integration test uses a
temporary daemon configuration and policy but does change the selected
physical USB device state. Run it only in the isolated lab VM, with the
installed service inactive, the USB disk unmounted, and recovery available.
The live test uses the USBGuard CLI directly because the adapter must remain
gated until G-01 is approved; mocked unit tests cover the adapter methods.
It verifies kernel authorization and the USB disk node as well as USBGuard's
reported device state. After stopping its isolated daemon, it restores the
test USB's initial kernel authorization, waits up to 10 seconds for the
corresponding disk presence or absence, and verifies that no test disk is
mounted.
Override `BASH` or `PYTHON` if needed, for example
`make test PYTHON=python3.13`.

The quick status scripts are read-only except for private report files:

```bash
sudo env USBAM_G01_EXPECTED_HOSTNAME=vbox bash tests/quick/g01_status_check.sh
sudo env USBAM_TEST_USB_ID=048d:1234 bash tests/quick/usb_device_check.sh
sudo bash tests/quick/clock_time_check.sh
```

For monitoring, `usbguard-status.sh --json` writes one versioned status
document. `--watch [SECONDS]` emits compact newline-delimited JSON snapshots
(default interval: 5 seconds; valid range: 1-3600), suitable for terminal or
pipe consumers:

```bash
bash scripts/usbguard-status.sh --json
bash scripts/usbguard-status.sh --watch 10
```

## Approving USB devices

`scripts/usb-approve.sh` is the interactive and batch entry point. It fetches
the current blocked-device list, validates the requested IDs, then re-fetches
and compares each selected device while holding the global lock before any
policy mutation. Each approval requires an explicit confirmation.

```bash
# Temporary approval (default TTL comes from TEMP_TTL_SECONDS)
sudo bash scripts/usb-approve.sh --device 3 --type temporary

# Temporary approval with an explicit TTL
sudo bash scripts/usb-approve.sh --device 3 --type temporary --ttl 3600

# Permanent approval
sudo bash scripts/usb-approve.sh --device 3 --type permanent

# Batch approval: each device is processed and reported separately
sudo bash scripts/usb-approve.sh --device 3 --device 4 --type temporary --ttl 3600

# Read-only plan; no policy, TTL, or Audit writes
scripts/usb-approve.sh --device 3 --type temporary --ttl 3600 --dry-run
```

Omit `--device` to select blocked devices through the `whiptail` interface.
The confirmation screen includes preflight warnings for existing precedence,
duplicate, or persistent-rule conflicts; conflicting batch items are skipped
and reported individually while eligible items may continue.
Temporary approvals separately run `allow-device` for the currently connected
device and add a TTL policy rule for future matching devices. Permanent
approvals add and verify the persistent rule and separately allow the
connected device. A live policy write still requires root, the G-01 evidence
marker, the global lock, a functioning USBGuard daemon, and protected Audit
storage. The CLI does not create or bypass the G-01 marker; until the required
lab evidence is approved, write operations remain disabled.

`scripts/usbguard-status.sh --json` provides the versioned monitoring
contract (`schema_version: 1`), with daemon/IPC/timer health, TTL state counts,
and separate policy and Audit result objects. It is read-only and reports an
open restore journal as `service_status=degraded` with
`detail_code=restore_recovery_required`.

The G-01 integration wrapper is not a status-only check: it changes the
selected USB's kernel authorization in the isolated lab. It requires explicit
hostname, device-ID, write, and confirmation values and refuses to stop or
modify the installed USBGuard service:

```bash
sudo env USBAM_TEST_USB_ID=048d:1234 USBAM_G01_EXPECTED_HOSTNAME=vbox \
  USBAM_G01_ALLOW_WRITE=1 USBAM_G01_CONFIRM=YES \
  bash tests/quick/run_g01_integration.sh
```

Audit writes require the protected `/var/log/usbguard-approval-manager`
directory owned by `root:usbadmins` with mode `0750`; the Audit file is
`root:usbadmins` mode `0640`. The logger reports write failures to its caller,
which must preserve any already-applied policy result.

## Backup, restore, and policy exchange

Phase 5 provides validated JSON exchange and private `tar.gz` backups:

```bash
bash scripts/export-rules.sh > export.json
sudo bash scripts/import-rules.sh --file export.json
sudo bash scripts/backup-rules.sh
sudo bash scripts/restore-rules.sh --backup /var/backups/usbguard-approval-manager/backup.tar.gz
sudo bash scripts/restore-rules.sh --recover
```

Exports contain only validated permanent `allow` rules from non-system policy
sources and active TTL rules. Import validates the complete schema in a private
staging directory, displays a diff, requires an interactive confirmation, then
rechecks policy and TTL state under the global lock. Expired TTL entries are
reported as `SKIPPED - expired`; imported TTL entries receive new managed and
correlation IDs. All policy writes remain gated by the G-01 evidence marker.

Backups include the application configuration, TTL state, and approved export
only. Their manifest has a closed payload allowlist, byte sizes, modes, and
SHA-256 digests; backup files are private and rotated according to
`BACKUP_KEEP`. Archives are validated before extraction; absolute paths,
traversal, links, unexpected members, and integrity mismatches are rejected.

Restore is deliberately restricted to a configured `RuleFile`; if `RuleFolder`
is configured, it must contain no non-system files. The daemon must be proven
stopped, G-01 must be verified, and an interactive diff must be approved before
the transaction writes anything. Files are staged and atomically replaced
under the lock, with durable rollback copies and a journal. If the journal
exists, policy/state mutations remain blocked until `restore-rules.sh
--recover` verifies and restores the previous files. Restored non-expired TTL
records are marked `recovery_required`; restore never reapplies a temporary
rule or approves/blocks a device. These restrictions avoid replacing an
unmanaged policy source or letting USBGuard consume a mixed policy set.

`time-guards.sh` does not set or adjust the system clock. Its unit tests use
simulated provider evidence and clock samples; they do not prove that a real
time-sync source is trustworthy. The live VM's current synchronization is
evidence only, not approval of that source or the example jump threshold.

Before any real G-01 tests, complete [docs/lab-checklist.md](docs/lab-checklist.md).
The checklist is planning guidance, not evidence that the VM is currently ready.
The integration test does not restart the installed service or create the
verification marker. Keep G-01 `UNVERIFIED` until the clock and reboot cases
are independently completed and reviewed.
The latest VM observations are recorded in
[docs/g01-preflight-report.md](docs/g01-preflight-report.md); G-01 remains
`UNVERIFIED`.

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Create a private, integrity-checked Phase 5 backup archive.

set -uo pipefail
umask 077

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
LIB_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/lib"
PYTHON3="${PYTHON3:-python3}"
CONFIG_PATH="${USBAM_CONFIG_PATH:-/etc/usbguard-approval-manager/approval-manager.conf}"
STATE_DIR="${USBAM_STATE_DIR:-/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager}"
TTL_FILE="${USBAM_TTL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/ttl.json}"
RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE="${USBAM_RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/restore_journal.json}"
LOCK_FILE="${USBAM_LOCK_FILE:-/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager.lock}"
BACKUP_DIR="${USBAM_BACKUP_DIR:-/var/backups/usbguard-approval-manager}"
USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH="${USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH:-/etc/usbguard/usbguard-daemon.conf}"

usage() {
  printf 'Usage: %s [--help]\nCreates a validated backup in %s.\n' "$0" "$BACKUP_DIR"
}

if (( $# )); then
  if (( $# == 1 )) && [[ "$1" == "--help" ]]; then
    usage
    exit 0
  fi
  usage >&2
  exit 7
fi
if (( EUID != 0 )); then
  printf 'backup-rules: ERROR: root privileges are required\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi

source "$LIB_DIR/stages.sh"
source "$LIB_DIR/lifecycle.sh"
STATE_DIR="${USBAM_STATE_DIR:-/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager}"
TTL_FILE="${USBAM_TTL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/ttl.json}"
RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE="${USBAM_RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/restore_journal.json}"

if [[ -L "$CONFIG_PATH" ]]; then
  printf 'backup-rules: ERROR: configuration must not be a symbolic link\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi
if [[ -f "$CONFIG_PATH" ]]; then
  CONFIG_SOURCE="$CONFIG_PATH"
else
  CONFIG_SOURCE="$SCRIPT_DIR/../conf/approval-manager.conf.example"
fi
if [[ ! -f "$CONFIG_SOURCE" || ! -r "$CONFIG_SOURCE" ]] || ! load_config "$CONFIG_SOURCE"; then
  printf 'backup-rules: ERROR: configuration is missing or invalid\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi

if ! lock_acquire 5; then
  printf 'backup-rules: ERROR: unable to acquire the state lock\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi
if ! lifecycle_require_no_journal; then
  exit 9
fi
if ! validate_ttl_json "$TTL_FILE"; then
  printf 'backup-rules: ERROR: ttl.json is invalid\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi
if [[ -L "$TTL_FILE" ]]; then
  printf 'backup-rules: ERROR: ttl.json must not be a symbolic link\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi

work_dir="$(mktemp -d)" || {
  printf 'backup-rules: ERROR: unable to create private staging directory\n' >&2
  exit 1
}
chmod 700 "$work_dir" || {
  rmdir "$work_dir" 2>/dev/null || true
  printf 'backup-rules: ERROR: unable to secure staging directory\n' >&2
  exit 1
}
cleanup() {
  rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
  if (( LOCK_HELD )); then
    lock_release
  fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT

if [[ -f "$TTL_FILE" ]]; then
  cp -- "$TTL_FILE" "$work_dir/ttl.json" || {
    printf 'backup-rules: ERROR: unable to stage ttl.json\n' >&2
    exit 1
  }
else
  printf '%s\n' '{"schema_version":1,"records":[]}' > "$work_dir/ttl.json"
fi
chmod 600 "$work_dir/ttl.json" || exit 1
if ! lifecycle_collect_export_json > "$work_dir/export.json"; then
  exit 1
fi
chmod 600 "$work_dir/export.json" || exit 1
if ! "$PYTHON3" "$USBAM_LIFECYCLE_HELPER" backup-create \
  --config "$CONFIG_SOURCE" \
  --ttl "$work_dir/ttl.json" \
  --export "$work_dir/export.json" \
  --directory "$BACKUP_DIR" \
  --keep "$(get_config BACKUP_KEEP)"; then
  exit 1
fi

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# scripts/backup.sh
# Phase 5 - Backup with journal awareness, split-locking, atomic-write, audit
# SRS v4.5: Creates backup of RuleFile, RuleFolder, ttl.json, clock_state.json
# to /var/backups/usbguard-approval-manager/ with timestamp,
# keeps BACKUP_KEEP versions, uses atomic-write, locking, audit, result.sh JSON
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# גיבוי שלב 5 עם מודעות יומן, נעילת פיצול, כתיבה אטומית ו-logs audit.
# יוצר גיבוי של RuleFile, RuleFolder, ttl.json, clock_state.json
# לתיקיית /var/backups/usbguard-approval-manager/ עם תג timestamp.
# שומר BACKUP_KEEP גרסאות, משתמש בכתיבה אטומית, נעילות, audit ו-result.sh JSON.
# תוכנת כך שגיבוי לא ישנה את מדיניות ה-USBGuard הקיימת אלא רק יעתיק נתונים.

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
LIB_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/lib"
if [[ ! -d "$LIB_DIR" ]]; then
  # fallback when called from repo root or different layout
  LIB_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/../scripts/lib"
fi
if [[ ! -d "$LIB_DIR" ]]; then
  LIB_DIR="$(cd -- "$SCRIPT_DIR" && pwd)/lib"
fi
# Also support final location /opt style
if [[ ! -f "$LIB_DIR/config-reader.sh" ]]; then
  for cand in "/opt/usbguard-approval-manager/scripts/lib" "./scripts/lib" "$SCRIPT_DIR/lib"; do
    if [[ -f "$cand/config-reader.sh" ]]; then
      LIB_DIR="$cand"
      break
    fi
  done
fi

# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/config-reader.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/lock.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/logger.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/result.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/validators.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/usbguard-policy.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/journal.sh"

CONFIG_PATH="${USBAM_CONFIG_PATH:-/etc/usbguard-approval-manager/approval-manager.conf}"
STATE_DIR="${USBAM_STATE_DIR:-/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager}"
TTL_FILE="$STATE_DIR/ttl.json"
CLOCK_STATE_FILE="$STATE_DIR/clock_state.json"
RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE="${USBAM_RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/restore_journal.json}"
BACKUP_BASE_DIR="${USBAM_BACKUP_BASE_DIR:-/var/backups/usbguard-approval-manager}"
STATE_LOCK_FILE="${USBAM_STATE_LOCK_FILE:-/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager-state.lock}"
POLICY_LOCK_FILE="${USBAM_POLICY_LOCK_FILE:-/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager-policy.lock}"
ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER="$LIB_DIR/atomic-write.py"
AUDIT_WRITER="$LIB_DIR/audit_write.py"

BACKUP_ERROR=""
BACKUP_LAST_DIR=""
BACKUP_CORRELATION_ID=""

backup_error() {
  BACKUP_ERROR="$1"
  printf 'backup: ERROR: %s\n' "$1" >&2
}

backup_fsync_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  python3 -c 'import os,sys; fd=os.open(sys.argv[1], os.O_DIRECTORY); os.fsync(fd); os.close(fd)' "$dir" 2>/dev/null || true
}

backup_ensure_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  local mode="${2:-700}"
  if [[ -z "$dir" ]]; then
    backup_error "ensure dir path empty"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$dir" != /* ]]; then
    backup_error "dir must be absolute: $dir"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -L "$dir" ]]; then
    backup_error "dir is symlink - refusing: $dir"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "$dir" ]]; then
    if ! mkdir -p "$dir"; then
      backup_error "unable to create dir $dir"
      return 1
    fi
  fi
  if ! chmod "$mode" "$dir" 2>/dev/null; then
    backup_error "chmod $mode failed for $dir"
    return 1
  fi
  backup_fsync_dir "$(dirname -- "$dir")"
  return 0
}

backup_generate_ids() {
  local epoch utc short_uuid

  if ! epoch="$(date +%s)" || [[ ! "$epoch" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    backup_error "unable to get epoch"
    return 1
  fi
  utc="$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ 2>/dev/null || date -u +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S 2>/dev/null || printf '%s' "$epoch")"
  if [[ -r /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid ]]; then
    short_uuid="$(head -c 8 /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid | tr -d '-')"
    # better: full uuid first 8
    if command -v cut >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      short_uuid="$(cut -d'-' -f1 /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid 2>/dev/null || echo "$epoch")"
    fi
  else
    short_uuid="$(python3 -c "import uuid; print(uuid.uuid4().hex[:8])" 2>/dev/null || echo "$epoch")"
  fi
  BACKUP_CORRELATION_ID="$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid 2>/dev/null || python3 -c "import uuid; print(uuid.uuid4())")"
  printf '%s %s %s %s\n' "$epoch" "$utc" "$short_uuid" "$BACKUP_CORRELATION_ID"
}

backup_sha256_file() {
  local file="$1"
  if [[ ! -f "$file" || -L "$file" ]]; then
    printf 'missing\n'
    return 0
  fi
  if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    sha256sum -- "$file" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}' || printf 'error\n'
  else
    python3 -c "import hashlib,sys; h=hashlib.sha256(open(sys.argv[1],'rb').read()).hexdigest(); print(h)" "$file" 2>/dev/null || printf 'error\n'
  fi
}

backup_copy_file_secure() {
  local src="$1"
  local dst="$2"
  local dst_dir
  dst_dir="$(dirname -- "$dst")"
  if [[ -L "$src" ]]; then
    backup_error "source is symlink refusing: $src"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -f "$src" ]]; then
    backup_error "source not regular file: $src"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! backup_ensure_dir "$dst_dir" 700; then
    return 1
  fi
  local tmp_dst
  tmp_dst="$dst_dir/.$(basename -- "$dst").tmp.$$.$RANDOM"
  if ! cp --no-dereference -p -- "$src" "$tmp_dst"; then
    backup_error "cp failed $src -> $tmp_dst"
    rm -f -- "$tmp_dst"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! chmod 600 "$tmp_dst"; then
    rm -f -- "$tmp_dst"
    backup_error "chmod 600 tmp failed"
    return 1
  fi
  # fsync file
  python3 -c 'import os,sys; fd=os.open(sys.argv[1], os.O_RDONLY); os.fsync(fd); os.close(fd)' "$tmp_dst" 2>/dev/null || true
  if ! mv -f -- "$tmp_dst" "$dst"; then
    rm -f -- "$tmp_dst"
    backup_error "mv tmp to final failed"
    return 1
  fi
  chmod 600 "$dst" || true
  backup_fsync_dir "$dst_dir"
  return 0
}

backup_copy_folder_secure() {
  local src_folder="$1"
  local dst_folder="$2"
  if [[ -L "$src_folder" ]]; then
    backup_error "RuleFolder source is symlink: $src_folder"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "$src_folder" ]]; then
    backup_error "RuleFolder not directory: $src_folder"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! backup_ensure_dir "$dst_folder" 700; then
    return 1
  fi
  local entry
  local copied=0
  for entry in "$src_folder"/*; do
    [[ -e "$entry" ]] || continue
    if [[ -L "$entry" ]]; then
      log_info "backup: skipping symlink in RuleFolder: $entry" 2>/dev/null || printf 'INFO: skipping symlink %s\n' "$entry"
      continue
    fi
    if [[ -f "$entry" ]]; then
      local base
      base="$(basename -- "$entry")"
      if backup_copy_file_secure "$entry" "$dst_folder/$base"; then
        copied=$((copied+1))
      else
        return 1
      fi
    fi
  done
  # fsync folder
  backup_fsync_dir "$dst_folder"
  # return 0 even if empty folder
  return 0
}

backup_prune_old() {
  local keep="$1"
  local base_dir="$2"
  if [[ ! "$keep" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || (( keep < 1 )); then
    backup_error "invalid keep value $keep"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "$base_dir" ]]; then
    return 0
  fi
  # list dirs sorted lexical (timestamp sortable)
  local -a dirs
  mapfile -t dirs < <(find "$base_dir" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d -printf '%p\n' 2>/dev/null | sort || ls -1 "$base_dir" 2>/dev/null | sort | while read -r n; do printf '%s/%s\n' "$base_dir" "$n"; done)
  local count=${#dirs[@]}
  if (( count <= keep )); then
    return 0
  fi
  local to_delete=$((count - keep))
  local i d
  for ((i=0; i<to_delete; i++)); do
    d="${dirs[i]}"
    # safety: must contain manifest.json and must be inside base_dir
    if [[ "$d" != "$base_dir"/* ]]; then
      continue
    fi
    if [[ -f "$d/manifest.json" ]]; then
      log_info "backup: pruning old backup $d" 2>/dev/null || true
      rm -rf -- "$d"
      backup_fsync_dir "$base_dir"
    fi
  done
  return 0
}

backup_release_locks() {
  journal_release_both_locks 2>/dev/null || true
}

usage() {
  cat <<EOF
Usage: $0 [--help]

Backup USBGuard Approval Manager state and policy

Creates backup of RuleFile, RuleFolder, ttl.json, clock_state.json
to $BACKUP_BASE_DIR/<timestamp>_<uuid>

Options:
  --help          Show this help

Exit codes:
  0 success
  1 operational failure
  7 validation failure
  9 recovery required (restore journal exists)
  14 audit write failed

Environment:
  USBAM_CONFIG_PATH, USBAM_STATE_DIR, USBAM_BACKUP_BASE_DIR,
  USBAM_STATE_LOCK_FILE, USBAM_POLICY_LOCK_FILE
EOF
}

main() {
  local arg
  for arg in "$@"; do
    case "$arg" in
      --help)
        usage
        exit 0
        ;;
      *)
        printf 'ERROR: unknown argument %s\n' "$arg" >&2
        usage >&2
        exit 7
        ;;
    esac
  done

  result_start

  # Load config if exists - data-only via config-reader, no source/eval
  local backup_keep="5"
  if [[ -f "$CONFIG_PATH" ]]; then
    if ! load_config "$CONFIG_PATH"; then
      backup_error "config invalid at $CONFIG_PATH"
      result_validation_failed "config invalid"
      result_print_json
      exit 7
    fi
    if get_config BACKUP_KEEP >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      backup_keep="$(get_config BACKUP_KEEP)"
    fi
  fi
  if [[ ! "$backup_keep" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || (( backup_keep < 1 || backup_keep > 100 )); then
    backup_keep="5"
  fi

  # Check restore journal before any operation - if exists block
  if [[ -f "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" ]]; then
    backup_error "restore journal exists at $RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE - recovery required, blocking backup"
    result_recovery_required "restore journal exists - recovery required"
    result_print_json
    exit 9
  fi
  if ! journal_check_and_block "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE"; then
    local rc=$?
    if (( rc == 9 )); then
      result_recovery_required "restore journal exists"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
  fi

  # Setup trap for locks release
  trap 'backup_release_locks' EXIT INT TERM

  # Acquire split locks - state first, then policy to avoid deadlock
  if ! journal_acquire_both_locks 5; then
    backup_error "unable to acquire state+policy locks (backup)"
    result_operation_failed "lock acquisition failed: $JOURNAL_ERROR"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi

  log_info "backup: locks acquired state=$JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FILE policy=$JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true

  # Validate ttl.json if exists
  if [[ -f "$TTL_FILE" ]]; then
    if ! validate_ttl_json "$TTL_FILE"; then
      backup_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      backup_error "ttl.json invalid: $VALIDATORS_ERROR - recovery required"
      result_recovery_required "ttl.json invalid: $VALIDATORS_ERROR_CODE"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
  fi

  # Get persistent policy paths
  if ! get_persistent_policy_paths; then
    backup_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    backup_error "policy source unavailable: $USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE"
    result_operation_failed "policy source unavailable"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi

  local rule_file="" rule_folder=""
  if [[ -n "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]+x}" ]]; then
    rule_file="${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]}"
  fi
  if [[ -n "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]+x}" ]]; then
    rule_folder="${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]}"
  fi

  if [[ -z "$rule_file" && -z "$rule_folder" ]]; then
    backup_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    backup_error "no RuleFile or RuleFolder configured"
    result_operation_failed "policy source unconfigured"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi

  # Ensure backup base dir exists with secure mode
  if ! backup_ensure_dir "$BACKUP_BASE_DIR" 700; then
    backup_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    result_operation_failed "unable to create backup base dir"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi

  # Generate timestamp and correlation id
  local gen_line epoch utc short_uuid correlation_id
  gen_line="$(backup_generate_ids)"
  read -r epoch utc short_uuid correlation_id <<< "$gen_line"
  if [[ -z "$epoch" || -z "$utc" || -z "$correlation_id" ]]; then
    backup_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    backup_error "failed to generate timestamp/correlation"
    result_operation_failed "id generation failed"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi
  # override global correlation id for result.sh to use same id
  RESULT_CORRELATION_ID="$correlation_id"

  local backup_dir_name="${utc}_${short_uuid}"
  # sanitize: allow only alnum, T, Z, -, _, :
  # replace : with -
  backup_dir_name="${backup_dir_name//:/-}"
  local backup_dir="$BACKUP_BASE_DIR/$backup_dir_name"

  if [[ -e "$backup_dir" ]]; then
    backup_dir="${backup_dir}_$RANDOM"
  fi

  if ! mkdir -p "$backup_dir"; then
    backup_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    backup_error "mkdir backup dir failed $backup_dir"
    result_operation_failed "mkdir backup dir failed"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi
  chmod 700 "$backup_dir" || true
  BACKUP_LAST_DIR="$backup_dir"

  # Copy files with secure handling
  local rule_file_backup_name="" rule_file_sha="" rule_file_size=""
  local ttl_sha="" ttl_size="" clock_sha="" clock_size=""
  local rule_folder_copied="0"

  if [[ -n "$rule_file" ]]; then
    local rf_base
    rf_base="$(basename -- "$rule_file")"
    rule_file_backup_name="$rf_base"
    if ! backup_copy_file_secure "$rule_file" "$backup_dir/$rf_base"; then
      rm -rf -- "$backup_dir"
      backup_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      result_operation_failed "RuleFile backup copy failed"
      result_print_json
      exit 1
    fi
    rule_file_sha="$(backup_sha256_file "$backup_dir/$rf_base")"
    rule_file_size="$(stat -c '%s' "$backup_dir/$rf_base" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"
  fi

  if [[ -n "$rule_folder" ]]; then
    if ! backup_copy_folder_secure "$rule_folder" "$backup_dir/RuleFolder"; then
      rm -rf -- "$backup_dir"
      backup_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      result_operation_failed "RuleFolder backup copy failed"
      result_print_json
      exit 1
    fi
    # count files
    rule_folder_copied="$(find "$backup_dir/RuleFolder" -type f 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d ' ')"
  fi

  if [[ -f "$TTL_FILE" ]]; then
    if ! backup_copy_file_secure "$TTL_FILE" "$backup_dir/ttl.json"; then
      rm -rf -- "$backup_dir"
      backup_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      result_operation_failed "ttl.json backup copy failed"
      result_print_json
      exit 1
    fi
    ttl_sha="$(backup_sha256_file "$backup_dir/ttl.json")"
    ttl_size="$(stat -c '%s' "$backup_dir/ttl.json" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"
  else
    ttl_sha="missing"
    ttl_size="0"
  fi

  if [[ -f "$CLOCK_STATE_FILE" ]]; then
    if ! backup_copy_file_secure "$CLOCK_STATE_FILE" "$backup_dir/clock_state.json"; then
      rm -rf -- "$backup_dir"
      backup_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      result_operation_failed "clock_state.json backup copy failed"
      result_print_json
      exit 1
    fi
    clock_sha="$(backup_sha256_file "$backup_dir/clock_state.json")"
    clock_size="$(stat -c '%s' "$backup_dir/clock_state.json" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"
  else
    clock_sha="missing"
    clock_size="0"
  fi

  # Get ttl records count
  local ttl_records_count=0
  if [[ -f "$backup_dir/ttl.json" ]]; then
    ttl_records_count="$(jq '.records | length' "$backup_dir/ttl.json" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"
    if [[ ! "$ttl_records_count" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
      ttl_records_count=0
    fi
  fi

  local hostname
  hostname="$(hostname 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)"
  local uid
  uid="$(id -u 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"
  local pid
  pid="$$"

  # Build manifest JSON atomically via jq then atomic-write.py
  local manifest_json
  if ! manifest_json="$(jq -n \
    --argjson schema_version 1 \
    --argjson backup_epoch "$epoch" \
    --arg backup_timestamp "$utc" \
    --arg correlation_id "$correlation_id" \
    --arg hostname "$hostname" \
    --argjson pid "$pid" \
    --argjson uid "$uid" \
    --argjson backup_keep "$backup_keep" \
    --arg source_rule_file "${rule_file:-}" \
    --arg source_rule_folder "${rule_folder:-}" \
    --arg rule_file_backup_name "${rule_file_backup_name:-}" \
    --arg rule_file_sha "${rule_file_sha:-missing}" \
    --argjson rule_file_size "${rule_file_size:-0}" \
    --arg ttl_sha "${ttl_sha:-missing}" \
    --argjson ttl_size "${ttl_size:-0}" \
    --arg clock_sha "${clock_sha:-missing}" \
    --argjson clock_size "${clock_size:-0}" \
    --argjson ttl_records_count "$ttl_records_count" \
    --argjson rule_folder_copied "${rule_folder_copied:-0}" \
    '{
      schema_version: $schema_version,
      backup_epoch: $backup_epoch,
      backup_timestamp: $backup_timestamp,
      correlation_id: $correlation_id,
      hostname: $hostname,
      pid: $pid,
      creator_uid: $uid,
      backup_keep: $backup_keep,
      source_rule_file: $source_rule_file,
      source_rule_folder: $source_rule_folder,
      ttl_records_count: $ttl_records_count,
      files: {
        rule_file: {
          original_path: $source_rule_file,
          backup_name: $rule_file_backup_name,
          sha256: $rule_file_sha,
          size: $rule_file_size
        },
        rule_folder: {
          original_path: $source_rule_folder,
          backup_dir: "RuleFolder",
          file_count: $rule_folder_copied
        },
        ttl_json: {
          backup_name: "ttl.json",
          sha256: $ttl_sha,
          size: $ttl_size
        },
        clock_state_json: {
          backup_name: "clock_state.json",
          sha256: $clock_sha,
          size: $clock_size
        }
      }
    }')"; then
    rm -rf -- "$backup_dir"
    backup_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    backup_error "failed to build manifest json"
    result_operation_failed "manifest build failed"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi

  # Atomic write manifest.json
  if [[ ! -r "$ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER" ]]; then
    rm -rf -- "$backup_dir"
    backup_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    backup_error "atomic-write helper missing $ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER"
    result_operation_failed "atomic-write helper missing"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi

  if ! python3 "$ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER" --file "$backup_dir/manifest.json" --data "$manifest_json"; then
    rm -rf -- "$backup_dir"
    backup_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    backup_error "atomic write manifest failed"
    result_operation_failed "manifest atomic write failed"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi
  chmod 600 "$backup_dir/manifest.json" 2>/dev/null || true
  backup_fsync_dir "$backup_dir"
  backup_fsync_dir "$BACKUP_BASE_DIR"

  # Prune old backups respecting BACKUP_KEEP
  if ! backup_prune_old "$backup_keep" "$BACKUP_BASE_DIR"; then
    log_error "backup: pruning failed but backup succeeded" 2>/dev/null || true
    # not fatal
  fi

  # Release locks before audit (audit doesn't need lock)
  backup_release_locks
  trap - EXIT INT TERM

  # Audit - best effort
  local audit_failed=0
  if [[ -r "$AUDIT_WRITER" ]]; then
    if ! python3 "$AUDIT_WRITER" \
      --action "backup" \
      --policy-result "not_applicable" \
      --correlation-id "$correlation_id" \
      --details "backup created at $backup_dir ttl_records=$ttl_records_count keep=$backup_keep"; then
      log_error "backup: audit write failed" 2>/dev/null || true
      audit_failed=1
    fi
  else
    # fallback via logger.sh log_audit if audit_write missing
    if declare -F log_audit >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      if ! log_audit "backup" "not_applicable" "$correlation_id" "backup created at $backup_dir"; then
        audit_failed=1
      fi
    fi
  fi

  # Also operational log
  if declare -F log_info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    log_info "backup created at $backup_dir correlation_id=$correlation_id" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi

  if (( audit_failed )); then
    result_policy_applied_audit_failed "backup created at $backup_dir but audit failed"
    result_print_json
    exit 14
  fi

  result_success "backup created at $backup_dir"
  # add detail as item for better observability (optional)
  # result_add_item would need batch but we just print simple success
  result_print_json
  exit 0
}

main "$@"
