#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Journal helpers for restore/recovery safety.
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# עזרי יומן (journal) לבטיחות restore/recovery.
# יומן קיים ⇒ mutations חסומות עד --recover (אימות ושחזור).
# מנהל נעילות state/policy (native lock files), UUID v4, אימות סכימה.
#
# פונקציות עיקריות:
#   journal_exists / journal_validate / journal_create / journal_read / journal_delete
#   journal_check_and_block - חסימת mutations אם יומן קיים
#   journal_acquire_*_lock / journal_release_*_lock - נעילות state/policy
#   journal_last_correlation_id - correlation id אחרון
# scripts/lib/journal.sh
# Phase 5 - restore_journal.json management with atomic-write and split-locking
# SRS v4.5: Backup/Restore with journal, G-01 gate, Split-Locking
#
# This library is the only owner of restore_journal.json lifecycle.
# It provides creation, existence check, validation and deletion
# with atomic writes and state/policy split locks.
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# ניהולjournal restore_journal.json בשלב 5 עם כתיבה אטומית ונעילת פיצול.
# ספרייה זו היא הבעלים היחיד של מחזור החיים של restore_journal.json.
# היא מספקת יצירה, בדיקת קיימות, אימות ומחיקה
# עם כתיבה אטומית ונעילות הפרדת מצב/מדיניות.
# יומן קיים ⇒ mutations חסומות עד --recover (אימות ושחזור).
# מנהל נעילות state/policy (קבצי נעילה נפרדים), UUID v4, אימות סכימה.
# Phase 5 - restore_journal.json management with atomic-write and split-locking
# SRS v4.5: Backup/Restore with journal, G-01 gate, Split-Locking
#
# This library is the only owner of restore_journal.json lifecycle.
# It provides creation, existence check, validation and deletion
# with atomic writes and state/policy split locks.

if [[ -z "${USBAM_JOURNAL_LOADED+x}" ]]; then
  readonly USBAM_JOURNAL_VERSION="1.1.0"
  readonly USBAM_JOURNAL_SCHEMA_VERSION=1

  declare -g JOURNAL_ERROR=""
  declare -g JOURNAL_ERROR_CODE=""

  # Paths - overridable via env for tests
  declare -g JOURNAL_STATE_DIR="${USBAM_STATE_DIR:-/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager}"
  declare -g JOURNAL_FILE="${USBAM_RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE:-$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR/restore_journal.json}"

  declare -g JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FILE="${USBAM_STATE_LOCK_FILE:-/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager-state.lock}"
  declare -g JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FILE="${USBAM_POLICY_LOCK_FILE:-/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager-policy.lock}"
  declare -g JOURNAL_LEGACY_LOCK_FILE="${USBAM_LOCK_FILE:-/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager.lock}"

  # Split-lock FDs - independent from lock.sh global LOCK_FD
  declare -g JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FD=""
  declare -g JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_HELD=0
  declare -g JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_OWNER_PID=""
  declare -g JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FD=""
  declare -g JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_HELD=0
  declare -g JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_OWNER_PID=""

  declare -g JOURNAL_ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER=""
  declare -g JOURNAL_LAST_CORRELATION_ID=""

  # Resolve helper location
  _journal_resolve_atomic_write() {
    local base_dir candidate
    base_dir="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
    for candidate in \
      "$base_dir/atomic-write.py" \
      "$base_dir/../lib/atomic-write.py" \
      "$base_dir/../../scripts/lib/atomic-write.py" \
      "/opt/usbguard-approval-manager/scripts/lib/atomic-write.py" \
      "./scripts/lib/atomic-write.py"
    do
      if [[ -f "$candidate" && -r "$candidate" ]]; then
        JOURNAL_ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER="$candidate"
        return 0
      fi
    done
    # Fallback - assume in same lib dir as this file's parent
    if [[ -r "$base_dir/atomic-write.py" ]]; then
      JOURNAL_ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER="$base_dir/atomic-write.py"
      return 0
    fi
    JOURNAL_ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER="$base_dir/atomic-write.py"
    return 0
  }
  _journal_resolve_atomic_write

  declare -gr USBAM_JOURNAL_LOADED=1
fi

# --- journal: הדפסת שגיאה ---
journal_error() {
  JOURNAL_ERROR="$1"
  JOURNAL_ERROR_CODE="${2:-journal_failed}"
  printf 'journal: ERROR: %s\n' "$1" >&2
}

# --- journal: יצירת UUID v4 ---
journal_generate_uuid() {
  if [[ -r /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid ]]; then
    cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid
  else
    python3 -c "import uuid; print(uuid.uuid4())"
  fi
}

# --- journal: אימות UUID v4 ---
journal_is_uuid_v4() {
  local uuid="$1"
  [[ "$uuid" =~ ^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-4[0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$ ]]
}

# --- journal: הבטחת תיקיית state ---
journal_ensure_state_dir() {
  if [[ -z "$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR" ]]; then
    journal_error "state dir is empty" "invalid_path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR" != /* ]]; then
    journal_error "state dir must be absolute" "invalid_path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR" == *".."* ]]; then
    journal_error "state dir must not contain .." "invalid_path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -L "$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR" ]]; then
    journal_error "state dir is a symlink - refusing" "symlink_not_allowed"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR" ]]; then
    if ! mkdir -p "$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR"; then
      journal_error "unable to create state dir $JOURNAL_STATE_DIR" "state_dir_unavailable"
      return 1
    fi
  fi
  if ! chmod 700 "$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR" 2>/dev/null; then
    journal_error "unable to chmod state dir" "chmod_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  # fsync state dir for durability
  python3 -c 'import os,sys; fd=os.open(sys.argv[1], os.O_DIRECTORY); os.fsync(fd); os.close(fd)' "$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR" 2>/dev/null || true
  return 0
}

# --- journal: אימות קובץ lock ---
journal_validate_lock_file() {
  local lock_path="$1"
  local expected actual

  if [[ -z "$lock_path" ]]; then
    journal_error "lock file path empty" "invalid_lock_path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$lock_path" != /* ]]; then
    journal_error "lock file must be absolute: $lock_path" "invalid_lock_path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -L "$lock_path" ]]; then
    journal_error "lock file is symlink: $lock_path" "symlink_not_allowed"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -f "$lock_path" ]]; then
    journal_error "lock file missing: $lock_path" "lock_missing"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -r "$lock_path" || ! -w "$lock_path" ]]; then
    journal_error "lock file not rw: $lock_path" "lock_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi
  # Ownership/mode check: for /run/lock/* expect 0:0:600 when root, else current user 600
  if [[ "$lock_path" == /run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager* ]]; then
    # if running as root, expect 0:0:600, else current user
    if (( $(id -u) == 0 )); then
      expected="0:0:600"
    else
      expected="$(id -u):$(id -g):600" || {
        journal_error "unable to determine expected lock owner" "owner_check_failed"
        return 1
      }
    fi
  else
    expected="$(id -u):$(id -g):600" || {
      journal_error "unable to determine expected lock owner" "owner_check_failed"
      return 1
    }
  fi
  actual="$(stat -c '%u:%g:%a' -- "$lock_path" 2>/dev/null)" || {
    journal_error "unable to stat lock file: $lock_path" "stat_failed"
    return 1
  }
  if [[ "$actual" != "$expected" ]]; then
    journal_error "unsafe lock file $lock_path expected $expected got $actual" "unsafe_lock"
    return 1
  fi
  return 0
}

# --- journal: רכישת state lock ---
journal_acquire_state_lock() {
  local timeout="${1:-5}"
  if (( $# != 1 )) || [[ ! "$timeout" =~ ^[0-9]+([.][0-9]+)?$ ]]; then
    journal_error "state lock timeout must be non-negative number" "invalid_timeout"
    return 1
  fi
  if (( JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_HELD != 0 )); then
    journal_error "state lock already held by this process" "lock_already_held"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! journal_validate_lock_file "$JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FILE"; then
    # try legacy lock as fallback for backward compatibility
    if [[ "$JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FILE" != "$JOURNAL_LEGACY_LOCK_FILE" ]] && [[ -f "$JOURNAL_LEGACY_LOCK_FILE" ]]; then
      if journal_validate_lock_file "$JOURNAL_LEGACY_LOCK_FILE"; then
        # fallback uses legacy file but logs warning
        JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FILE="$JOURNAL_LEGACY_LOCK_FILE"
      else
        return 1
      fi
    else
      return 1
    fi
  fi
  if ! exec {JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FD}>>"$JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FILE"; then
    JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FD=""
    journal_error "unable to open state lock file" "open_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! flock --exclusive --wait "$timeout" "$JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FD"; then
    exec {JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FD}>&-
    JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FD=""
    journal_error "timed out acquiring state lock" "lock_timeout"
    return 1
  fi
  JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_HELD=1
  JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_OWNER_PID="$BASHPID"
  return 0
}

# --- journal: שחרור state lock ---
journal_release_state_lock() {
  if (( JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_HELD == 0 )); then
    return 0
  fi
  if [[ "$JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_OWNER_PID" != "$BASHPID" ]]; then
    journal_error "only owner may release state lock" "owner_mismatch"
    return 1
  fi
  JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_HELD=0
  JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_OWNER_PID=""
  if ! exec {JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FD}>&-; then
    JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FD=""
    journal_error "failed to close state lock fd" "close_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  JOURNAL_STATE_LOCK_FD=""
  return 0
}

# --- journal: רכישת policy lock ---
journal_acquire_policy_lock() {
  local timeout="${1:-5}"
  if (( $# != 1 )) || [[ ! "$timeout" =~ ^[0-9]+([.][0-9]+)?$ ]]; then
    journal_error "policy lock timeout must be non-negative number" "invalid_timeout"
    return 1
  fi
  if (( JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_HELD != 0 )); then
    journal_error "policy lock already held" "lock_already_held"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! journal_validate_lock_file "$JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FILE"; then
    if [[ "$JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FILE" != "$JOURNAL_LEGACY_LOCK_FILE" ]] && [[ -f "$JOURNAL_LEGACY_LOCK_FILE" ]]; then
      if journal_validate_lock_file "$JOURNAL_LEGACY_LOCK_FILE"; then
        JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FILE="$JOURNAL_LEGACY_LOCK_FILE"
      else
        return 1
      fi
    else
      return 1
    fi
  fi
  if ! exec {JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FD}>>"$JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FILE"; then
    JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FD=""
    journal_error "unable to open policy lock file" "open_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! flock --exclusive --wait "$timeout" "$JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FD"; then
    exec {JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FD}>&-
    JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FD=""
    journal_error "timed out acquiring policy lock" "lock_timeout"
    return 1
  fi
  JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_HELD=1
  JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_OWNER_PID="$BASHPID"
  return 0
}

# --- journal: שחרור policy lock ---
journal_release_policy_lock() {
  if (( JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_HELD == 0 )); then
    return 0
  fi
  if [[ "$JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_OWNER_PID" != "$BASHPID" ]]; then
    journal_error "only owner may release policy lock" "owner_mismatch"
    return 1
  fi
  JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_HELD=0
  JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_OWNER_PID=""
  if ! exec {JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FD}>&-; then
    JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FD=""
    journal_error "failed to close policy lock fd" "close_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  JOURNAL_POLICY_LOCK_FD=""
  return 0
}

# --- journal: רכישת שני locks ---
journal_acquire_both_locks() {
  local timeout="${1:-5}"
  if ! journal_acquire_state_lock "$timeout"; then
    return 1
  fi
  if ! journal_acquire_policy_lock "$timeout"; then
    journal_release_state_lock || true
    return 1
  fi
  return 0
}

# --- journal: שחרור שני locks ---
journal_release_both_locks() {
  # release in reverse order to avoid deadlock
  local rc=0
  if ! journal_release_policy_lock; then
    rc=1
  fi
  if ! journal_release_state_lock; then
    rc=1
  fi
  return $rc
}

# --- journal: האם יומן קיים ---
journal_exists() {
  [[ -f "$JOURNAL_FILE" ]]
}

# --- journal: אימות יומן ---
journal_validate() {
  local file_path="${1:-$JOURNAL_FILE}"
  JOURNAL_ERROR=""
  JOURNAL_ERROR_CODE=""

  if [[ -L "$file_path" ]]; then
    journal_error "journal file is symlink" "symlink_not_allowed"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -f "$file_path" ]]; then
    journal_error "journal file not found: $file_path" "not_found"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! jq -e . "$file_path" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    journal_error "journal file is not valid JSON" "invalid_json"
    return 1
  fi
  # control chars check - python helper would reject but we check via jq raw
  if grep -q $'[\x00-\x08\x0B\x0C\x0E-\x1F\x7F]' "$file_path" 2>/dev/null; then
    journal_error "journal contains control characters" "control_chars"
    return 1
  fi
  local schema
  schema="$(jq -r '.schema_version // empty' "$file_path" 2>/dev/null)"
  if [[ "$schema" != "$USBAM_JOURNAL_SCHEMA_VERSION" ]]; then
    journal_error "invalid schema_version $schema" "invalid_schema"
    return 1
  fi
  local required_fields=("created_at" "created_at_utc" "backup_path" "backup_timestamp" "correlation_id" "pid" "reason" "status")
  local field
  for field in "${required_fields[@]}"; do
    if ! jq -e "has(\"$field\")" "$file_path" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      journal_error "journal missing field $field" "missing_field"
      return 1
    fi
    if [[ "$(jq -r ".${field} // empty" "$file_path" 2>/dev/null)" == "" ]]; then
      # allow 0 for pid/created_at ?
      if [[ "$field" == "created_at" || "$field" == "pid" ]]; then
        continue
      fi
      journal_error "journal field $field empty" "empty_field"
      return 1
    fi
  done
  local cid
  cid="$(jq -r '.correlation_id // empty' "$file_path")"
  if ! journal_is_uuid_v4 "$cid"; then
    journal_error "journal correlation_id not UUID v4" "invalid_correlation_id"
    return 1
  fi
  local status
  status="$(jq -r '.status // empty' "$file_path")"
  case "$status" in
    in_progress|completed|failed) ;;
    *)
      journal_error "journal status invalid: $status" "invalid_status"
      return 1
      ;;
  esac
  return 0
}

# --- journal: יצירת יומן ---
journal_create() {
  local backup_path="$1"
  local reason="${2:-restore}"
  local correlation_id="${3:-}"

  JOURNAL_ERROR=""
  JOURNAL_ERROR_CODE=""

  if (( $# < 1 || $# > 3 )); then
    journal_error "journal_create requires backup_path" "invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -z "$backup_path" ]]; then
    journal_error "backup_path empty" "invalid_backup_path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$backup_path" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]; then
    journal_error "backup_path contains control characters" "invalid_backup_path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$backup_path" != /* ]]; then
    journal_error "backup_path must be absolute: $backup_path" "invalid_backup_path"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$reason" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]; then
    journal_error "reason contains control characters" "invalid_reason"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ${#reason} -gt 128 ]]; then
    journal_error "reason too long" "reason_too_long"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! journal_ensure_state_dir; then
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ -n "$correlation_id" ]]; then
    if ! journal_is_uuid_v4 "$correlation_id"; then
      journal_error "correlation_id must be UUID v4" "invalid_correlation_id"
      return 1
    fi
  else
    correlation_id="$(journal_generate_uuid)"
    if ! journal_is_uuid_v4 "$correlation_id"; then
      journal_error "failed to generate uuid" "uuid_failed"
      return 1
    fi
  fi
  JOURNAL_LAST_CORRELATION_ID="$correlation_id"

  local created_at created_at_utc backup_timestamp pid uid

  if ! created_at="$(date +%s)" || [[ ! "$created_at" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    journal_error "unable to get epoch" "clock_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi
  created_at_utc="$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ 2>/dev/null || date +%s)"
  backup_timestamp="$(basename "$backup_path")"
  if [[ -z "$backup_timestamp" ]]; then
    backup_timestamp="$backup_path"
  fi
  # sanitize backup_timestamp for json (avoid control chars already checked)
  pid="$$"
  uid="$(id -u 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"

  # Build JSON - use jq for safe escaping
  local journal_json
  if ! journal_json="$(jq -n \
    --argjson schema_version "$USBAM_JOURNAL_SCHEMA_VERSION" \
    --argjson created_at "$created_at" \
    --arg created_at_utc "$created_at_utc" \
    --arg backup_path "$backup_path" \
    --arg backup_timestamp "$backup_timestamp" \
    --arg correlation_id "$correlation_id" \
    --argjson pid "$pid" \
    --argjson uid "$uid" \
    --arg reason "$reason" \
    --arg status "in_progress" \
    '{
      schema_version: $schema_version,
      created_at: $created_at,
      created_at_utc: $created_at_utc,
      backup_path: $backup_path,
      backup_timestamp: $backup_timestamp,
      correlation_id: $correlation_id,
      pid: $pid,
      initiator_uid: $uid,
      reason: $reason,
      status: $status
    }')"; then
    journal_error "failed to build journal json" "json_build_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  # Ensure atomic-write helper exists
  if [[ ! -r "$JOURNAL_ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER" ]]; then
    # attempt fallback relative discovery
    local alt="$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR/../scripts/lib/atomic-write.py"
    if [[ -r "$alt" ]]; then
      JOURNAL_ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER="$alt"
    else
      journal_error "atomic-write helper not found" "helper_missing"
      return 1
    fi
  fi

  if ! python3 "$JOURNAL_ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER" --file "$JOURNAL_FILE" --data "$journal_json"; then
    journal_error "atomic write of journal failed" "atomic_write_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! chmod 600 "$JOURNAL_FILE" 2>/dev/null; then
    journal_error "chmod journal failed" "chmod_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  python3 -c 'import os,sys; fd=os.open(sys.argv[1], os.O_DIRECTORY); os.fsync(fd); os.close(fd)' "$JOURNAL_STATE_DIR" 2>/dev/null || true

  # Validate what we wrote
  if ! journal_validate "$JOURNAL_FILE"; then
    journal_error "journal invalid after creation: $JOURNAL_ERROR" "post_write_invalid"
    return 1
  fi

  return 0
}

# --- journal: קריאת יומן ---
journal_read() {
  local file_path="${1:-$JOURNAL_FILE}"
  if [[ ! -f "$file_path" ]]; then
    journal_error "journal file not found for read" "not_found"
    return 1
  fi
  cat -- "$file_path"
}

# --- journal: מחיקת יומן ---
journal_delete() {
  JOURNAL_ERROR=""
  JOURNAL_ERROR_CODE=""
  local file_path="${1:-$JOURNAL_FILE}"

  if [[ -L "$file_path" ]]; then
    journal_error "journal file is symlink - refusing delete" "symlink_not_allowed"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -f "$file_path" ]]; then
    # idempotent delete - missing is ok
    return 0
  fi

  if ! rm -f -- "$file_path"; then
    journal_error "failed to delete journal file" "delete_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  # fsync dir to ensure deletion durable
  local dir
  dir="$(dirname -- "$file_path")"
  python3 -c 'import os,sys; fd=os.open(sys.argv[1], os.O_DIRECTORY); os.fsync(fd); os.close(fd)' "$dir" 2>/dev/null || true
  return 0
}

# --- journal: חסימת mutations אם יומן קיים ---
journal_check_and_block() {
  local file_path="${1:-$JOURNAL_FILE}"
  if [[ -f "$file_path" ]]; then
    # If file exists, try validate - even invalid journal must block
    if journal_validate "$file_path" 2>/dev/null; then
      printf 'journal: ERROR: restore journal exists at %s - recovery required, blocking operation\n' "$file_path" >&2
    else
      printf 'journal: ERROR: invalid restore journal exists at %s - recovery required\n' "$file_path" >&2
    fi
    return 9
  fi
  return 0
}

# Helper to get correlation_id from last create
# --- journal: correlation id אחרון ---
journal_last_correlation_id() {
  printf '%s\n' "$JOURNAL_LAST_CORRELATION_ID"
}

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Validated USBGuard backup, export, and crash-safe restore helpers."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime
import gzip
import hashlib
import io
import json
import os
import secrets
import stat
import sys
import tarfile
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE = 4 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_ARCHIVE_SIZE = 16 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_ARCHIVE_MEMBERS = 8
MAX_RULE_COUNT = 10000
EXPORT_KEYS = {"schema_version", "permanent_rules", "ttl_records"}
TTL_KEYS = {
    "managed_id",
    "rule",
    "rule_sha256",
    "expires_at",
    "status",
    "correlation_id",
}
TTL_STATUSES = {"pending", "active", "inconsistent", "recovery_required", "expired"}
PAYLOAD_PATHS = {
    "config/approval-manager.conf",
    "state/ttl.json",
    "policy/export.json",
}
TARGET_KEYS = {"config", "ttl", "policy"}


class LifecycleError(Exception):
    """A validation, archive, or restore transaction error."""


class _BoundedReader:
    def __init__(self, source: Any, limit: int) -> None:
        self._source = source
        self._limit = limit
        self._read = 0

    def read(self, size: int = -1) -> bytes:
        requested = self._limit - self._read + 1 if size < 0 else size
        chunk = self._source.read(min(requested, self._limit - self._read + 1))
        self._read += len(chunk)
        if self._read > self._limit:
            raise LifecycleError("uncompressed archive exceeds the size limit")
        return chunk


def _reject_constant(value: str) -> None:
    raise ValueError(f"non-standard JSON constant: {value}")


def _unique_object(pairs: list[tuple[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    result: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError(f"duplicate JSON key: {key}")
        result[key] = value
    return result


def _json_loads(data: bytes, label: str) -> Any:
    if len(data) > MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE:
        raise LifecycleError(f"{label} exceeds the document size limit")
    try:
        return json.loads(
            data.decode("utf-8"),
            object_pairs_hook=_unique_object,
            parse_constant=_reject_constant,
        )
    except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as error:
        raise LifecycleError(f"{label} is invalid: {error}") from error


def _read_regular(path: Path, label: str, limit: int = MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE) -> tuple[bytes, os.stat_result]:
    try:
        file_fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0))
    except OSError as error:
        raise LifecycleError(f"cannot safely open {label}: {error}") from error
    try:
        info = os.fstat(file_fd)
        if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode):
            raise LifecycleError(f"{label} is not a regular file")
        chunks: list[bytes] = []
        size = 0
        while True:
            chunk = os.read(file_fd, min(65536, limit + 1 - size))
            if not chunk:
                break
            chunks.append(chunk)
            size += len(chunk)
            if size > limit:
                raise LifecycleError(f"{label} exceeds the size limit")
        return b"".join(chunks), info
    finally:
        os.close(file_fd)


def _sha256(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def _canonical_allow_rule(value: Any, label: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or value != value.strip():
        raise LifecycleError(f"{label} must be a non-empty canonical rule")
    if any(ord(character) < 32 or ord(character) == 127 for character in value):
        raise LifecycleError(f"{label} contains a control character")
    if "00-system.rules" in value or not value.startswith("allow "):
        raise LifecycleError(f"{label} is not an exportable allow rule")
    return value


def _validate_config_text(data: bytes, label: str) -> None:
    try:
        text = data.decode("utf-8")
    except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
        raise LifecycleError(f"{label} is not valid UTF-8") from error
    if any(
        (ord(character) < 32 and character != "\n") or ord(character) == 127
        for character in text
    ):
        raise LifecycleError(f"{label} contains a control character")


def validate_export(document: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(document, dict) or set(document) != EXPORT_KEYS:
        raise LifecycleError("export must contain exactly schema_version, permanent_rules, and ttl_records")
    if type(document["schema_version"]) is not int or document["schema_version"] != 1:
        raise LifecycleError("unsupported export schema_version")
    permanent = document["permanent_rules"]
    ttl_records = document["ttl_records"]
    if not isinstance(permanent, list) or not isinstance(ttl_records, list):
        raise LifecycleError("permanent_rules and ttl_records must be arrays")
    if len(permanent) + len(ttl_records) > MAX_RULE_COUNT:
        raise LifecycleError("export contains too many rules")

    normalized_permanent: list[str] = []
    seen_rules: set[str] = set()
    for index, value in enumerate(permanent):
        rule = _canonical_allow_rule(value, f"permanent_rules[{index}]")
        if rule in seen_rules:
            raise LifecycleError("duplicate permanent rule")
        seen_rules.add(rule)
        normalized_permanent.append(rule)

    normalized_ttl: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen_ttl_rules: set[str] = set()
    for index, value in enumerate(ttl_records):
        if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != {"rule", "expires_at"}:
            raise LifecycleError(f"ttl_records[{index}] must contain exactly rule and expires_at")
        rule = _canonical_allow_rule(value["rule"], f"ttl_records[{index}].rule")
        expires_at = value["expires_at"]
        if type(expires_at) is not int or not 0 < expires_at <= 253402300799:
            raise LifecycleError(f"ttl_records[{index}].expires_at must be a positive integer")
        if rule in seen_rules or rule in seen_ttl_rules:
            raise LifecycleError("rule appears more than once or in both export categories")
        seen_ttl_rules.add(rule)
        normalized_ttl.append({"rule": rule, "expires_at": expires_at})
    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "permanent_rules": normalized_permanent,
        "ttl_records": normalized_ttl,
    }


def validate_ttl_state(document: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(document, dict) or set(document) != {"schema_version", "records"}:
        raise LifecycleError("ttl.json must contain exactly schema_version and records")
    if type(document["schema_version"]) is not int or document["schema_version"] != 1:
        raise LifecycleError("unsupported ttl.json schema_version")
    if not isinstance(document["records"], list):
        raise LifecycleError("ttl.json records must be an array")
    managed_ids: set[str] = set()
    correlations: set[str] = set()
    for index, record in enumerate(document["records"]):
        if not isinstance(record, dict) or set(record) != TTL_KEYS:
            raise LifecycleError(f"ttl.json record {index} has an invalid shape")
        managed_id = record["managed_id"]
        correlation_id = record["correlation_id"]
        try:
            if str(uuid.UUID(managed_id)) != managed_id or uuid.UUID(managed_id).version != 4:
                raise ValueError
            if (
                str(uuid.UUID(correlation_id)) != correlation_id
                or uuid.UUID(correlation_id).version != 4
            ):
                raise ValueError
        except (AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as error:
            raise LifecycleError(f"ttl.json record {index} has an invalid UUID") from error
        if managed_id in managed_ids or correlation_id in correlations:
            raise LifecycleError("ttl.json contains duplicate identifiers")
        managed_ids.add(managed_id)
        correlations.add(correlation_id)
        rule = record["rule"]
        if not isinstance(rule, str) or not rule or any(
            ord(character) < 32 or ord(character) == 127 for character in rule
        ):
            raise LifecycleError(f"ttl.json record {index} has an invalid rule")
        if "00-system.rules" in rule or not rule.startswith("allow "):
            raise LifecycleError(f"ttl.json record {index} contains a protected or non-allow rule")
        if not isinstance(record["rule_sha256"], str) or record["rule_sha256"] != _sha256(
            rule.encode("utf-8")
        ):
            raise LifecycleError(f"ttl.json record {index} has an invalid rule hash")
        if type(record["expires_at"]) is not int or record["expires_at"] <= 0:
            raise LifecycleError(f"ttl.json record {index} has an invalid expires_at")
        if not isinstance(record["status"], str) or record["status"] not in TTL_STATUSES:
            raise LifecycleError(f"ttl.json record {index} has an invalid status")
        if record["expires_at"] > 253402300799:
            raise LifecycleError(f"ttl.json record {index} has an out-of-range expires_at")
    return document


def _fsync_directory(path: Path) -> None:
    directory_fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, "O_DIRECTORY", 0))
    try:
        os.fsync(directory_fd)
    finally:
        os.close(directory_fd)


def _atomic_replace(path: Path, data: bytes, mode: int = 0o600) -> None:
    parent = path.parent
    try:
        resolved_parent = parent.resolve(strict=True)
    except OSError as error:
        raise LifecycleError(f"target directory is unavailable: {parent}") from error
    if resolved_parent != parent.absolute():
        raise LifecycleError(f"target directory contains a symbolic link: {parent}")
    directory_fd = os.open(
        resolved_parent,
        os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, "O_DIRECTORY", 0) | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0),
    )
    temp_name = f".{path.name}.{secrets.token_hex(8)}.tmp"
    try:
        try:
            info = os.stat(path.name, dir_fd=directory_fd, follow_symlinks=False)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            info = None
        if info is not None and not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode):
            raise LifecycleError(f"refusing to replace non-regular target: {path}")
        fd = os.open(
            temp_name,
            os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0),
            mode,
            dir_fd=directory_fd,
        )
        try:
            os.fchmod(fd, mode)
            view = memoryview(data)
            while view:
                written = os.write(fd, view)
                if written <= 0:
                    raise OSError("short write")
                view = view[written:]
            os.fsync(fd)
        finally:
            os.close(fd)
        os.replace(temp_name, path.name, src_dir_fd=directory_fd, dst_dir_fd=directory_fd)
        os.fsync(directory_fd)
    except (OSError, LifecycleError) as error:
        try:
            os.unlink(temp_name, dir_fd=directory_fd)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        if isinstance(error, LifecycleError):
            raise
        raise LifecycleError(f"atomic replacement failed for {path}: {error}") from error
    finally:
        os.close(directory_fd)


def create_backup(
    config_path: Path,
    ttl_path: Path,
    export_path: Path,
    backup_dir: Path,
    keep: int,
) -> Path:
    if type(keep) is not int or not 1 <= keep <= 100:
        raise LifecycleError("backup retention must be an integer from 1 to 100")
    if backup_dir.is_symlink():
        raise LifecycleError("backup directory must not be a symbolic link")
    backup_dir.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if not backup_dir.is_dir() or stat.S_IMODE(backup_dir.stat().st_mode) != 0o700:
        raise LifecycleError("backup directory must be a directory with mode 0700")

    config, _ = _read_regular(config_path, "configuration")
    ttl_raw, _ = _read_regular(ttl_path, "ttl.json")
    export_raw, _ = _read_regular(export_path, "export JSON")
    _validate_config_text(config, "configuration")
    validate_ttl_state(_json_loads(ttl_raw, "ttl.json"))
    export = validate_export(_json_loads(export_raw, "export JSON"))
    payloads = {
        "config/approval-manager.conf": config,
        "state/ttl.json": ttl_raw,
        "policy/export.json": (
            json.dumps(export, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n"
        ).encode("utf-8"),
    }
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "entries": [
            {
                "path": name,
                "size": len(data),
                "mode": 0o600,
                "sha256": _sha256(data),
            }
            for name, data in sorted(payloads.items())
        ],
    }
    now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)
    stem = "usbguard-approval-manager-" + now.strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
    destination = backup_dir / f"{stem}.tar.gz"
    if destination.exists() or destination.is_symlink():
        destination = backup_dir / f"{stem}-{secrets.token_hex(4)}.tar.gz"
    temp_path = backup_dir / f".{destination.name}.{secrets.token_hex(8)}.tmp"
    try:
        with tarfile.open(temp_path, mode="w:gz", format=tarfile.PAX_FORMAT) as archive:
            for name, data in sorted(payloads.items()):
                info = tarfile.TarInfo(name)
                info.size = len(data)
                info.mode = 0o600
                info.mtime = 0
                archive.addfile(info, io.BytesIO(data))
            raw_manifest = (
                json.dumps(manifest, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n"
            ).encode("utf-8")
            info = tarfile.TarInfo("manifest.json")
            info.size = len(raw_manifest)
            info.mode = 0o600
            info.mtime = 0
            archive.addfile(info, io.BytesIO(raw_manifest))
        os.chmod(temp_path, 0o600)
        with temp_path.open("rb") as stream:
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.replace(temp_path, destination)
        _fsync_directory(backup_dir)
    except (OSError, tarfile.TarError) as error:
        try:
            temp_path.unlink()
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        raise LifecycleError(f"backup creation failed: {error}") from error

    backups: list[Path] = []
    for candidate in backup_dir.iterdir():
        if candidate.name.startswith("usbguard-approval-manager-") and candidate.name.endswith(
            ".tar.gz"
        ):
            if candidate.is_symlink() or not candidate.is_file():
                raise LifecycleError(f"unsafe backup entry: {candidate.name}")
            backups.append(candidate)
    backups.sort(key=lambda item: (item.stat().st_mtime_ns, item.name), reverse=True)
    for old_backup in backups[keep:]:
        old_backup.unlink()
    _fsync_directory(backup_dir)
    return destination


def read_backup(backup_path: Path) -> dict[str, bytes]:
    raw_archive, _ = _read_regular(backup_path, "backup archive", MAX_ARCHIVE_SIZE)
    try:
        compressed = io.BytesIO(raw_archive)
        with gzip.GzipFile(fileobj=compressed, mode="rb") as decompressed:
            bounded = _BoundedReader(decompressed, MAX_ARCHIVE_SIZE)
            with tarfile.open(fileobj=bounded, mode="r|") as archive:
                payloads: dict[str, bytes] = {}
                members_seen: set[str] = set()
                for member in archive:
                    if len(members_seen) >= MAX_ARCHIVE_MEMBERS:
                        raise LifecycleError("archive contains too many entries")
                    name = member.name
                    if (
                        not name
                        or name.startswith("/")
                        or "\\" in name
                        or any(part in {"", ".", ".."} for part in name.split("/"))
                    ):
                        raise LifecycleError("archive contains an unsafe path")
                    if name not in PAYLOAD_PATHS | {"manifest.json"}:
                        raise LifecycleError(f"archive contains an unexpected file: {name}")
                    if name in members_seen:
                        raise LifecycleError(f"archive contains a duplicate file: {name}")
                    members_seen.add(name)
                    if not member.isfile() or member.issym() or member.islnk():
                        raise LifecycleError(f"archive entry is not a regular file: {name}")
                    if member.mode != 0o600 or member.size > MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE:
                        raise LifecycleError(f"archive entry has unsafe mode or size: {name}")
                    source = archive.extractfile(member)
                    if source is None:
                        raise LifecycleError(f"cannot read archive entry: {name}")
                    data = source.read(MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE + 1)
                    if len(data) != member.size:
                        raise LifecycleError(f"archive entry size mismatch: {name}")
                    payloads[name] = data
        if "manifest.json" not in payloads:
            raise LifecycleError("archive manifest is missing")
        manifest = _json_loads(payloads.pop("manifest.json"), "manifest")
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict) or set(manifest) != {"schema_version", "entries"}:
            raise LifecycleError("manifest has an invalid shape")
        if type(manifest["schema_version"]) is not int or manifest["schema_version"] != 1:
            raise LifecycleError("unsupported manifest schema_version")
        entries = manifest["entries"]
        if not isinstance(entries, list):
            raise LifecycleError("manifest entries must be an array")
        expected: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        for entry in entries:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict) or set(entry) != {"path", "size", "mode", "sha256"}:
                raise LifecycleError("manifest entry has an invalid shape")
            name = entry["path"]
            if (
                not isinstance(name, str)
                or name not in PAYLOAD_PATHS
                or name in expected
            ):
                raise LifecycleError("manifest contains an unexpected or duplicate path")
            if (
                type(entry["size"]) is not int
                or entry["size"] < 0
                or type(entry["mode"]) is not int
                or entry["mode"] != 0o600
                or not isinstance(entry["sha256"], str)
                or len(entry["sha256"]) != 64
                or any(char not in "0123456789abcdef" for char in entry["sha256"])
            ):
                raise LifecycleError(f"manifest metadata is invalid for {name}")
            expected[name] = entry
        if set(expected) != set(payloads):
            raise LifecycleError("archive files do not match the manifest")
        for name, data in payloads.items():
            entry = expected[name]
            if len(data) != entry["size"] or _sha256(data) != entry["sha256"]:
                raise LifecycleError(f"manifest integrity check failed for {name}")
        if set(payloads) != PAYLOAD_PATHS:
            raise LifecycleError("backup is missing a required payload")
        validate_ttl_state(_json_loads(payloads["state/ttl.json"], "backup ttl.json"))
        validate_export(_json_loads(payloads["policy/export.json"], "backup policy export"))
        _validate_config_text(payloads["config/approval-manager.conf"], "backup configuration")
        return payloads
    except (OSError, tarfile.TarError, gzip.BadGzipFile, EOFError) as error:
        raise LifecycleError(f"cannot read backup archive: {error}") from error


def extract_backup(backup_path: Path, staging_dir: Path) -> None:
    payloads = read_backup(backup_path)
    if staging_dir.exists() or staging_dir.is_symlink():
        raise LifecycleError("staging directory must not already exist")
    staging_dir.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=False)
    for name, data in payloads.items():
        target = staging_dir / name
        target.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        _atomic_replace(target, data)
    _fsync_directory(staging_dir)


def _load_targets(targets_path: Path) -> dict[str, Path]:
    raw, _ = _read_regular(targets_path, "restore targets")
    values = _json_loads(raw, "restore targets")
    if not isinstance(values, dict) or set(values) != TARGET_KEYS:
        raise LifecycleError("restore targets must contain exactly config, ttl, and policy")
    result: dict[str, Path] = {}
    for key, value in values.items():
        if not isinstance(value, str) or not os.path.isabs(value):
            raise LifecycleError(f"restore target {key} must be an absolute path")
        if "00-system.rules" in value or ".." in Path(value).parts:
            raise LifecycleError(f"restore target {key} is outside the safe path allowlist")
        result[key] = Path(value)
    if len(set(result.values())) != len(result):
        raise LifecycleError("restore targets must be distinct")
    return result


def _read_target(path: Path) -> bytes | None:
    try:
        data, _ = _read_regular(path, f"restore target {path}", MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE)
        return data
    except LifecycleError as error:
        if not path.exists() and not path.is_symlink():
            return None
        raise error


def _write_json_atomic(path: Path, document: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    raw = (json.dumps(document, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
    _atomic_replace(path, raw)


def _load_journal(journal_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    raw, info = _read_regular(journal_path, "restore journal")
    if stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode) != 0o600:
        raise LifecycleError("restore journal must have mode 0600")
    document = _json_loads(raw, "restore journal")
    keys = {"schema_version", "transaction_id", "status", "targets"}
    if not isinstance(document, dict) or set(document) != keys:
        raise LifecycleError("restore journal has an invalid shape")
    if type(document["schema_version"]) is not int or document["schema_version"] != 1:
        raise LifecycleError("unsupported restore journal schema_version")
    try:
        transaction_id = document["transaction_id"]
        if str(uuid.UUID(transaction_id)) != transaction_id or uuid.UUID(transaction_id).version != 4:
            raise ValueError
    except (AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as error:
        raise LifecycleError("restore journal transaction ID is invalid") from error
    if not isinstance(document["status"], str) or document["status"] not in {
        "in_progress",
        "recovery_required",
    }:
        raise LifecycleError("restore journal has an invalid status")
    if not isinstance(document["targets"], list) or not document["targets"]:
        raise LifecycleError("restore journal has no targets")
    return document


def _verify_journal_targets(
    journal: dict[str, Any], targets: dict[str, Path]
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    entries: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for entry in journal["targets"]:
        expected_keys = {
            "key",
            "path",
            "had_previous",
            "previous_sha256",
            "desired_sha256",
            "rollback_name",
            "progress",
        }
        if not isinstance(entry, dict) or set(entry) != expected_keys:
            raise LifecycleError("restore journal target has an invalid shape")
        key = entry["key"]
        if (
            not isinstance(key, str)
            or key not in TARGET_KEYS
            or key in entries
            or not isinstance(entry["path"], str)
            or entry["path"] != str(targets[key])
        ):
            raise LifecycleError("restore journal contains a target outside the current allowlist")
        if (
            type(entry["had_previous"]) is not bool
            or entry["rollback_name"] != f"{key}.rollback"
            or not isinstance(entry["progress"], str)
            or entry["progress"] not in {"prepared", "replaced", "restored"}
        ):
            raise LifecycleError("restore journal target metadata is invalid")
        for hash_name in ("previous_sha256", "desired_sha256"):
            digest = entry[hash_name]
            if digest is not None and (
                not isinstance(digest, str)
                or len(digest) != 64
                or any(char not in "0123456789abcdef" for char in digest)
            ):
                raise LifecycleError("restore journal contains an invalid hash")
        if entry["had_previous"] != (entry["previous_sha256"] is not None):
            raise LifecycleError("restore journal previous-state metadata is inconsistent")
        entries[key] = entry
    if set(entries) != TARGET_KEYS:
        raise LifecycleError("restore journal does not cover the complete target allowlist")
    return entries


def _current_hash(path: Path) -> str | None:
    data = _read_target(path)
    return None if data is None else _sha256(data)


def _rollback_transaction(
    journal_path: Path,
    rollback_dir: Path,
    journal: dict[str, Any],
    targets: dict[str, Path],
) -> None:
    entries = _verify_journal_targets(journal, targets)
    for key in ("policy", "ttl", "config"):
        entry = entries[key]
        target = targets[key]
        current_hash = _current_hash(target)
        previous_hash = entry["previous_sha256"]
        desired_hash = entry["desired_sha256"]
        if current_hash == previous_hash:
            continue
        if current_hash != desired_hash:
            raise LifecycleError(
                f"restore target {key} has an unexpected hash; preserving journal and evidence"
            )
        if entry["had_previous"]:
            rollback_path = rollback_dir / entry["rollback_name"]
            rollback_data, _ = _read_regular(rollback_path, f"{key} rollback copy")
            if _sha256(rollback_data) != previous_hash:
                raise LifecycleError(f"{key} rollback copy failed integrity verification")
            _atomic_replace(target, rollback_data)
        else:
            target.unlink()
            _fsync_directory(target.parent)
        if _current_hash(target) != previous_hash:
            raise LifecycleError(f"failed to verify rollback for {key}")
    _remove_journal(journal_path)


def _remove_journal(journal_path: Path) -> None:
    try:
        journal_path.unlink()
        _fsync_directory(journal_path.parent)
    except OSError as error:
        raise LifecycleError(f"could not durably remove restore journal: {error}") from error


def apply_restore(
    journal_path: Path,
    rollback_dir: Path,
    targets_path: Path,
    staged_dir: Path,
) -> None:
    targets = _load_targets(targets_path)
    if journal_path.exists() or journal_path.is_symlink():
        raise LifecycleError("restore journal already exists; use --recover")
    if staged_dir.is_symlink() or not staged_dir.is_dir():
        raise LifecycleError("restore staging directory is unsafe")
    sources = {
        "config": staged_dir / "config/approval-manager.conf",
        "ttl": staged_dir / "state/ttl.json",
        "policy": staged_dir / "policy/rules.conf",
    }
    desired: dict[str, bytes] = {}
    for key, path in sources.items():
        desired[key], _ = _read_regular(path, f"staged {key}")
    ttl_state = validate_ttl_state(_json_loads(desired["ttl"], "staged ttl.json"))
    for record in ttl_state["records"]:
        if record["status"] in {"pending", "active", "inconsistent"}:
            record["status"] = "recovery_required"
    desired["ttl"] = (
        json.dumps(ttl_state, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n"
    ).encode("utf-8")
    validate_ttl_state(_json_loads(desired["ttl"], "restored ttl.json"))

    if rollback_dir.is_symlink():
        raise LifecycleError("restore rollback directory must not be a symbolic link")
    rollback_dir.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if not rollback_dir.is_dir() or stat.S_IMODE(rollback_dir.stat().st_mode) != 0o700:
        raise LifecycleError("restore rollback directory must have mode 0700")
    transaction_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    rollback_dir = rollback_dir / transaction_id
    rollback_dir.mkdir(mode=0o700)

    entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for key in ("config", "ttl", "policy"):
        target = targets[key]
        previous = _read_target(target)
        previous_hash = None if previous is None else _sha256(previous)
        rollback_name = f"{key}.rollback"
        if previous is not None:
            _atomic_replace(rollback_dir / rollback_name, previous)
        entries.append(
            {
                "key": key,
                "path": str(target),
                "had_previous": previous is not None,
                "previous_sha256": previous_hash,
                "desired_sha256": _sha256(desired[key]),
                "rollback_name": rollback_name,
                "progress": "prepared",
            }
        )
    journal = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "transaction_id": transaction_id,
        "status": "in_progress",
        "targets": entries,
    }
    _write_json_atomic(journal_path, journal)

    try:
        for key in ("policy", "ttl", "config"):
            _atomic_replace(targets[key], desired[key])
            entries_by_key = {entry["key"]: entry for entry in journal["targets"]}
            entries_by_key[key]["progress"] = "replaced"
            _write_json_atomic(journal_path, journal)
        for key in ("config", "ttl", "policy"):
            if _current_hash(targets[key]) != _sha256(desired[key]):
                raise LifecycleError(f"restore verification failed for {key}")
        _remove_journal(journal_path)
    except Exception as error:
        try:
            _rollback_transaction(journal_path, rollback_dir, journal, targets)
        except Exception as rollback_error:
            journal["status"] = "recovery_required"
            try:
                _write_json_atomic(journal_path, journal)
                journal_error = ""
            except Exception as journal_write_error:
                journal_error = f"; journal update also failed ({journal_write_error})"
            raise LifecycleError(
                f"restore failed ({error}); rollback requires --recover "
                f"({rollback_error}){journal_error}"
            ) from error
        raise LifecycleError(f"restore failed and was rolled back: {error}") from error


def recover_restore(journal_path: Path, rollback_root: Path, targets_path: Path) -> None:
    targets = _load_targets(targets_path)
    if not journal_path.exists() or journal_path.is_symlink():
        raise LifecycleError("restore journal is missing or unsafe")
    journal = _load_journal(journal_path)
    _verify_journal_targets(journal, targets)
    rollback_dir = rollback_root / journal["transaction_id"]
    if rollback_dir.is_symlink() or not rollback_dir.is_dir():
        raise LifecycleError("restore rollback evidence is missing or unsafe")
    try:
        _rollback_transaction(journal_path, rollback_dir, journal, targets)
    except Exception as error:
        journal["status"] = "recovery_required"
        try:
            _write_json_atomic(journal_path, journal)
            journal_error = ""
        except Exception as journal_write_error:
            journal_error = f"; journal update also failed ({journal_write_error})"
        raise LifecycleError(
            f"restore recovery is incomplete ({error}){journal_error}"
        ) from error


def validate_export_file(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    raw, _ = _read_regular(path, "import JSON")
    return validate_export(_json_loads(raw, "import JSON"))


def _parse_args(arguments: list[str]) -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    backup = subparsers.add_parser("backup-create")
    backup.add_argument("--config", type=Path, required=True)
    backup.add_argument("--ttl", type=Path, required=True)
    backup.add_argument("--export", type=Path, required=True)
    backup.add_argument("--directory", type=Path, required=True)
    backup.add_argument("--keep", type=int, required=True)

    extract = subparsers.add_parser("backup-extract")
    extract.add_argument("--backup", type=Path, required=True)
    extract.add_argument("--staging", type=Path, required=True)

    apply = subparsers.add_parser("restore-apply")
    apply.add_argument("--journal", type=Path, required=True)
    apply.add_argument("--rollback-root", type=Path, required=True)
    apply.add_argument("--targets", type=Path, required=True)
    apply.add_argument("--staging", type=Path, required=True)

    recover = subparsers.add_parser("restore-recover")
    recover.add_argument("--journal", type=Path, required=True)
    recover.add_argument("--rollback-root", type=Path, required=True)
    recover.add_argument("--targets", type=Path, required=True)

    validate = subparsers.add_parser("validate-export")
    validate.add_argument("--file", type=Path, required=True)

    validate_backup = subparsers.add_parser("validate-backup")
    validate_backup.add_argument("--backup", type=Path, required=True)
    return parser.parse_args(arguments)


def main(arguments: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    options = _parse_args(sys.argv[1:] if arguments is None else arguments)
    try:
        if options.command == "backup-create":
            print(
                create_backup(
                    options.config, options.ttl, options.export, options.directory, options.keep
                )
            )
        elif options.command == "backup-extract":
            extract_backup(options.backup, options.staging)
        elif options.command == "restore-apply":
            apply_restore(
                options.journal, options.rollback_root, options.targets, options.staging
            )
        elif options.command == "restore-recover":
            recover_restore(options.journal, options.rollback_root, options.targets)
        elif options.command == "validate-export":
            print(json.dumps(validate_export_file(options.file), ensure_ascii=False))
        elif options.command == "validate-backup":
            read_backup(options.backup)
        return 0
    except (LifecycleError, OSError, ValueError) as error:
        print(f"lifecycle: ERROR: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared Phase 5 data handling. Persistent rules are read only from configured,
# non-system policy sources and are limited to validated allow rules.
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# טיפול שלב 5 משותף בנתונים מתמשכים. כללי קבע נקראים אך ורק ממקורות
# מדיניות מוגדרים שאינם מערכתיים ומוגבלים לכללים מאומן של allow.
# ספרייה אחראית על איסוף, אימות וייצוא כללי קבע בתקינה SEC-16.

USBAM_LIFECYCLE_LIB_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
USBAM_LIFECYCLE_HELPER="$USBAM_LIFECYCLE_LIB_DIR/lifecycle.py"

# --- lifecycle: הדפסת שגיאה ---
lifecycle_error() {
  printf 'lifecycle: ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
}

# --- lifecycle: דרישת عدم journal ---
# אם restore journal קיים ⇒ mutations חסומות.
lifecycle_require_no_journal() {
  if [[ -e "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" || -L "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" ]]; then
    lifecycle_error "restore journal exists; run restore-rules.sh --recover first"
    return 1
  fi
}

# --- lifecycle: איסוף export JSON ---
# קורא policy sources + TTL, מאמת, מסדר, מדפיס JSON.
lifecycle_collect_export_json() {
  local work_dir policy_path policy_file candidate basename line canonical
  local ttl_records permanent_json normalized_output
  local -a source_files=()
  local -a permanent_rules=()

  if ! get_persistent_policy_paths; then
    lifecycle_error "persistent policy sources are unavailable"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -n "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]:-}" ]]; then
    source_files+=("${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]}")
  fi
  if [[ -n "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]:-}" ]]; then
    policy_path="${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]}"
    if [[ "$(stat -c '%u' -- "$policy_path")" != "0" ]] ||
      (( (8#$(stat -c '%a' -- "$policy_path") & 0022) != 0 )); then
      lifecycle_error "RuleFolder ownership or permissions are unsafe"
      return 1
    fi
    work_dir="$(mktemp -d)" || {
      lifecycle_error "unable to create a private staging directory"
      return 1
    }
    chmod 700 "$work_dir" || {
      rmdir "$work_dir" 2>/dev/null || true
      lifecycle_error "unable to secure the staging directory"
      return 1
    }
    if ! find "$policy_path" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -print0 > "$work_dir/files"; then
      rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
      lifecycle_error "unable to enumerate RuleFolder"
      return 1
    fi
    while IFS= read -r -d '' candidate; do
      basename="${candidate##*/}"
      if [[ -L "$candidate" ]]; then
        rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
        lifecycle_error "RuleFolder contains a symbolic link"
        return 1
      fi
      if [[ "$basename" == "00-system.rules" ]]; then
        continue
      fi
      if [[ ! -f "$candidate" ]]; then
        rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
        lifecycle_error "RuleFolder contains an unexpected non-file entry"
        return 1
      fi
      source_files+=("$candidate")
    done < "$work_dir/files"
    rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
  fi

  for policy_file in "${source_files[@]}"; do
    if [[ -L "$policy_file" || ! -f "$policy_file" || ! -r "$policy_file" ]]; then
      lifecycle_error "persistent policy source is unsafe or unreadable"
      return 1
    fi
    if [[ "$(stat -c '%u' -- "$policy_file")" != "0" ]] ||
      (( (8#$(stat -c '%a' -- "$policy_file") & 0022) != 0 )); then
      lifecycle_error "persistent policy file ownership or permissions are unsafe"
      return 1
    fi
    basename="${policy_file##*/}"
    if [[ "$basename" == "00-system.rules" ]]; then
      continue
    fi
    while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
      if [[ "$line" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]; then
        lifecycle_error "persistent policy contains a control character"
        return 1
      fi
      line="${line#"${line%%[![:space:]]*}"}"
      line="${line%"${line##*[![:space:]]}"}"
      [[ -z "$line" || "$line" == \#* ]] && continue
      if ! usbguard_validate_rule "$line"; then
        lifecycle_error "persistent policy contains an invalid rule"
        return 1
      fi
      canonical="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$line")"
      if [[ "$canonical" == allow\ * ]]; then
        permanent_rules+=("$canonical")
      fi
    done < "$policy_file"
  done

  work_dir="$(mktemp -d)" || {
    lifecycle_error "unable to create a private export staging directory"
    return 1
  }
  chmod 700 "$work_dir" || {
    rmdir "$work_dir" 2>/dev/null || true
    lifecycle_error "unable to secure the export staging directory"
    return 1
  }
  : > "$work_dir/permanent.rules"
  if (( ${#permanent_rules[@]} )); then
    printf '%s\n' "${permanent_rules[@]}" | LC_ALL=C sort -u > "$work_dir/permanent.rules" || {
      rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
      lifecycle_error "unable to stage permanent rules"
      return 1
    }
  fi
  if ! permanent_json="$(jq -Rsc 'split("\n") | map(select(length > 0))' \
    "$work_dir/permanent.rules")"; then
    rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
    lifecycle_error "unable to serialize permanent rules"
    return 1
  fi
  ttl_records='[]'
  if [[ -e "$TTL_FILE" || -L "$TTL_FILE" ]]; then
    if ! validate_ttl_json "$TTL_FILE"; then
      rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
      lifecycle_error "ttl.json is invalid"
      return 1
    fi
    if [[ -L "$TTL_FILE" || ! -f "$TTL_FILE" ]]; then
      rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
      lifecycle_error "ttl.json is not a regular file"
      return 1
    fi
    if ! ttl_records="$(jq -ce '
      [.records[] | select(.status == "active") | {rule: .rule, expires_at: .expires_at}]
    ' "$TTL_FILE")"; then
      rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
      lifecycle_error "unable to stage active TTL records"
      return 1
    fi
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r line _; do
      [[ -n "$line" ]] || continue
      if ! usbguard_validate_rule "$line" || [[ "$line" != allow\ * ]] ||
        [[ "$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$line")" != "$line" ]]; then
        rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
        lifecycle_error "active TTL contains an invalid or non-canonical rule"
        return 1
      fi
    done < <(jq -r '
      .records[] | select(.status == "active") | [.rule, .expires_at] | @tsv
    ' "$TTL_FILE")
  fi
  if ! jq -cn --argjson permanent "$permanent_json" --argjson ttl "$ttl_records" \
    '{schema_version:1,permanent_rules:$permanent,ttl_records:$ttl}' > "$work_dir/export.json"; then
    rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
    lifecycle_error "unable to serialize export"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! normalized_output="$("$PYTHON3" "$USBAM_LIFECYCLE_HELPER" \
    validate-export --file "$work_dir/export.json")"; then
    rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
    return 1
  fi
  rm -rf -- "$work_dir"
  printf '%s\n' "$normalized_output"
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shared process lock for state and policy mutations.
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# ספריית נעילה משותפת (process lock) לכל פעולות שינוי מצב/מדיניות.
# מטרת הספרייה: להבטיח שרק תהליך אחד מבצע מוטציה במקביל (exclusivity),
# ולמנוע race conditions על קבצי state/policy. הנעילה משתמשת ב-flock(2)
# על קובץ נעילה ייעודי (/run/lock/...), ומוודאית בעלות/הרשאות קובץ.
# כל פונקציה מחזירה 0 בהצלחה, nonzero בכשל; הודעות שגיאה ל-stderr.
#
# פונקציות עיקריות:
#   lock_acquire <timeout>   - רכישת נעילה אקסקלוסיבית עם timeout
#   lock_release             - שחרור הנעילה (נקרא גם ב-EXIT trap)
#   lock_validate_file      - בדיקת קובץ הנעילה (regular, לא symlink, בעלות/mode)
#   lock_install_exit_trap  - התקנת trap לשחרור אוטומטי ביציאה
# Shared process lock for state and policy mutations.

if [[ "$(declare -p USBAM_LOCK_LIBRARY_LOADED 2>/dev/null || true)" != "declare -r USBAM_LOCK_LIBRARY_LOADED="* ]]; then
  readonly USBAM_LOCK_DEFAULT_PATH="/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager.lock"
  declare -g LOCK_FILE="$USBAM_LOCK_DEFAULT_PATH"
  declare -g LOCK_FD=""
  declare -g LOCK_HELD=0
  declare -g LOCK_OWNER_PID=""
  declare -gr USBAM_LOCK_LIBRARY_LOADED=1
fi

lock_error() {
  printf 'lock: ERROR: %s\n' "$1" >&2
}

# --- נעילה: בדיקת קובץ הנעילה ---
# מוודא שהקובץ קיים, רגיל (לא symlink), עם בעלות/mode בטוחים.
lock_validate_file() {
  local expected actual

  if [[ -L "$LOCK_FILE" || ! -f "$LOCK_FILE" ]]; then
    lock_error "lock file is missing, not regular, or a symbolic link: $LOCK_FILE"
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ "$LOCK_FILE" == "$USBAM_LOCK_DEFAULT_PATH" ]]; then
    expected="0:0:600"
  else
    expected="$(id -u):$(id -g):600" || {
      lock_error "unable to determine current lock-file owner"
      return 1
    }
  fi

  actual="$(stat -c '%u:%g:%a' -- "$LOCK_FILE")" || {
    lock_error "unable to inspect lock file: $LOCK_FILE"
    return 1
  }
  if [[ "$actual" != "$expected" ]]; then
    lock_error "unsafe lock-file ownership or mode: expected $expected, got $actual"
    return 1
  fi
}

# --- נעילה: התקנת EXIT trap ---
# מבטיחה שחרור נעילה אוטומטי ביציאה; דוחה אם trap קיים.
lock_install_exit_trap() {
  local current_trap
  current_trap="$(trap -p EXIT)"
  case "$current_trap" in
    "trap -- 'lock_release' EXIT"|"trap -- 'lock_release;"*" EXIT") return 0 ;;
  esac
  # Accept traps that call lock_release (e.g. test cleanup traps)
  if [[ "$current_trap" == *"lock_release"* ]]; then
    return 0
  fi
  if [[ -z "$current_trap" ]]; then
    trap 'lock_release' EXIT
    return 0
  fi
  lock_error "an EXIT trap already exists; run lock_release first in it before acquiring the lock"
  return 1
}

# --- נעילה: רכישת נעילה ---
# timeout חובה (שניות). בודק שלא כבר מוחזק, מאמת קובץ, פותח fd, flock.
lock_acquire() {
  # רכישת נעילה אקסקלוסיבית עם timeout
  # מאמתת ש-Lock אינו מוחזק כבר, מאמתת את קובץ הנעילה,
  # פותח את הקובץ ומפעילה flock עם timeout שנקבע.
  local timeout="${1:-}"

  if (( $# != 1 )) ||
    [[ ! "$timeout" =~ ^[0-9]+([.][0-9]+)?$ ]]; then
    lock_error "timeout must be a non-negative number of seconds"
    return 1
  fi
  if (( LOCK_HELD != 0 )); then
    lock_error "this process already holds the global lock"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! lock_validate_file; then
    return 1
  fi

  if ! exec {LOCK_FD}>>"$LOCK_FILE"; then
    LOCK_FD=""
    lock_error "unable to open lock file: $LOCK_FILE"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! flock --exclusive --wait "$timeout" "$LOCK_FD"; then
    exec {LOCK_FD}>&-
    LOCK_FD=""
    lock_error "timed out or failed while acquiring the global lock"
    return 1
  fi

  LOCK_HELD=1
  LOCK_OWNER_PID="$BASHPID"
  if ! lock_install_exit_trap; then
    lock_release
    return 1
  fi
}

# --- נעילה: שחרור ---
# רק התהליך שרכש יכול לשחרר. סוגר fd ומאפס state.
lock_release() {
  # שחרור הנעילה - מאמתת ש-Lock Held, ש-Owner PID תואם,
  # ומאתירה את תיאור lock descriptor. מחזירה 0 בהצלחה.
  if (( LOCK_HELD == 0 )); then
    return 0
  fi
  if [[ "$LOCK_OWNER_PID" != "$BASHPID" ]]; then
    lock_error "only the process that acquired the lock may release it"
    return 1
  fi

  LOCK_HELD=0
  LOCK_OWNER_PID=""
  if ! exec {LOCK_FD}>&-; then
    LOCK_FD=""
    lock_error "failed to close the lock descriptor"
    return 1
  fi
  LOCK_FD=""
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Separate operational logging from the protected Audit JSONL stream.
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# ספריית לוגים: הפרדה בין לוג תפעולי (syslog) לזרם Audit מוגן (JSONL).
# לוג תפעולי: הודעות info/error דרך פקודת logger (syslog).
# לוג Audit: writing protected through audit_write.py (root:usbadmins, mode 0640).
# כל פונקציה מחזירה nonzero בכשל כתיבה; caller חייב keep policy result.
#
# פונקציות:
#   log_operational <priority> <message> - כתיבה ל-syslog עם tag usbguard-approval-manager
#   log_info <msg...>                   - קיצור ל-user.info
#   log_error <msg...>                  - קיצור ל-user.err
#   log_audit <action> <policy_result> <correlation_id> [details] - Audit JSONL
#
# אזהרה: Audit write failure must be reported; policy outcome preserved.
# Separate operational logging from the protected Audit JSONL stream.

if [[ "$(declare -p USBAM_LOGGER_LIBRARY_LOADED 2>/dev/null || true)" != "declare -r USBAM_LOGGER_LIBRARY_LOADED="* ]]; then
  readonly USBAM_LOGGER_VERSION="1.0.0"
  readonly USBAM_LOGGER_SYSLOG_TAG="usbguard-approval-manager"
  readonly USBAM_LOGGER_AUDIT_WRITER="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)/audit_write.py"
  declare -gr USBAM_LOGGER_LIBRARY_LOADED=1
fi

# --- לוג: כתיבה תפעולית ל-syslog ---
# בודק control chars, זמינות logger, כותב עם tag/priority.
log_operational() {
  # כתיבת לוג תפעולי ל-syslog
  # בודקת ש-message אינה מכילה תווים שליטה, וודאית ש-utils של logger זמינים
  local LC_ALL=C
  local priority="$1"
  local message="$2"

  if [[ "$message" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]; then
    printf 'logger: ERROR: operational message contains control characters\n' >&2
    return 1
  fi
  if ! command -v logger >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    printf 'logger: ERROR: system logger utility is unavailable\n' >&2
    return 1
  fi
  if ! logger --tag "$USBAM_LOGGER_SYSLOG_TAG" --priority "$priority" -- "$message"; then
    printf 'logger: ERROR: failed to write operational log\n' >&2
    return 1
  fi
}

# --- לוג: info ---
log_info() {
  log_operational user.info "$*"
}

# --- לוג: error ---
log_error() {
  log_operational user.err "$*"
}

# --- לוג: Audit JSONL via python helper ---
# 4 args: action, policy_result, correlation_id, optional details.
log_audit() {
  # כתיבת Einnot Audit JSONL דרך helper של python
  # מקבלת 3-4 ארגומנטים: action, policy_result, correlation_id, ופרטים אופציונליים
  # מדפיסה שגיאה אם מספר ארגומנטים שגוי, אם audit_writer חסר/לא קריא,
  # או אם הכתיבה כשלה - caller must preserve policy outcome
  if (( $# < 3 || $# > 4 )); then
    printf 'logger: ERROR: log_audit requires action, policy result, correlation ID, and optional details\n' >&2
    return 2
  fi
  if [[ ! -r "$USBAM_LOGGER_AUDIT_WRITER" ]]; then
    printf 'logger: ERROR: Audit writer is missing or unreadable\n' >&2
    return 1
  fi
  if ! python3 "$USBAM_LOGGER_AUDIT_WRITER" \
    --action "$1" \
    --policy-result "$2" \
    --correlation-id "$3" \
    --details "${4:-}"; then
    printf 'logger: ERROR: Audit write failed; policy outcome must be preserved\n' >&2
    return 1
  fi
}
