#!/usr/bin/env bash
# scripts/lib/result.sh
# USBGuard Approval Manager — Error / Result Model
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# מודל תוצאות/שגיאות משותף (SRS §21, SEC-16).
# מגדיר קודי יציאה (EXIT_*), מצבי תוצאה (RESULT_*), ופונקציות לניהול
# state גלובלי (RESULT_STATE, RESULT_EXIT_CODE, RESULT_DETAIL, ...),
# והדפסת JSON סופי (result_print_json).
# משמש כל סקריפט להחזרת תוצאה עקיבה ו-exit codes תקניים.
#
# קודי יציאה:
#   0=OK, 1=operation failure, 7=validation, 9=inconsistent/recovery, 13=partial/cancelled, 14=audit.
#
# פונקציות עיקריות:
#   result_start                      - איפוס state ל-RESULT_SUCCESS
#   result_set <state> <exit> [detail]- קביעת state/exit/detail
#   result_success/policy_failed/...  - קיצורי state
#   result_add_item ...               - הוספת item ל-JSON
#   result_print_json                 - הדפסת JSON
#   result_exit                       - exit עם RESULT_EXIT_CODE
# scripts/lib/result.sh
# USBGuard Approval Manager — Error / Result Model
#
# Shared operation outcomes, JSON results, and exit codes (SRS section 21 and SEC-16).
#
# Exit codes:
#   EXIT_OK=0           Operation succeeded
#   EXIT_OPERATION=1    Precondition/operational failure before policy change
#   EXIT_VALIDATION=7   Input/config validation failure
#   EXIT_INCONSISTENT=9 Unknown policy outcome / recovery required
#   EXIT_PARTIAL=13     Batch: some devices succeeded, some failed
#   EXIT_AUDIT=14       Policy applied but audit write failed
#   EXIT_CANCELLED=130  User cancelled (SIGINT/SIGTERM), no policy change

if [[ "$(declare -p RESULT_MODEL_LOADED 2>/dev/null || true)" != \
  "declare -r RESULT_MODEL_LOADED="* ]]; then
  readonly RESULT_MODEL_VERSION="1.2.0"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exit code constants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  readonly EXIT_OK=0
  readonly EXIT_OPERATION=1
  readonly EXIT_VALIDATION=7
  readonly EXIT_INCONSISTENT=9
  readonly EXIT_PARTIAL=13
  readonly EXIT_AUDIT=14
  readonly EXIT_CANCELLED=130

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Result state constants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  readonly RESULT_SUCCESS="SUCCESS"
  readonly RESULT_OPERATION_FAILED="OPERATION_FAILED"
  readonly RESULT_VALIDATION_FAILED="VALIDATION_FAILED"
  readonly RESULT_POLICY_FAILED="POLICY_FAILED"
  readonly RESULT_POLICY_APPLIED_AUDIT_FAILED="POLICY_APPLIED_AUDIT_FAILED"
  readonly RESULT_UNKNOWN="UNKNOWN"
  readonly RESULT_RECOVERY_REQUIRED="RECOVERY_REQUIRED"
  readonly RESULT_PARTIAL="PARTIAL"
  readonly RESULT_CANCELLED="CANCELLED"

  declare -g RESULT_STATE="$RESULT_SUCCESS"
  declare -g RESULT_EXIT_CODE="$EXIT_OK"
  declare -g RESULT_DETAIL=""
  declare -g RESULT_CORRELATION_ID=""
  declare -g RESULT_POLICY_STATUS="not_applicable"
  declare -g RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS="not_applicable"
  declare -g RESULT_POLICY_DETAIL_CODE=""
  declare -g RESULT_AUDIT_DETAIL_CODE=""
  declare -ga RESULT_ITEM_JSON=()
  declare -gr RESULT_MODEL_LOADED=1
fi

# --- תוצאה: איפוס state ---
# מאפס את כל שדות RESULT_* לברירת מחדל (success, exit 0).
result_start() {
  RESULT_CORRELATION_ID=""
  RESULT_STATE="$RESULT_SUCCESS"
  RESULT_EXIT_CODE="$EXIT_OK"
  RESULT_DETAIL=""
  RESULT_POLICY_STATUS="not_applicable"
  RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS="not_applicable"
  RESULT_POLICY_DETAIL_CODE=""
  RESULT_AUDIT_DETAIL_CODE=""
  RESULT_ITEM_JSON=()
}

# --- תוצאה: בדיקת מיפוי state/exit ---
result_mapping_supported() {
  # בודקת אם שילוב state ו-exit_code נתמך - תומכת במיפויים המוגדרים של result codes
  case "$1:$2" in
    "$RESULT_SUCCESS:$EXIT_OK"|\
      "$RESULT_OPERATION_FAILED:$EXIT_OPERATION"|\
      "$RESULT_VALIDATION_FAILED:$EXIT_VALIDATION"|\
      "$RESULT_POLICY_FAILED:$EXIT_OPERATION"|\
      "$RESULT_POLICY_APPLIED_AUDIT_FAILED:$EXIT_AUDIT"|\
      "$RESULT_UNKNOWN:$EXIT_INCONSISTENT"|\
      "$RESULT_RECOVERY_REQUIRED:$EXIT_INCONSISTENT"|\
      "$RESULT_PARTIAL:$EXIT_PARTIAL"|\
      "$RESULT_CANCELLED:$EXIT_CANCELLED") return 0 ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

# --- תוצאה: state מדיניות תקין ---
result_policy_status_supported() {
  # בודקת אם status של policy נתמך - values: applied|not_applied|unknown|not_applicable
  case "$1" in
    applied|not_applied|unknown|not_applicable) return 0 ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

# --- תוצאה: state audit תקין ---
result_audit_status_supported() {
  # בודקת אם status של audit נתמך - values: written|failed|not_applicable
  case "$1" in
    written|failed|not_applicable) return 0 ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

# --- תוצאה: סטטוסי item תקינים ---
result_item_statuses_supported() {
  # בודקת אם שילוב state:policy_status:audit_status נתמך - תומכת בקומבינציות מוגדרות
  local state="$1"
  local policy_status="$2"
  local audit_status="$3"

  case "$state:$policy_status:$audit_status" in
    "$RESULT_SUCCESS:not_applicable:not_applicable"|\
      "$RESULT_SUCCESS:applied:written"|\
      "$RESULT_OPERATION_FAILED:not_applied:not_applicable"|\
      "$RESULT_VALIDATION_FAILED:not_applied:not_applicable"|\
      "$RESULT_POLICY_FAILED:not_applied:not_applicable"|\
      "$RESULT_POLICY_APPLIED_AUDIT_FAILED:applied:failed"|\
      "$RESULT_UNKNOWN:unknown:not_applicable"|\
      "$RESULT_RECOVERY_REQUIRED:unknown:not_applicable"|\
      "$RESULT_CANCELLED:not_applied:not_applicable") return 0 ;;
    "$RESULT_PARTIAL":*)
      result_policy_status_supported "$policy_status" &&
        result_audit_status_supported "$audit_status"
      return
      ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

# --- תוצאה: קביעת state/exit/detail ---
# מאמת מיפוי, מעדכן גלובלים.
result_set() {
  # קובעת state גלובלי של תוצאה - מאמתת mapping נתמך, גנרטור correlation_id,
  # ומעדכנת את כל משתני ה-state הקשורים (RESULT_STATE, RESULT_EXIT_CODE,
  # RESULT_DETAIL, RESULT_POLICY_STATUS, RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS וכו')
  local state="$1"
  local exit_code="$2"
  local detail="${3:-}"
  local correlation_id="$RESULT_CORRELATION_ID"

  if ! result_mapping_supported "$state" "$exit_code"; then
    printf 'result: ERROR: unsupported result state or exit-code mapping\n' >&2
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ -z "$correlation_id" ]]; then
    if [[ ! -r /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid ]] ||
      ! IFS= read -r correlation_id < /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid; then
      printf 'result: ERROR: unable to generate correlation ID\n' >&2
      return 1
    fi
  fi

  RESULT_STATE="$state"
  RESULT_EXIT_CODE="$exit_code"
  RESULT_DETAIL="$detail"
  RESULT_CORRELATION_ID="$correlation_id"

  RESULT_POLICY_DETAIL_CODE=""
  RESULT_AUDIT_DETAIL_CODE=""
  case "$state" in
    "$RESULT_POLICY_FAILED")
      RESULT_POLICY_STATUS="not_applied"
      RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS="not_applicable"
      ;;
    "$RESULT_POLICY_APPLIED_AUDIT_FAILED")
      RESULT_POLICY_STATUS="applied"
      RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS="failed"
      ;;
    "$RESULT_UNKNOWN"|"$RESULT_RECOVERY_REQUIRED")
      RESULT_POLICY_STATUS="unknown"
      RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS="not_applicable"
      ;;
    "$RESULT_PARTIAL")
      RESULT_POLICY_STATUS="unknown"
      RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS="not_applicable"
      ;;
    "$RESULT_OPERATION_FAILED"|"$RESULT_VALIDATION_FAILED"|"$RESULT_CANCELLED")
      RESULT_POLICY_STATUS="not_applied"
      RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS="not_applicable"
      ;;
    *)
      RESULT_POLICY_STATUS="not_applicable"
      RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS="not_applicable"
      ;;
  esac
}

# --- תוצאה: getter state ---
result_get_state() {
  # מחזירה את מצב התוצאה הנוכחי (RESULT_STATE)
  printf '%s\n' "$RESULT_STATE"
}

# --- תוצאה: getter exit code ---
result_get_exit_code() {
  # מחזירה את קוד היציאה הנוכחי (RESULT_EXIT_CODE)
  printf '%s\n' "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE"
}

# --- תוצאה: getter detail ---
result_get_detail() {
  # מחזירה את פרטי התוצאה הנוכחיים (RESULT_DETAIL)
  printf '%s\n' "$RESULT_DETAIL"
}

# --- תוצאה: success ---
result_success() {
  result_set "$RESULT_SUCCESS" "$EXIT_OK" "${1:-}"
}

# --- תוצאה: policy applied ---
result_policy_applied() {
  result_set "$RESULT_SUCCESS" "$EXIT_OK" "${1:-}"
  RESULT_POLICY_STATUS="applied"
  RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS="written"
}

# --- תוצאה: operation failed ---
result_operation_failed() {
  result_set "$RESULT_OPERATION_FAILED" "$EXIT_OPERATION" "${1:-}"
}

# --- תוצאה: validation failed ---
result_validation_failed() {
  result_set "$RESULT_VALIDATION_FAILED" "$EXIT_VALIDATION" "${1:-}"
}

# --- תוצאה: policy failed ---
result_policy_failed() {
  result_set "$RESULT_POLICY_FAILED" "$EXIT_OPERATION" "${1:-}"
}

# --- תוצאה: policy applied but audit failed ---
result_policy_applied_audit_failed() {
  result_set "$RESULT_POLICY_APPLIED_AUDIT_FAILED" "$EXIT_AUDIT" "${1:-}"
}

# --- תוצאה: unknown policy outcome ---
result_unknown() {
  result_set "$RESULT_UNKNOWN" "$EXIT_INCONSISTENT" "${1:-}"
}

# --- תוצאה: recovery required ---
result_recovery_required() {
  result_set "$RESULT_RECOVERY_REQUIRED" "$EXIT_INCONSISTENT" "${1:-}"
}

# --- תוצאה: partial (batch) ---
result_partial() {
  local detail="${1:-}"
  local policy_status="${2:-unknown}"
  local audit_status="${3:-not_applicable}"
  local policy_detail_code="${4:-}"
  local audit_detail_code="${5:-}"

  if ! result_policy_status_supported "$policy_status" ||
    ! result_audit_status_supported "$audit_status"; then
    printf 'result: ERROR: unsupported policy or audit result status\n' >&2
    return 1
  fi

  result_set "$RESULT_PARTIAL" "$EXIT_PARTIAL" "$detail" || return
  RESULT_POLICY_STATUS="$policy_status"
  RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS="$audit_status"
  RESULT_POLICY_DETAIL_CODE="$policy_detail_code"
  RESULT_AUDIT_DETAIL_CODE="$audit_detail_code"
}

# --- תוצאה: add item to JSON array ---
result_add_item() {
  if (( $# < 6 || $# > 8 )); then
    printf 'result: ERROR: per-item result requires 6 to 8 arguments\n' >&2
    return 2
  fi

  local item_id="$1"
  local state="$2"
  local exit_code="$3"
  local detail="${4:-}"
  local policy_status="$5"
  local audit_status="$6"
  local policy_detail_code="${7:-}"
  local audit_detail_code="${8:-}"
  local item_json

  if [[ -z "$item_id" ]] ||
    ! result_mapping_supported "$state" "$exit_code" ||
    ! result_item_statuses_supported "$state" "$policy_status" "$audit_status"; then
    printf 'result: ERROR: invalid per-item result\n' >&2
    return 1
  fi

  printf -v item_json \
    '{"item_id":"%s","result_state":"%s","exit_code":%s,"detail":"%s","policy_result":{"status":"%s","detail_code":%s},"audit_result":{"status":"%s","detail_code":%s}}' \
    "$(result_json_escape "$item_id")" \
    "$(result_json_escape "$state")" \
    "$exit_code" \
    "$(result_json_escape "$detail")" \
    "$(result_json_escape "$policy_status")" \
    "$(result_json_optional_string "$policy_detail_code")" \
    "$(result_json_escape "$audit_status")" \
    "$(result_json_optional_string "$audit_detail_code")"
  RESULT_ITEM_JSON+=("$item_json")
}

# --- תוצאה: cancelled ---
result_cancelled() {
  result_set "$RESULT_CANCELLED" "$EXIT_CANCELLED" "${1:-}"
}

# --- תוצאה: exit with RESULT_EXIT_CODE ---
result_exit() {
  exit "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE"
}

# --- תוצאה: JSON escape ---
result_json_escape() {
  local LC_ALL=C
  local value="$1"
  local escaped=""
  local character hex
  local index

  # Iterate as bytes so UTF-8 text stays intact while JSON controls are escaped.
  for ((index = 0; index < ${#value}; index++)); do
    character="${value:index:1}"
    case "$character" in
      '"') escaped+='\"' ;;
      '\') escaped+='\\' ;;
      $'\b') escaped+='\b' ;;
      $'\f') escaped+='\f' ;;
      $'\n') escaped+='\n' ;;
      $'\r') escaped+='\r' ;;
      $'\t') escaped+='\t' ;;
      *)
        printf -v hex '%02X' "'$character"
        if (( 16#$hex < 32 || 16#$hex == 127 )); then
          escaped+="\\u00${hex}"
        else
          escaped+="$character"
        fi
        ;;
    esac
  done

  printf '%s' "$escaped"
}

# --- תוצאה: optional string JSON ---
result_json_optional_string() {
  local value="$1"
  if [[ -z "$value" ]]; then
    printf 'null'
  else
    printf '"%s"' "$(result_json_escape "$value")"
  fi
}

# --- תוצאה: הדפסת JSON ---
result_print_json() {
  local state detail correlation_id policy_status audit_status
  local policy_detail_code audit_detail_code item_json
  local item_index
  state="$(result_json_escape "$RESULT_STATE")"
  detail="$(result_json_escape "$RESULT_DETAIL")"
  correlation_id="$(result_json_escape "$RESULT_CORRELATION_ID")"
  policy_status="$(result_json_escape "$RESULT_POLICY_STATUS")"
  audit_status="$(result_json_escape "$RESULT_AUDIT_STATUS")"
  policy_detail_code="$(result_json_optional_string "$RESULT_POLICY_DETAIL_CODE")"
  audit_detail_code="$(result_json_optional_string "$RESULT_AUDIT_DETAIL_CODE")"

  printf '{\n'
  printf '  "result_state": "%s",\n' "$state"
  printf '  "exit_code": %s,\n' "$RESULT_EXIT_CODE"
  printf '  "detail": "%s",\n' "$detail"
  printf '  "correlation_id": "%s",\n' "$correlation_id"
  printf '  "policy_result": {"status":"%s","detail_code":%s},\n' \
    "$policy_status" "$policy_detail_code"
  printf '  "audit_result": {"status":"%s","detail_code":%s},\n' \
    "$audit_status" "$audit_detail_code"
  printf '  "items": ['
  for ((item_index = 0; item_index < ${#RESULT_ITEM_JSON[@]}; item_index++)); do
    item_json="${RESULT_ITEM_JSON[item_index]}"
    if (( item_index > 0 )); then
      printf ','
    fi
    printf '\n    %s' "$item_json"
  done
  if (( ${#RESULT_ITEM_JSON[@]} > 0 )); then
    printf '\n  '
  fi
  printf ']\n'
  printf '}\n'
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Self-test when run directly
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
if [[ "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" == "${0}" ]]; then
  echo "result.sh version $RESULT_MODEL_VERSION"
  echo "Exit codes:"
  echo "  EXIT_OK=$EXIT_OK"
  echo "  EXIT_OPERATION=$EXIT_OPERATION"
  echo "  EXIT_VALIDATION=$EXIT_VALIDATION"
  echo "  EXIT_INCONSISTENT=$EXIT_INCONSISTENT"
  echo "  EXIT_PARTIAL=$EXIT_PARTIAL"
  echo "  EXIT_AUDIT=$EXIT_AUDIT"
  echo "  EXIT_CANCELLED=$EXIT_CANCELLED"
fi

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# stages.sh - TTL lifecycle and commit logic (SRS v4.5 Section 12, 13)
# handles pending -> active -> expired transitions with atomic writes
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# ניהול מחזור חיים של TTL ו לוגיקה של commit (SRS v4.5 סעיפים 12, 13).
# מנהלת מעברים של TTL - pending -> active -> expired עם כתיבה אטומית.
# אחריות: מעקב אחר חוקי TTL פעילים, פקיעת חוקים, וייצוא כללי קבע מאומן.
# משתמשת בנעילות split-state ו-split-policy למניעת תנאים של race.
#
# משתני מפתח: STAGES_ERROR, STAGES_ERROR_CODE, STATE_DIR, TTL_FILE, CLOCK_STATE_FILE,
# RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE, USBAM_LAST_MANAGED_ID, USBAM_LAST_CORRELATION_ID

if [[ -z "${USBAM_STAGES_LOADED+x}" ]]; then
  readonly USBAM_STAGES_VERSION="1.0.0"
  declare -g STAGES_ERROR=""
  declare -g STAGES_ERROR_CODE=""
  declare -g STATE_DIR="${USBAM_STATE_DIR:-/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager}"
  # נתיב ברירת מחדל לתיקיית מצב - בו מאוחסן ttl.json
  declare -g TTL_FILE="${USBAM_TTL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/ttl.json}"
  # נתיב ברירת מחדל לקובץ מצב השעון - משמש לאימות קפיצת שעון
  declare -g CLOCK_STATE_FILE="${USBAM_CLOCK_STATE_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/clock_state.json}"
  # נתיב ברירת מחדל ליומן שחזור - משמש להתאוששות לאחר שחזור
  declare -g RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE="${USBAM_RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/restore_journal.json}"
  # משתני מעקב אחר האחרון שטופל
  declare -g USBAM_LAST_MANAGED_ID=""
  declare -g USBAM_LAST_CORRELATION_ID=""
  declare -gr USBAM_STAGES_LOADED=1
fi

# Source dependencies if not already loaded
SCRIPT_LIB_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
source "$SCRIPT_LIB_DIR/config-reader.sh"
source "$SCRIPT_LIB_DIR/validators.sh"
source "$SCRIPT_LIB_DIR/usbguard-policy.sh"
source "$SCRIPT_LIB_DIR/time-guards.sh"
source "$SCRIPT_LIB_DIR/result.sh"
source "$SCRIPT_LIB_DIR/lock.sh"

if [[ -n "${USBAM_LOCK_FILE:-}" ]]; then
  LOCK_FILE="$USBAM_LOCK_FILE"
fi

# --- stages: הדפסת שגיאה ---
stages_error() {
  STAGES_ERROR="$1"
  STAGES_ERROR_CODE="${2:-stages_failed}"
  printf 'stages: ERROR: %s\n' "$1" >&2
}

# --- stages: הרצת לוגיקה תחת lock ---
stages_run_locked() {
  # מריצה פעולה תחת נעילה - רוכשת lock אם נדרש, מפעילה את הפעולה המבוקשת,
  # ומשחררת את הלock בתום הפעולה. מגנה מפני מצבים של lock_not_owned.
  local operation="$1"
  local acquired_lock=0 rc
  shift

  if (( LOCK_HELD == 0 )); then
    if ! lock_acquire 5; then
      stages_error "unable to acquire the state and policy lock" "lock_failed"
      return 1
    fi
    acquired_lock=1
  elif [[ "$LOCK_OWNER_PID" != "$BASHPID" ]]; then
    stages_error "current process does not own the state and policy lock" "lock_not_owned"
    return 1
  fi

  if "$operation" "$@"; then
    rc=0
  else
    rc=$?
  fi

  if (( acquired_lock )) && ! lock_release; then
    stages_error "failed to release the state and policy lock" "lock_release_failed"
    return 9
  fi
  return "$rc"
}

# --- stages: הבטחת תיקיית state ---
stages_ensure_state_dir() {
  # וודאת תיקיית מצב - דורשת lock Held, אינה מאפשרת סימבול-לינק,
  # יוצרת תיקייה אם אינה קיימת ומגדירה הרשאות 700
  if (( LOCK_HELD == 0 )) || [[ "$LOCK_OWNER_PID" != "$BASHPID" ]]; then
    stages_error "state directory changes require the global lock" "lock_not_held"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -L "$STATE_DIR" ]]; then
    stages_error "state directory must not be a symbolic link" "unsafe_state_dir"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "$STATE_DIR" ]]; then
    if ! mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR"; then
      stages_error "unable to create state directory $STATE_DIR" "state_dir_unavailable"
      return 1
    fi
  fi
  if ! chmod 700 "$STATE_DIR"; then
    stages_error "unable to secure state directory $STATE_DIR" "state_dir_permissions"
    return 1
  fi
  return 0
}

# --- stages: אתחול TTL file אם חסר ---
_stages_init_ttl_file_if_missing() {
  # אתחול ttl.json אם אינו קיים - וודאת תיקיית מצב, יצירת JSON ראשוני אם נדרש,
  # ושימוש בכתיבה אטומית דרך atomic-write.py
  if ! stages_ensure_state_dir; then
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -f "$TTL_FILE" ]]; then
    local initial_json='{"schema_version":1,"records":[]}'
    local atomic_helper="$SCRIPT_LIB_DIR/atomic-write.py"
    if [[ ! -r "$atomic_helper" ]] ||
      ! python3 "$atomic_helper" --file "$TTL_FILE" --data "$initial_json"; then
      stages_error "failed to initialize ttl.json atomically" "init_failed"
      return 1
    fi
  fi
  return 0
}

# Create a pending record JSON object (not yet written to file)
# --- stages: יצירת record ממתין ---
stages_create_pending_record() {
  # יוצרת אובייקט JSON של רשומת pending - אינה כתובה לקובץ עד לשלב מאוחר יותר
  local rule="$1"
  local expires_at="$2"
  local correlation_id="${3:-}"

  if (( $# < 2 || $# > 3 )); then
    stages_error "stages_create_pending_record requires rule and expires_at" "invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi

  # Validate rule via usbguard-policy helper
  # אימות חוק באמצעות helper של usbguard-policy
  if ! declare -F usbguard_validate_rule >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    stages_error "usbguard policy validator not loaded" "dependency_missing"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! usbguard_validate_rule "$rule"; then
    stages_error "invalid rule for pending record" "invalid_rule"
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ ! "$expires_at" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || (( expires_at <= 0 )); then
    stages_error "expires_at must be positive integer" "invalid_expires_at"
    return 1
  fi

  local managed_id
  managed_id="$(generate_uuid_v4)"
  if ! validators_is_uuid_v4 "$managed_id"; then
    stages_error "failed to generate valid managed_id" "uuid_generation_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ -z "$correlation_id" ]]; then
    correlation_id="$(generate_uuid_v4)"
  fi
  if ! validators_is_uuid_v4 "$correlation_id"; then
    stages_error "correlation_id must be UUID v4" "invalid_correlation_id"
    return 1
  fi

  local canonical_rule
  canonical_rule="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$rule")"
  local rule_hash
  rule_hash="$(validators_hash_rule "$canonical_rule")"

  USBAM_LAST_MANAGED_ID="$managed_id"
  USBAM_LAST_CORRELATION_ID="$correlation_id"

  # Build JSON record
  # Build JSON record with managed_id, rule, sha256, expires_at, status, and correlation_id
  jq -n \
    --arg managed_id "$managed_id" \
    --arg rule "$canonical_rule" \
    --arg sha256 "$rule_hash" \
    --argjson expires_at "$expires_at" \
    --arg status "pending" \
    --arg correlation_id "$correlation_id" \
    '{
      managed_id: $managed_id,
      rule: $rule,
      rule_sha256: $sha256,
      expires_at: $expires_at,
      status: $status,
      correlation_id: $correlation_id
    }'
}

# Atomically add a record to ttl.json
# --- stages: הוספת record ---
_stages_add_record() {
  # מוספת רשומה חדשה ל-ttl.json בכתיבה אטומית
  # מאמתת תוכן קיים, מוסיפה את הרשומה החדשה via jq, ומבצעת write אטומי
  local record_json="$1"
  local file_path="${2:-$TTL_FILE}"

  if (( $# < 1 || $# > 2 )); then
    stages_error "stages_add_record requires record JSON" "invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! stages_ensure_state_dir; then
    return 1
  fi
  if ! stages_init_ttl_file_if_missing; then
    return 1
  fi

  if ! validate_ttl_json "$file_path"; then
    stages_error "existing ttl.json invalid, cannot add record: $VALIDATORS_ERROR" "existing_invalid"
    return 1
  fi

  # Append record
  local new_content
  if ! new_content="$(jq --argjson new_record "$record_json" '.records += [$new_record]' "$file_path")"; then
    stages_error "failed to append record to ttl.json" "jq_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  local atomic_helper="$SCRIPT_LIB_DIR/atomic-write.py"
  if [[ ! -r "$atomic_helper" ]] ||
    ! python3 "$atomic_helper" --file "$file_path" --data "$new_content"; then
    stages_error "atomic write of ttl.json failed" "atomic_write_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! validate_ttl_json "$file_path"; then
    stages_error "ttl.json invalid after adding record" "post_write_invalid"
    return 1
  fi

  return 0
}

# Update record status with transition validation
# --- stages: עדכון סטטוס ---
_stages_update_status() {
  # מעדכנת את סטטוס הרשומה עם אימות מעבר סטטוס
  # מאמתת מעבר חוקי (pending->active, expired->...), וכותבת את השינוי אטומי
  local managed_id="$1"
  local new_status="$2"
  local file_path="${3:-$TTL_FILE}"

  if (( $# < 2 || $# > 3 )); then
    stages_error "stages_update_status requires managed_id and new_status" "invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! validators_is_uuid_v4 "$managed_id"; then
    stages_error "managed_id must be UUID v4" "invalid_managed_id"
    return 1
  fi

  case "$new_status" in
    pending|active|inconsistent|recovery_required|expired)
      ;;
    *)
      stages_error "invalid target status $new_status" "invalid_status"
      return 1
      ;;
  esac

  if ! validate_ttl_json "$file_path"; then
    stages_error "ttl.json invalid before status update" "existing_invalid"
    return 1
  fi

  local current_status
  current_status="$(jq -r ".records[] | select(.managed_id == \"$managed_id\") | .status // empty" "$file_path")"
  if [[ -z "$current_status" ]]; then
    stages_error "managed_id $managed_id not found" "not_found"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! validate_ttl_record_transition "$current_status" "$new_status"; then
    stages_error "transition $current_status -> $new_status not allowed for $managed_id" "invalid_transition"
    return 1
  fi

  local new_content
  if ! new_content="$(jq --arg mid "$managed_id" --arg ns "$new_status" '(.records[] | select(.managed_id == $mid) | .status) = $ns' "$file_path")"; then
    stages_error "failed to update status via jq" "jq_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  local atomic_helper="$SCRIPT_LIB_DIR/atomic-write.py"
  if [[ ! -r "$atomic_helper" ]] ||
    ! python3 "$atomic_helper" --file "$file_path" --data "$new_content"; then
    stages_error "atomic write failed for status update" "atomic_write_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! validate_ttl_json "$file_path"; then
    stages_error "ttl.json invalid after status update" "post_write_invalid"
    return 1
  fi

  return 0
}

# Full temporary approval transaction (stages.sh core)
# Usage: stages_approve_temporary <rule> <ttl_seconds> [correlation_id]
# Returns managed_id on success, updates ttl.json to active
# עסקת אישוי זמני מלא (ליבת stages.sh)
# שימוש: stages_approve_temporary <rule> <ttl_seconds> [correlation_id]
# מחזירה managed_id בהצלחה, מעדכנת ttl.json למצב active
# --- stages: אישור זמני ---
_stages_approve_temporary() {
  # Full temporary approval transaction (stages.sh core)
  # Usage: stages_approve_temporary <rule> <ttl_seconds> [correlation_id]
  # Returns managed_id on success, updates ttl.json to active
  # עסקת אישוי זמני מלא (ליבת stages.sh)
  # שימוש: stages_approve_temporary <rule> <ttl_seconds> [correlation_id]
  # מחזירה managed_id בהצלחה, מעדכנת ttl.json למצב active
  local rule="$1"
  local ttl_seconds="$2"
  local correlation_id="${3:-}"
  local device_id="${4:-}"
  local expires_at current_epoch

  if (( $# < 2 || $# > 4 )); then
    stages_error "stages_approve_temporary requires rule, ttl_seconds, and optional device ID" "invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ ! "$ttl_seconds" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || (( ttl_seconds < 60 )); then
    stages_error "ttl_seconds must be >=60" "invalid_ttl"
    return 1
  fi

  # Check restore journal - if exists, block
  # בדיקת יומן שחזור - אם קיים, חסימת כל שינוי מדיניות
  if [[ -f "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" ]]; then
    stages_error "restore journal exists, recovery required before any policy change" "restore_journal_exists"
    return 9
  fi

  if ! is_g01_verified; then
    stages_error "G-01 verification is required before policy changes" "g01_not_verified"
    return 7
  fi

  if ! validate_ttl_json "$TTL_FILE"; then
    stages_error "ttl.json is invalid; recovery is required before policy changes" "existing_invalid"
    return 9
  fi

  local canonical_rule
  canonical_rule="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$rule")"
  if ! usbguard_check_precedence "$canonical_rule"; then
    stages_error "precedence conflict for rule $canonical_rule" "precedence_conflict"
    return 7
  fi

  local line active_id active_rule
  for line in "${USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES[@]}"; do
    if usbguard_parse_rule_line "$line" active_id active_rule &&
      [[ "$active_rule" == "$canonical_rule" ]]; then
      stages_error "matching rule already exists in active policy" "duplicate_rule"
      return 7
    fi
  done

  if usbguard_rule_is_persistent "$canonical_rule"; then
    stages_error "matching permanent rule already exists; TTL would be redundant" \
      "persistent_overlap"
    return 7
  else
    local persistent_check_status=$?
    if (( persistent_check_status != 1 )); then
      stages_error "persistent policy could not be checked safely" \
        "${USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE:-persistent_policy_unavailable}"
      return 9
    fi
  fi

  local has_records
  has_records="$(has_non_expired_records "$TTL_FILE")"
  if ! detect_clock_jump "$CLOCK_STATE_FILE" "$has_records"; then
    local trust_status="$CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS"
    stages_error "clock not trusted, cannot create TTL record: $trust_status ($CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE)" "clock_untrusted"
    return 9
  fi

  # Get current epoch only after establishing a trusted clock sample.
  # קריאת אפוך נוכחי רק לאחר establishment of a trusted clock sample
  if ! current_epoch="$(date +%s)"; then
    stages_error "unable to read current epoch" "clock_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi
  expires_at=$((current_epoch + ttl_seconds))

  # Step 1: Create pending record
  # שלב 1: יצירת רשומת pending
  local pending_record
  if ! pending_record="$(stages_create_pending_record "$rule" "$expires_at" "$correlation_id")"; then
    return 1
  fi
  local managed_id
  managed_id="$(echo "$pending_record" | jq -r .managed_id 2>/dev/null)"
  if [[ -z "$managed_id" || "$managed_id" == "null" ]]; then
    managed_id="$USBAM_LAST_MANAGED_ID"
  fi
  local corr_id
  corr_id="$(echo "$pending_record" | jq -r .correlation_id 2>/dev/null)"
  if [[ -z "$corr_id" || "$corr_id" == "null" ]]; then
    corr_id="$USBAM_LAST_CORRELATION_ID"
  fi

  # Step 2: Write pending to ttl.json
  # שלב 2: כתיבת pending ל-ttl.json
  if ! stages_add_record "$pending_record" "$TTL_FILE"; then
    return 1
  fi

  # A live device and its future policy rule are separate USBGuard operations.
  # התקן חי וממשת המדיניות העתידית הם פעולות נפרדות של USBGuard.
  if [[ -n "$device_id" ]] && ! approve_device "$device_id"; then
    if ! stages_update_status "$managed_id" "recovery_required" "$TTL_FILE"; then
      stages_error "device approval failed and TTL record could not be marked for recovery: ${STAGES_ERROR_CODE:-status_update_failed}" \
        "atomic_write_failed"
      return 9
    fi
    stages_error "device approval failed after TTL record creation" "device_approval_failed"
    return 9
  fi

  # Try to add temporary rule
  if ! add_temporary_rule "$canonical_rule"; then
    # Compensation: check actual state
    # If rule was actually added but verification failed, we need to handle
    if usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical "$canonical_rule" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      # Rule exists, but we are in inconsistent state - try to update to active if we can verify
      if ! stages_update_status "$managed_id" "inconsistent" "$TTL_FILE"; then
        stages_error "temporary rule outcome is uncertain and TTL record could not be marked inconsistent: ${STAGES_ERROR_CODE:-status_update_failed}" \
          "atomic_write_failed"
        return 9
      fi
      stages_error "temporary rule added but verification uncertain" "inconsistent_state"
      return 9
    else
      local rule_check_error="$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE"
      if ! stages_update_status "$managed_id" "recovery_required" "$TTL_FILE"; then
        stages_error "temporary rule failed and TTL record could not be marked for recovery: ${STAGES_ERROR_CODE:-status_update_failed}" \
          "atomic_write_failed"
        return 9
      fi
      if [[ -n "$device_id" ]] || [[ "$rule_check_error" != "rule_not_found" ]]; then
        stages_error "temporary approval outcome is uncertain: $USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" "inconsistent_state"
        return 9
      fi
      stages_error "failed to add temporary rule: $USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE" "policy_failed"
      return 1
    fi
  fi

  # Step 4: Verify rule exists and device approved
  if ! usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical "$canonical_rule" >/dev/null; then
    stages_update_status "$managed_id" "inconsistent" "$TTL_FILE" || true
    stages_error "temporary rule not found after addition" "verification_failed"
    return 9
  fi

  # Step 5: Update to active
  if ! stages_update_status "$managed_id" "active" "$TTL_FILE"; then
    # If status update fails after policy applied, we are in a critical state
    # The rule is active but ttl.json still pending - this is inconsistent
    stages_error "policy applied but ttl.json update to active failed" "atomic_write_failed"
    return 9
  fi

  printf '%s\n' "$managed_id"
  return 0
}

# Permanent approval transaction
# --- stages: אישור קבוע ---
_stages_approve_permanent() {
  # עסקת אישוי קבוע - בודקת תנאים מקדימים, מוסיפה חוק קבוע,
  # ואם צוין התקן - מאשרת אותו. תומכת בסיווג שגיאות שונות.
  local rule="$1"
  local correlation_id="${2:-}"
  local device_id="${3:-}"

  if (( $# < 1 || $# > 3 )); then
    stages_error "stages_approve_permanent requires rule and optional device ID" "invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ -f "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" ]]; then
    stages_error "restore journal exists, recovery required" "restore_journal_exists"
    return 9
  fi

  if ! is_g01_verified; then
    stages_error "G-01 verification is required before policy changes" "g01_not_verified"
    return 7
  fi

  local canonical_rule
  canonical_rule="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$rule")"

  if ! usbguard_check_precedence "$canonical_rule"; then
    stages_error "precedence conflict" "precedence_conflict"
    return 7
  fi

  if ! add_permanent_rule "$canonical_rule"; then
    local policy_error="$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE"
    case "$policy_error" in
      verification_failed|persistence_verification_failed|command_failed|command_timeout)
        stages_error "permanent rule outcome is uncertain: $policy_error" \
          "permanent_rule_outcome_unknown"
        return 9
        ;;
      precedence_conflict|duplicate_rule)
        stages_error "permanent rule rejected: $policy_error" "precedence_conflict"
        return 7
        ;;
      *)
        stages_error "failed to add permanent rule: $policy_error" "policy_failed"
        return 1
        ;;
    esac
  fi

  if [[ -n "$device_id" ]] && ! approve_device "$device_id"; then
    stages_error "permanent rule was added but device approval failed" \
      "device_approval_failed_after_rule"
    return 9
  fi

  # For permanent, we don't create TTL record, but we should audit
  printf 'Permanent rule added: %s (ID %s)\n' "$canonical_rule" "$USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID"
  return 0
}

# --- stages: ייבוא זמני ---
_stages_import_temporary() {
  local rule="$1"
  local expires_at="$2"
  local canonical_rule current_epoch has_records pending_record managed_id
  local active_id active_rule line

  if (( $# != 2 )) || [[ ! "$expires_at" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || (( expires_at <= 0 )); then
    stages_error "stages_import_temporary requires a rule and positive absolute expiry" \
      "invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -f "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" || -L "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" ]]; then
    stages_error "restore journal exists, recovery required before any policy change" \
      "restore_journal_exists"
    return 9
  fi
  if ! is_g01_verified; then
    stages_error "G-01 verification is required before policy changes" "g01_not_verified"
    return 7
  fi
  if ! validate_ttl_json "$TTL_FILE"; then
    stages_error "ttl.json is invalid; recovery is required before policy changes" "existing_invalid"
    return 9
  fi
  if ! usbguard_validate_rule "$rule"; then
    stages_error "invalid imported temporary rule" "invalid_rule"
    return 1
  fi
  canonical_rule="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$rule")"
  if ! usbguard_check_precedence "$canonical_rule"; then
    stages_error "precedence conflict for imported rule" "precedence_conflict"
    return 7
  fi
  for line in "${USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES[@]}"; do
    if usbguard_parse_rule_line "$line" active_id active_rule &&
      [[ "$active_rule" == "$canonical_rule" ]]; then
      stages_error "matching rule already exists in active policy" "duplicate_rule"
      return 7
    fi
  done
  if usbguard_rule_is_persistent "$canonical_rule"; then
    stages_error "matching permanent rule already exists; TTL would be redundant" \
      "persistent_overlap"
    return 7
  else
    local persistent_check_status=$?
    if (( persistent_check_status != 1 )); then
      stages_error "persistent policy could not be checked safely" \
        "${USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE:-persistent_policy_unavailable}"
      return 9
    fi
  fi
  has_records="$(has_non_expired_records "$TTL_FILE")"
  if ! detect_clock_jump "$CLOCK_STATE_FILE" "$has_records"; then
    stages_error "clock not trusted, cannot import TTL record: $CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS" \
      "clock_untrusted"
    return 9
  fi
  if ! current_epoch="$(date +%s)" || (( expires_at <= current_epoch )); then
    stages_error "imported TTL has expired or the current clock is unavailable" \
      "expired_import"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! pending_record="$(stages_create_pending_record "$canonical_rule" "$expires_at")"; then
    return 1
  fi
  managed_id="$(printf '%s' "$pending_record" | jq -r '.managed_id // empty')"
  if [[ -z "$managed_id" ]]; then
    managed_id="$USBAM_LAST_MANAGED_ID"
  fi
  if [[ ! "$managed_id" =~ ^[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}$ ]] ||
    ! stages_add_record "$pending_record" "$TTL_FILE"; then
    stages_error "unable to persist imported pending TTL record" "atomic_write_failed"
    return 9
  fi
  if ! add_temporary_rule "$canonical_rule"; then
    local policy_error="$USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE"
    if usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical "$canonical_rule" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      stages_update_status "$managed_id" "inconsistent" "$TTL_FILE" || true
      stages_error "imported temporary rule outcome is uncertain" "inconsistent_state"
      return 9
    fi
    if ! stages_update_status "$managed_id" "recovery_required" "$TTL_FILE"; then
      stages_error "failed rule addition and TTL recovery marker write" "atomic_write_failed"
      return 9
    fi
    stages_error "failed to add imported temporary rule: $policy_error" "policy_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical "$canonical_rule" >/dev/null; then
    stages_update_status "$managed_id" "inconsistent" "$TTL_FILE" || true
    stages_error "imported temporary rule was not found after addition" "verification_failed"
    return 9
  fi
  if ! stages_update_status "$managed_id" "active" "$TTL_FILE"; then
    stages_error "imported policy applied but TTL activation failed" "atomic_write_failed"
    return 9
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "$managed_id"
}

# --- stages: wrapper אתחול ---
stages_init_ttl_file_if_missing() {
  stages_run_locked _stages_init_ttl_file_if_missing "$@"
}

# --- stages: wrapper הוספה ---
stages_add_record() {
  stages_run_locked _stages_add_record "$@"
}

# --- stages: wrapper עדכון ---
stages_update_status() {
  stages_run_locked _stages_update_status "$@"
}

# --- stages: wrapper אישור זמני ---
stages_approve_temporary() {
  stages_run_locked _stages_approve_temporary "$@"
}

# --- stages: wrapper אישור קבוע ---
stages_approve_permanent() {
  stages_run_locked _stages_approve_permanent "$@"
}

# --- stages: wrapper ייבוא זמני ---
stages_import_temporary() {
  stages_run_locked _stages_import_temporary "$@"
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Calendar-time checks using verified system NTP synchronization evidence.
# בדיקות זמן לוח שנה באמצעות ראיות נמוטרון NTP מאומתות.
#
# --- הסבר בעברית ---
# בדיקות זמן לוחיות באמצעות ראיות אימות סנכרון NTP של מערכת ההפעלה.
# אחריות: להבטיח שהתאריכים והשעונים במערכת מהימנים לפני כל פעולת מדיניות.
# תומך בבדיקת קפיצות שעון, פג תוקף TTL, ואילוצי זמן גלובליים.
#
# תלויות: clock_state.py - מצב שעון נוכחי, NVIDIA NTP provider status

if [[ -z "${USBAM_TIME_GUARDS_LOADED+x}" ]]; then
  readonly USBAM_TIME_GUARDS_VERSION="1.0.0"
  readonly USBAM_CLOCK_STATE_HELPER="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)/clock_state.py"
  declare -g CLOCK_STATE_PATH="/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager/clock_state.json"
  declare -g CLOCK_SYNC_PROVIDER=""
  declare -g CLOCK_SYNC_STATUS=""
  declare -g CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS="untrusted"
  declare -g CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_sync_provider_unverified"
  declare -gr USBAM_TIME_GUARDS_LOADED=1
fi

clock_error() {
  printf 'time-guards: ERROR: %s\n' "$1" >&2
}

clock_sync_evidence() {
  # איסוף ראיות סנכרון שעון - בודקת provider NTP (ntpsec/ntp),
  # status של timedatectl, ועמיתי NTP תקינים דרך ntpq.
  # מחזירה CLOCK_SYNC_PROVIDER, CLOCK_SYNC_STATUS, ו-CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE
  CLOCK_SYNC_PROVIDER=""
  CLOCK_SYNC_STATUS=""
  CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_sync_evidence_unavailable"

  local provider sync_status peers
  if ! command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1 ||
    ! command -v systemctl >/dev/null 2>&1 ||
    ! command -v timedatectl >/dev/null 2>&1 ||
    ! command -v ntpq >/dev/null 2>&1 ||
    ! command -v awk >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_sync_tools_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi

  provider=""
  if timeout --foreground --signal=TERM --kill-after=1s 5s \
    systemctl is-active --quiet ntpsec; then
    provider="ntpsec"
  elif timeout --foreground --signal=TERM --kill-after=1s 5s \
    systemctl is-active --quiet ntp; then
    provider="ntp"
  else
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_sync_provider_inactive"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! sync_status="$(
    timeout --foreground --signal=TERM --kill-after=1s 5s \
      timedatectl show -p NTPSynchronized --value 2>/dev/null
  )"; then
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_sync_status_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$sync_status" != "yes" ]]; then
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_sync_not_synchronized"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! peers="$(
    timeout --foreground --signal=TERM --kill-after=1s 5s \
      ntpq -pn 2>/dev/null
  )" || ! awk '
    $1 ~ /^\*/ {
      selected++
      if ($3 !~ /^[0-9]+$/ || $3 < 1 || $3 > 15 ||
          $7 !~ /^[0-7]+$/ || $7 ~ /^0+$/) {
        invalid = 1
      }
    }
    END { exit !(selected == 1 && !invalid) }
  ' <<< "$peers"; then
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_sync_peer_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi

  CLOCK_SYNC_PROVIDER="$provider"
  CLOCK_SYNC_STATUS="synchronized"
  CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_sync_verified"
  return 0
}

get_current_epoch() {
  local epoch
  if ! epoch="$(date +%s)" || [[ ! "$epoch" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    clock_error "unable to read a non-negative Unix Epoch"
    return 1
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "$epoch"
}

clock_decimal_less_than() {
  local left="$1"
  local right="$2"

  while [[ "${#left}" -gt 1 && "${left:0:1}" == "0" ]]; do
    left="${left:1}"
  done
  while [[ "${#right}" -gt 1 && "${right:0:1}" == "0" ]]; do
    right="${right:1}"
  done
  if [[ "${#left}" -lt "${#right}" ]]; then
    return 0
  fi
  if [[ "${#left}" -gt "${#right}" ]]; then
    return 1
  fi
  [[ "$left" < "$right" ]]
}

is_clock_reasonable() {
  local epoch minimum
  if (( $# > 1 )); then
    clock_error "is_clock_reasonable accepts at most one Epoch value"
    return 1
  fi
  if (( $# == 1 )); then
    epoch="$1"
  elif ! epoch="$(get_current_epoch)"; then
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! "$epoch" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    clock_error "Epoch must be a non-negative integer"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! minimum="$(clock_config_value MIN_REASONABLE_EPOCH)"; then
    clock_error "MIN_REASONABLE_EPOCH is unavailable"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! "$minimum" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] ||
    ! clock_decimal_less_than "$minimum" "$epoch"; then
    return 1
  fi
}

clock_config_value() {
  local key="$1"
  local declaration
  declaration="$(declare -p CONFIG_VALUES 2>/dev/null || true)"
  if [[ "$declaration" == "declare -A CONFIG_VALUES="* ]]; then
    if [[ -n "${CONFIG_VALUES[$key]+present}" ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "${CONFIG_VALUES[$key]}"
      return 0
    fi
  fi
  if [[ -n "${!key-}" ]]; then
    printf '%s\n' "${!key}"
    return 0
  fi
  return 1
}

clock_read_boot_id() {
  local boot_id
  if ! IFS= read -r boot_id < /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id ||
    [[ ! "$boot_id" =~ ^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$ ]]; then
    clock_error "unable to read a valid boot_id"
    return 1
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "$boot_id"
}

clock_read_boottime_seconds() {
  local uptime
  if ! IFS=' ' read -r uptime _ < /proc/uptime ||
    [[ ! "$uptime" =~ ^([0-9]+)([.][0-9]+)?$ ]]; then
    clock_error "unable to read CLOCK_BOOTTIME seconds"
    return 1
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "${uptime%%.*}"
}

detect_clock_jump() {
  local state_path="${1:-$CLOCK_STATE_PATH}"
  local has_records="${2:-}"
  local epoch boot_id boottime provider sync_status minimum max_jump
  local helper_status

  CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS="untrusted"
  CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_evidence_unavailable"
  if (( $# > 2 )) || [[ -z "$state_path" ]] ||
    [[ "$has_records" != "yes" && "$has_records" != "no" ]]; then
    clock_error "detect_clock_jump requires a state path and yes/no non-expired-record flag"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! epoch="$(get_current_epoch)" || ! is_clock_reasonable "$epoch"; then
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_epoch_unreasonable"
    return 9
  fi
  if ! clock_sync_evidence; then
    if [[ "$has_records" == "yes" ]]; then
      CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS="recovery_required"
      CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_state_recovery_required"
      return 9
    fi
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="${CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE:-clock_sync_provider_unverified}"
    return 9
  fi
  provider="$CLOCK_SYNC_PROVIDER"
  sync_status="$CLOCK_SYNC_STATUS"
  if [[ -z "$provider" || "$sync_status" != "synchronized" ]]; then
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_sync_not_verified"
    return 9
  fi
  if ! boot_id="$(clock_read_boot_id)" ||
    ! boottime="$(clock_read_boottime_seconds)"; then
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_monotonic_evidence_unavailable"
    return 9
  fi
  if ! minimum="$(clock_config_value MIN_REASONABLE_EPOCH)" ||
    ! max_jump="$(clock_config_value MAX_CLOCK_JUMP_SECONDS)" ||
    [[ ! "$minimum" =~ ^[0-9]+$ || ! "$max_jump" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    clock_error "clock threshold configuration is missing or invalid"
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_configuration_invalid"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -r "$USBAM_CLOCK_STATE_HELPER" ]]; then
    clock_error "clock-state validator is missing or unreadable"
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_state_helper_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! "$epoch" =~ ^[0-9]+$ || ! "$boottime" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_sample_invalid"
    return 9
  fi

  if CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS="$(
    python3 "$USBAM_CLOCK_STATE_HELPER" \
      --state "$state_path" \
      --epoch "$epoch" \
      --provider "$provider" \
      --sync-status "$sync_status" \
      --boot-id "$boot_id" \
      --boottime-seconds "$boottime" \
      --min-reasonable-epoch "$minimum" \
      --max-clock-jump-seconds "$max_jump" \
      --has-non-expired-records "$has_records"
  )"; then
    CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_trusted"
    return 0
  else
    helper_status=$?
  fi

  case "$CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS" in
    recovery_required)
      CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_state_recovery_required"
      return 9
      ;;
    untrusted_clock)
      CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_untrusted"
      return 9
      ;;
    *)
      CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS="untrusted"
      CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE="clock_state_error"
      if (( helper_status != 9 )); then
        clock_error "clock-state evaluation or durable state update failed"
        return 1
      fi
      return 9
      ;;
  esac
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# USBGuard Approval Manager - TUI whiptail wrappers (Phase 4 - SRS v4.5)
# Provides safe whiptail wrappers with control-char validation
# SEC-01, FR-02 compliant: no source/eval, strict input validation

if [[ -z "${USBAM_TUI_LOADED+x}" ]]; then
  readonly USBAM_TUI_VERSION="1.0.0"
  readonly USBAM_TUI_DEFAULT_HEIGHT=20
  readonly USBAM_TUI_DEFAULT_WIDTH=78
  readonly USBAM_TUI_DEFAULT_MENU_HEIGHT=10
  declare -gr USBAM_TUI_LOADED=1
fi

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Internal helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
tui_error() {
  printf 'tui: ERROR: %s\n' "$1" >&2
}

tui_has_control_chars() {
  # Returns 0 if contains any control char (including newline, tab, etc.)
  local s="$1"
  [[ "$s" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]
}

tui_has_control_chars_except_newline_tab() {
  # Returns 0 if contains control chars other than \n and \t
  # Allows multiline messages in msgbox
  local s="$1"
  local tmp="${s//$'\n'/}"
  tmp="${tmp//$'\t'/}"
  tmp="${tmp//$'\r'/}"  # strip CR, then check remaining
  [[ "$tmp" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]
}

tui_validate_no_control() {
  local value="$1"
  local context="${2:-field}"
  if tui_has_control_chars "$value"; then
    tui_error "$context contains control characters"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$value" == -* ]]; then
    tui_error "$context starts with dash (potential option injection)"
    return 1
  fi
  return 0
}

tui_validate_allow_newline() {
  local value="$1"
  local context="${2:-field}"
  if tui_has_control_chars_except_newline_tab "$value"; then
    tui_error "$context contains forbidden control characters"
    return 1
  fi
  # Also forbid leading dash for title but not for body? Check only title elsewhere
  return 0
}

tui_require_whiptail() {
  if ! command -v whiptail >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    tui_error "whiptail is not installed or not in PATH"
    return 1
  fi
  return 0
}

tui_validate_positive_int() {
  local val="$1"
  local name="${2:-value}"
  if [[ ! "$val" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || (( val <= 0 )); then
    tui_error "$name must be positive integer, got '$val'"
    return 1
  fi
  return 0
}

tui_sanitize_display() {
  # Sanitize string for safe display in whiptail list: remove control chars, truncate
  local input="$1"
  local max_len="${2:-80}"
  local one_line="${input//$'\n'/ }"
  one_line="${one_line//$'\r'/ }"
  one_line="${one_line//$'\t'/ }"
  # Replace any remaining control chars with ?
  # Use bash pattern replacement in loop for portability
  local cleaned=""
  local i ch
  for ((i=0; i<${#one_line}; i++)); do
    ch="${one_line:i:1}"
    if [[ "$ch" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]; then
      cleaned+='?'
    else
      cleaned+="$ch"
    fi
  done
  if (( ${#cleaned} > max_len )); then
    cleaned="${cleaned:0:$((max_len-3))}..."
  fi
  printf '%s' "$cleaned"
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public whiptail wrappers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

tui_msgbox() {
  # tui_msgbox <title> <message> [height] [width]
  if (( $# < 2 || $# > 4 )); then
    tui_error "tui_msgbox requires title, message, [height], [width]"
    return 2
  fi
  local title="$1"
  local message="$2"
  local height="${3:-$USBAM_TUI_DEFAULT_HEIGHT}"
  local width="${4:-$USBAM_TUI_DEFAULT_WIDTH}"

  if ! tui_validate_no_control "$title" "msgbox title"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_allow_newline "$message" "msgbox message"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$height" "height"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$width" "width"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_require_whiptail; then return 1; fi

  local rc
  set +e
  whiptail --title "$title" --msgbox "$message" "$height" "$width"
  rc=$?
  set -e
  return $rc
}

tui_yesno() {
  # tui_yesno <title> <question> [height] [width] -> 0=yes, 1=no, 255=cancel/esc
  if (( $# < 2 || $# > 4 )); then
    tui_error "tui_yesno requires title, question, [height], [width]"
    return 2
  fi
  local title="$1"
  local question="$2"
  local height="${3:-$USBAM_TUI_DEFAULT_HEIGHT}"
  local width="${4:-$USBAM_TUI_DEFAULT_WIDTH}"

  if ! tui_validate_no_control "$title" "yesno title"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_allow_newline "$question" "yesno question"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$height" "height"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$width" "width"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_require_whiptail; then return 1; fi

  local rc
  set +e
  whiptail --title "$title" --yesno "$question" "$height" "$width"
  rc=$?
  set -e
  return $rc
}

tui_inputbox() {
  # tui_inputbox <title> <prompt> [height] [width] [default]
  # Prints user input to stdout, returns whiptail exit code
  if (( $# < 2 || $# > 5 )); then
    tui_error "tui_inputbox requires title, prompt, [height], [width], [default]"
    return 2
  fi
  local title="$1"
  local prompt="$2"
  local height="${3:-$USBAM_TUI_DEFAULT_HEIGHT}"
  local width="${4:-$USBAM_TUI_DEFAULT_WIDTH}"
  local default_value="${5:-}"

  if ! tui_validate_no_control "$title" "inputbox title"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_allow_newline "$prompt" "inputbox prompt"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$height" "height"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$width" "width"; then return 1; fi
  if [[ -n "$default_value" ]] && tui_has_control_chars "$default_value"; then
    tui_error "inputbox default contains control characters"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! tui_require_whiptail; then return 1; fi

  local result rc
  set +e
  result=$(whiptail --title "$title" --inputbox "$prompt" "$height" "$width" "$default_value" 3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
  rc=$?
  set -e

  if (( rc == 0 )); then
    if tui_has_control_chars "$result"; then
      tui_error "input contains control characters - rejected"
      return 1
    fi
    printf '%s' "$result"
  fi
  return $rc
}

tui_menu() {
  # tui_menu <title> <prompt> <height> <width> <menu_height> <tag> <item> ...
  # Prints selected tag to stdout
  if (( $# < 6 )); then
    tui_error "tui_menu requires title, prompt, height, width, menu_height, tag/item pairs"
    return 2
  fi
  local title="$1"
  local prompt="$2"
  local height="$3"
  local width="$4"
  local menu_height="$5"
  shift 5

  if (( $# % 2 != 0 )); then
    tui_error "tui_menu requires even number of tag/item arguments"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! tui_validate_no_control "$title" "menu title"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_allow_newline "$prompt" "menu prompt"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$height" "height"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$width" "width"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$menu_height" "menu_height"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_require_whiptail; then return 1; fi

  local -a items=("$@")
  local tag item
  local idx=0
  while (( idx < ${#items[@]} )); do
    tag="${items[idx]}"
    item="${items[idx+1]}"
    if ! tui_validate_no_control "$tag" "menu tag"; then return 1; fi
    # item may be multiline? For menu, we forbid control but allow spaces
    if tui_has_control_chars "$item"; then
      tui_error "menu item contains control characters: tag=$tag"
      return 1
    fi
    idx=$((idx+2))
  done

  local result rc
  set +e
  result=$(whiptail --title "$title" --menu "$prompt" "$height" "$width" "$menu_height" "${items[@]}" 3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
  rc=$?
  set -e

  if (( rc == 0 )); then
    printf '%s' "$result"
  fi
  return $rc
}

tui_checklist() {
  # tui_checklist <title> <prompt> <height> <width> <list_height> <tag> <item> <status> ...
  # Status ON/OFF. Prints selected tags (space-separated, quoted by whiptail) to stdout
  if (( $# < 6 )); then
    tui_error "tui_checklist requires title, prompt, height, width, list_height, tag/item/status triples"
    return 2
  fi
  local title="$1"
  local prompt="$2"
  local height="$3"
  local width="$4"
  local list_height="$5"
  shift 5

  if (( $# % 3 != 0 )); then
    tui_error "tui_checklist requires triples of tag/item/status"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! tui_validate_no_control "$title" "checklist title"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_allow_newline "$prompt" "checklist prompt"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$height" "height"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$width" "width"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_validate_positive_int "$list_height" "list_height"; then return 1; fi
  if ! tui_require_whiptail; then return 1; fi

  local -a entries=("$@")
  local tag item status
  local idx=0
  while (( idx < ${#entries[@]} )); do
    tag="${entries[idx]}"
    item="${entries[idx+1]}"
    status="${entries[idx+2]}"
    if ! tui_validate_no_control "$tag" "checklist tag"; then return 1; fi
    if tui_has_control_chars "$item"; then
      tui_error "checklist item contains control characters: tag=$tag"
      return 1
    fi
    if [[ "$status" != "ON" && "$status" != "OFF" ]]; then
      tui_error "checklist status must be ON or OFF, got '$status'"
      return 1
    fi
    idx=$((idx+3))
  done

  local result rc
  set +e
  result=$(whiptail --title "$title" --checklist "$prompt" "$height" "$width" "$list_height" "${entries[@]}" 3>&1 1>&2 2>&3)
  rc=$?
  set -e

  if (( rc == 0 )); then
    printf '%s' "$result"
  fi
  return $rc
}

  if (( $# < 6 )); then

    if ! tui_validate_no_control "$tag" "radiolist tag"; then return 1; fi
