#!/usr/bin/env bash
# USBGuard Approval Manager - USBGuard Policy Adapter
# Phase 2 Full Implementation - SRS v4.5 Approved 28.10.2026
# This adapter is the ONLY layer that talks to USBGuard.
# Read-only operations are safe everywhere. Write operations require G-01 verification
# and must only run in an isolated lab VM with USB passthrough and recovery route.
# USBGuard Approval Manager - USBGuard Policy Adapter
# שלב 2 מלא יישום - SRS v4.5 מאושר 28.10.2026
# שכבה זו היא היחידה שמדברת עם USBGuard.
# פעולות קריאה הן בטוחות בכל מקוד. פעולות כתיבה דורשות אימות G-01
# וצריכות לרוץ רק ב-VM מעבדה מבודד עם USB passthrough ונתיב התאוששות.

if [[ -z "${USBAM_USB_POLICY_LOADED+x}" ]]; then
  declare -g USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT=""
  declare -g USBAM_POLICY_STDERR=""
  declare -g USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE=""
  declare -g USBAM_POLICY_VERSION=""
  declare -g USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH="${USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH:-/etc/usbguard/usbguard-daemon.conf}"
  declare -ga USBAM_BLOCKED_DEVICES=()
  declare -ga USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES=()
  declare -gA USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS=()
  declare -g USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS="unknown"
  declare -g USBAM_IPC_STATUS="unknown"
  declare -g USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID=""
  declare -g USBAM_MATCHED_RULE_ID=""
  declare -g USBAM_LAST_DEVICE_ID=""
  declare -gr USBAM_USB_POLICY_LOADED=1
fi

source "$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)/lock.sh"

usbguard_policy_error() {
  # מדפיסה הודעת שגיאה עם תגית usbguard-policy ל-stderr
  printf 'usbguard-policy: ERROR: %s\n' "$1" >&2
}

usbguard_policy_require_lock() {
  # וודאת ש-lock Held for the complete operation
  # בודקת אם LOCK_HELD == 0 או ש-BASHPID אינו הבעלים הנוכחי,
  # ומאתחלת USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE ל-lock_not_held
  if (( LOCK_HELD == 0 )) || [[ "$LOCK_OWNER_PID" != "$BASHPID" ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "policy mutations require the global lock for the complete operation"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="lock_not_held"
    return 1
  fi
}

usbguard_policy_timeout() {
  if declare -F get_config >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    local configured_timeout
    if configured_timeout="$(get_config IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS 2>/dev/null)"; then
      printf '%s\n' "$configured_timeout"
      return 0
    fi
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "${IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:-5}"
}

# Internal runner - shared logic for read-only and write
_usbguard_run() {
  # ריצה פנימית משותפת - logic לקריאה וכתיבה ל-USBGuard
  # מאמתת timeout, בודקת נוכחות של timeout ו usbguard CLIs,
  # יוצרת temp_dir לתפוקת פקודה, מריצה את הפקודה עם timeout,
  # אוגרת stdout/stderr, ומחזירה codestatus
  local timeout_seconds temp_dir status
  USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT=""
  USBAM_POLICY_STDERR=""
  USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE=""

  if ! timeout_seconds="$(usbguard_policy_timeout)" ||
    [[ ! "$timeout_seconds" =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS must be a positive integer"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_timeout"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    usbguard_policy_error "the timeout utility is unavailable"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="timeout_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! command -v usbguard >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    usbguard_policy_error "the usbguard CLI is unavailable"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="usbguard_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! temp_dir="$(mktemp -d)"; then
    usbguard_policy_error "unable to create a private command-output directory"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="temporary_storage_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi

  if timeout --foreground --signal=TERM --kill-after=1s \
    "${timeout_seconds}s" usbguard "$@" \
    > "$temp_dir/stdout" 2> "$temp_dir/stderr"; then
    status=0
  else
    status=$?
  fi

  USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT="$(<"$temp_dir/stdout")"
  USBAM_POLICY_STDERR="$(<"$temp_dir/stderr")"
  if ! rm -rf -- "$temp_dir"; then
    usbguard_policy_error "unable to remove private command-output files"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="temporary_storage_cleanup_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  if (( status == 0 )); then
    return 0
  fi
  if (( status == 124 || status == 137 )); then
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="command_timeout"
    usbguard_policy_error "USBGuard query exceeded ${timeout_seconds}s"
    return 1
  fi
  USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="command_failed"
  usbguard_policy_error "USBGuard query failed with exit status $status"
  return 1
}

usbguard_policy_run_readonly() {
  # ריצת פקודת USBGuard לקריאה בלבד
  # תומכת בפקודות: --version, list-rules, list-devices --blocked
  # מזהה פקודות לא נתמכות ומחזירה שגיאה
  if (( $# == 0 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "a read-only USBGuard subcommand is required"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  case "$1:$#" in
    --version:1|list-rules:1|list-devices:1)
      ;;
    list-devices:2)
      if [[ "$2" != "--blocked" ]]; then
        usbguard_policy_error "unsupported read-only USBGuard query"
        USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_command"
        return 1
      fi
      ;;
    *)
      usbguard_policy_error "unsupported read-only USBGuard query"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_command"
      return 1
      ;;
  esac
  _usbguard_run "$@"
}

usbguard_policy_run_write() {
  # ריצת פקודת USBGuard לכתיבה
  # תומכת בפקודות: allow-device, block-device, append-rule, remove-rule
  # דורשת אימות G-01 ו-check ש-lock held לפני הריצה
  if (( $# == 0 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "a USBGuard write subcommand is required"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  case "$1" in
    allow-device|block-device|append-rule|remove-rule)
      : ;;
    *)
      usbguard_policy_error "unsupported USBGuard write operation: $1"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_command"
      return 1
      ;;
  esac
  if ! is_g01_verified; then
    usbguard_policy_error "policy changes require a verified G-01 evidence marker"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="g01_not_verified"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! usbguard_policy_require_lock; then
    return 1
  fi
  _usbguard_run "$@"
}

usbguard_policy_parse_records() {
  local output="$1"
  local -n records_ref="$2"
  local line
  local -a parsed=()

  while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
    [[ -z "$line" ]] && continue
    if [[ ! "$line" =~ ^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+: ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "USBGuard returned an unrecognized record"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_command_output"
      return 1
    fi
    parsed+=("$line")
  done <<< "$output"

  records_ref=("${parsed[@]}")
}

usbguard_canonicalize_rule() {
  local rule="$1"
  # Trim leading/trailing whitespace, preserve internal
  rule="${rule#"${rule%%[![:space:]]*}"}"
  rule="${rule%"${rule##*[![:space:]]}"}"
  printf '%s' "$rule"
}

usbguard_hash_rule() {
  local rule="$1"
  local canonical
  canonical="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$rule")"
  if ! command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    # Fallback to python3
    python3 -c "import hashlib,sys; print(hashlib.sha256(sys.argv[1].encode('utf-8')).hexdigest())" "$canonical"
  else
    printf '%s' "$canonical" | sha256sum | awk '{print $1}'
  fi
}

usbguard_parse_rule_line() {
  local line="$1"
  local id_var="$2"
  local rule_var="$3"
  if [[ "$line" =~ ^[[:space:]]*([0-9]+):[[:space:]]*(.*)$ ]]; then
    local id="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
    local rule="${BASH_REMATCH[2]}"
    rule="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$rule")"
    printf -v "$id_var" '%s' "$id"
    printf -v "$rule_var" '%s' "$rule"
    return 0
  fi
  usbguard_policy_error "invalid rule line format: $line"
  USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_command_output"
  return 1
}

usbguard_validate_device_id() {
  local device_id="$1"
  if [[ ! "$device_id" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "device ID must be a non-negative integer, got '$device_id'"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_device_id"
    return 1
  fi
  return 0
}

usbguard_validate_rule() {
  local rule="$1"
  if [[ -z "$rule" ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "rule must not be empty"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_rule"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$rule" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "rule contains control characters"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_rule"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$rule" == *"00-system.rules"* ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "rule attempts to reference protected 00-system.rules"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="protected_policy"
    return 1
  fi
  local canonical
  canonical="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$rule")"
  if [[ ! "$canonical" =~ ^(allow|block|reject)[[:space:]] ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "rule must start with allow, block or reject"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_rule"
    return 1
  fi
  return 0
}

usbguard_extract_id_pattern() {
  local rule="$1"
  # Extract id VID:PID if present
  if [[ "$rule" =~ id[[:space:]]+([0-9a-fA-F]{4}:[0-9a-fA-F]{4}) ]]; then
    printf '%s' "${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
    return 0
  fi
  printf ''
  return 0
}

# --- Read-only API (unchanged behavior, must pass existing tests) ---

get_usbguard_version() {
  USBAM_POLICY_VERSION=""
  if (( $# != 0 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "get_usbguard_version accepts no arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! usbguard_policy_run_readonly --version; then
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -z "$USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT" ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "USBGuard returned an empty version"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="empty_version"
    return 1
  fi
  USBAM_POLICY_VERSION="$USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT"
  printf '%s\n' "$USBAM_POLICY_VERSION"
}

list_blocked_devices() {
  if (( $# != 0 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "list_blocked_devices accepts no arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  USBAM_BLOCKED_DEVICES=()
  if ! usbguard_policy_run_readonly list-devices --blocked; then
    return 1
  fi
  usbguard_policy_parse_records "$USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT" USBAM_BLOCKED_DEVICES
}

list_active_rules() {
  if (( $# != 0 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "list_active_rules accepts no arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES=()
  if ! usbguard_policy_run_readonly list-rules; then
    return 1
  fi
  usbguard_policy_parse_records "$USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT" USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES
}

get_active_rules() {
  list_active_rules "$@"
}

get_persistent_policy_paths() {
  local raw_line line key value line_numbe
  local rule_file=""
  local rule_folder=""

  USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS=()
  USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE=""
  if (( $# != 0 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "get_persistent_policy_paths accepts no arguments"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -L "$USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH" ||
    ! -f "$USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH" ||
    ! -r "$USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH" ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "USBGuard daemon configuration is unavailable or unsafe"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="daemon_config_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi

  line_number=0
  while IFS= read -r raw_line || [[ -n "$raw_line" ]]; do
    line_number=$((line_number + 1))
    if [[ "$raw_line" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "control character in daemon configuration line $line_number"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_daemon_config"
      return 1
    fi
    line="${raw_line#"${raw_line%%[![:space:]]*}"}"
    line="${line%"${line##*[![:space:]]}"}"
    [[ -z "$line" || "$line" == \#* ]] && continue
    if [[ ! "$line" =~ ^(RuleFile|RuleFolder)[[:space:]]*=[[:space:]]*(.*)$ ]]; then
      continue
    fi

    key="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
    value="${BASH_REMATCH[2]}"
    value="${value#"${value%%[![:space:]]*}"}"
    value="${value%"${value##*[![:space:]]}"}"
    if [[ -z "$value" || "$value" != /* ||
      "$value" == *"/../"* || "$value" == */.. ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "invalid $key path in daemon configuration"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_policy_path"
      return 1
    fi
    if [[ "$value" == *"00-system.rules"* ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "daemon configuration points to protected 00-system.rules"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="protected_policy"
      return 1
    fi
    if [[ "$key" == "RuleFile" ]]; then
      if [[ -n "$rule_file" ]]; then
        usbguard_policy_error "duplicate RuleFile in daemon configuration"
        USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="ambiguous_policy_source"
        return 1
      fi
      rule_file="$value"
    else
      if [[ -n "$rule_folder" ]]; then
        usbguard_policy_error "duplicate RuleFolder in daemon configuration"
        USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="ambiguous_policy_source"
        return 1
      fi
      rule_folder="$value"
    fi
  done < "$USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH"

  if [[ -z "$rule_file" && -z "$rule_folder" ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "daemon configuration does not identify a persistent policy source"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="policy_source_unconfigured"
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ -n "$rule_file" ]]; then
    if [[ -L "$rule_file" || ! -f "$rule_file" || ! -r "$rule_file" ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "configured RuleFile is missing, unsafe, or unreadable"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="policy_source_unavailable"
      return 1
    fi
    USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]="$rule_file"
  fi
  if [[ -n "$rule_folder" ]] &&
    [[ -L "$rule_folder" || ! -d "$rule_folder" ||
      ! -r "$rule_folder" || ! -x "$rule_folder" ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "configured RuleFolder is missing, unsafe, or unreadable"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="policy_source_unavailable"
    USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS=()
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -n "$rule_folder" ]]; then
    USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]="$rule_folder"
  fi
  return 0
}

usbguard_policy_file_contains_rule() {
  local file_path="$1"
  local target_rule="$2"
  local line canonical

  while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
    if [[ "$line" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "control character in persistent policy file: $file_path"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_persistent_policy"
      return 2
    fi
    line="${line#"${line%%[![:space:]]*}"}"
    line="${line%"${line##*[![:space:]]}"}"
    [[ -z "$line" || "$line" == \#* ]] && continue
    if ! usbguard_validate_rule "$line"; then
      usbguard_policy_error "invalid rule in persistent policy file: $file_path"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_persistent_policy"
      return 2
    fi
    canonical="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$line")"
    if [[ "$canonical" == "$target_rule" ]]; then
      return 0
    fi
  done < "$file_path"
  return 1
}

usbguard_rule_is_persistent() {
  local target_rule="$1"
  local path status listing_file
  local -a policy_files=()

  if (( $# != 1 )) || ! usbguard_validate_rule "$target_rule"; then
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_arguments"
    return 2
  fi
  target_rule="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$target_rule")"
  if ! get_persistent_policy_paths; then
    return 2
  fi

  if [[ -n "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]:-}" ]]; then
    path="${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]}"
    if usbguard_policy_file_contains_rule "$path" "$target_rule"; then
      return 0
    else
      status=$?
      if (( status != 1 )); then
        return 2
      fi
    fi
  fi

  if [[ -n "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]:-}" ]]; then
    listing_file="$(mktemp)" || {
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="temporary_storage_unavailable"
      return 2
    }
    if ! find "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]}" \
      -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -print0 > "$listing_file"; then
      rm -f -- "$listing_file" || true
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="persistent_policy_unreadable"
      return 2
    fi

    while IFS= read -r -d '' path; do
      if [[ -L "$path" || ! -f "$path" || ! -r "$path" ]]; then
        rm -f -- "$listing_file" || true
        USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="unsafe_persistent_policy_entry"
        return 2
      fi
      if usbguard_policy_file_contains_rule "$path" "$target_rule"; then
        rm -f -- "$listing_file" || {
          USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="temporary_storage_cleanup_failed"
          return 2
        }
        return 0
      else
        status=$?
        if (( status != 1 )); then
          rm -f -- "$listing_file" || true
          return 2
        fi
      fi
    done < "$listing_file"

    if ! rm -f -- "$listing_file"; then
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="temporary_storage_cleanup_failed"
      return 2
    fi
  fi
  return 1
}

check_daemon_status() {
  local timeout_seconds status
  USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS="unknown"
  USBAM_IPC_STATUS="unknown"
  USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE=""

  if ! timeout_seconds="$(usbguard_policy_timeout)" ||
    [[ ! "$timeout_seconds" =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "IPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS must be a positive integer"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_timeout"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! command -v timeout >/dev/null 2>&1 ||
    ! command -v pgrep >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    usbguard_policy_error "timeout or pgrep utility is unavailable"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="status_utility_unavailable"
    return 1
  fi

  if timeout --foreground --signal=TERM --kill-after=1s \
    "${timeout_seconds}s" pgrep -x usbguard-daemon >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS="running"
  else
    status=$?
    if (( status == 1 )); then
      USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS="stopped"
      USBAM_IPC_STATUS="unavailable"
      return 9
    fi
    if (( status == 124 || status == 137 )); then
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="daemon_check_timeout"
    else
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="daemon_check_failed"
    fi
    usbguard_policy_error "unable to determine USBGuard daemon status"
    return 1
  fi

  if usbguard_policy_run_readonly list-devices; then
    USBAM_IPC_STATUS="connected"
    return 0
  fi
  USBAM_IPC_STATUS="unavailable"
  return 9
}

# --- New Phase 2: Write Operations ---

is_g01_verified() {
  local marker="/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager/g01-verified"
  local owner mode
  local -a marker_lines=()

  if [[ -L "$marker" || ! -f "$marker" || ! -r "$marker" ]]; then
    return 1
  fi
  if ! owner="$(stat -c '%u' -- "$marker")" ||
    ! mode="$(stat -c '%a' -- "$marker")"; then
    return 1
  fi
  [[ "$owner" == "0" && "$mode" == "600" ]] || return 1
  mapfile -t marker_lines < "$marker" || return 1
  (( ${#marker_lines[@]} == 1 )) &&
    [[ "${marker_lines[0]}" == "G01_STATUS=PASS" ]]
}

usbguard_check_precedence() {
  local new_rule="$1"
  local canonical_new
  canonical_new="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$new_rule")"
  local new_id_pattern
  new_id_pattern="$(usbguard_extract_id_pattern "$canonical_new")"

  if ! list_active_rules; then
    return 1
  fi

  local line existing_id existing_rule existing_id_pattern
  for line in "${USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES[@]}"; do
    usbguard_parse_rule_line "$line" existing_id existing_rule || continue
    # Fail closed unless the earlier block/reject is provably disjoint.
    if [[ "$existing_rule" =~ ^(block|reject)[[:space:]] &&
      "$canonical_new" =~ ^allow[[:space:]] ]]; then
      existing_id_pattern="$(usbguard_extract_id_pattern "$existing_rule")"
      if [[ -z "$existing_id_pattern" || -z "$new_id_pattern" ||
        "$new_id_pattern" == "$existing_id_pattern" ]]; then
        usbguard_policy_error "precedence conflict: existing block rule $existing_id ($existing_id_pattern) precedes new allow $new_id_pattern"
        USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="precedence_conflict"
        return 1
      fi
    fi
  done
  return 0
}

usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical() {
  local canonical_target="$1"
  canonical_target="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$canonical_target")"
  if [[ -z "$canonical_target" ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "canonical rule target is empty"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! list_active_rules; then
    return 1
  fi

  local line rid rtext
  local -a matches=()
  for line in "${USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES[@]}"; do
    usbguard_parse_rule_line "$line" rid rtext || continue
    if [[ "$rtext" == "$canonical_target" ]]; then
      matches+=("$rid")
    fi
  done

  if (( ${#matches[@]} == 0 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "no active rule matches canonical rule: $canonical_target"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="rule_not_found"
    return 1
  fi
  if (( ${#matches[@]} > 1 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "ambiguous match: ${#matches[@]} rules match canonical rule"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="ambiguous_rule_match"
    return 1
  fi

  USBAM_MATCHED_RULE_ID="${matches[0]}"
  printf '%s\n' "$USBAM_MATCHED_RULE_ID"
  return 0
}

approve_device() {
  local device_id="${1:-}"
  if (( $# != 1 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "approve_device requires exactly one device ID"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  usbguard_validate_device_id "$device_id" || return 1
  usbguard_policy_require_lock || return 1

  if ! check_daemon_status; then
    if [[ "$USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS" != "running" || "$USBAM_IPC_STATUS" != "connected" ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "daemon not running or IPC unavailable for approve_device"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="daemon_unavailable"
      return 1
    fi
  fi

  USBAM_LAST_DEVICE_ID="$device_id"
  if ! usbguard_policy_run_write allow-device "$device_id"; then
    return 1
  fi

  # Verify that the requested device still exists and is actually allowed.
  if ! usbguard_policy_run_readonly list-devices; then
    usbguard_policy_error "failed to verify device state after approve_device"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="verification_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  local line found=0 state
  while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
    if [[ "$line" =~ ^[[:space:]]*([0-9]+):[[:space:]]+(allow|block|reject)[[:space:]] ]]; then
      if [[ "${BASH_REMATCH[1]}" == "$device_id" ]]; then
        if (( found != 0 )); then
          usbguard_policy_error "device $device_id appears more than once in USBGuard state"
          USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="ambiguous_device"
          return 1
        fi
        found=1
        state="${BASH_REMATCH[2]}"
      fi
    fi
  done <<< "$USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT"
  if (( found == 0 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "device $device_id disappeared before approval could be verified"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="device_not_found"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$state" != "allow" ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "device $device_id is not allowed after allow-device"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="device_still_blocked"
    return 1
  fi

  return 0
}

add_temporary_rule() {
  local rule="${1:-}"
  if (( $# != 1 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "add_temporary_rule requires exactly one rule"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  usbguard_validate_rule "$rule" || return 1
  rule="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$rule")"
  usbguard_policy_require_lock || return 1

  if ! check_daemon_status; then
    if [[ "$USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS" != "running" || "$USBAM_IPC_STATUS" != "connected" ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "daemon not running or IPC unavailable for add_temporary_rule"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="daemon_unavailable"
      return 1
    fi
  fi

  if ! usbguard_check_precedence "$rule"; then
    return 1
  fi

  # Check duplicate - exact canonical match
  if ! list_active_rules; then
    return 1
  fi
  local line rid rtext
  for line in "${USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES[@]}"; do
    usbguard_parse_rule_line "$line" rid rtext || continue
    if [[ "$rtext" == "$rule" ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "temporary rule already exists as active rule $rid"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="duplicate_rule"
      return 1
    fi
  done

  if ! usbguard_policy_run_write append-rule --temporary "$rule"; then
    return 1
  fi

  USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT")"
  # Some USBGuard versions output new rule ID in stdout, some output rule itself
  # Try to parse ID if stdout is numeric
  if [[ "$USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    :
  else
    # If output is not numeric, try to find the rule by canonical match to get its ID
    if usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical "$rule"; then
      USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID="$USBAM_MATCHED_RULE_ID"
    else
      USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID=""
    fi
  fi

  # Verify rule exists
  if ! usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical "$rule" >/dev/null; then
    usbguard_policy_error "temporary rule not found after append-rule"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="verification_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  return 0
}

add_permanent_rule() {
  local rule="${1:-}"
  if (( $# != 1 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "add_permanent_rule requires exactly one rule"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  usbguard_validate_rule "$rule" || return 1
  rule="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$rule")"
  usbguard_policy_require_lock || return 1

  if ! check_daemon_status; then
    if [[ "$USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS" != "running" || "$USBAM_IPC_STATUS" != "connected" ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "daemon not running or IPC unavailable for add_permanent_rule"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="daemon_unavailable"
      return 1
    fi
  fi

  if ! get_persistent_policy_paths; then
    usbguard_policy_error "persistent policy source unavailable for permanent rule"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! usbguard_check_precedence "$rule"; then
    return 1
  fi

  if ! list_active_rules; then
    return 1
  fi
  local line rid rtext
  for line in "${USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES[@]}"; do
    usbguard_parse_rule_line "$line" rid rtext || continue
    if [[ "$rtext" == "$rule" ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "permanent rule already exists as active rule $rid"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="duplicate_rule"
      return 1
    fi
  done

  if ! usbguard_policy_run_write append-rule "$rule"; then
    return 1
  fi

  USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT")"
  if [[ ! "$USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    if usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical "$rule"; then
      USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID="$USBAM_MATCHED_RULE_ID"
    else
      USBAM_LAST_RULE_ID=""
    fi
  fi

  if ! usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical "$rule" >/dev/null; then
    usbguard_policy_error "permanent rule not found after append-rule"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="verification_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! usbguard_rule_is_persistent "$rule"; then
    usbguard_policy_error "permanent rule was not found in the configured persistent policy"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="persistence_verification_failed"
    return 1
  fi

  return 0
}

remove_rule_by_canonical_rule() {
  local canonical_rule="${1:-}"
  if (( $# != 1 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "remove_rule_by_canonical_rule requires exactly one canonical rule"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  usbguard_validate_rule "$canonical_rule" || return 1
  canonical_rule="$(usbguard_canonicalize_rule "$canonical_rule")"
  usbguard_policy_require_lock || return 1

  if ! check_daemon_status; then
    if [[ "$USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS" != "running" || "$USBAM_IPC_STATUS" != "connected" ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "daemon not running or IPC unavailable for remove_rule"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="daemon_unavailable"
      return 1
    fi
  fi

  local rule_id
  if ! rule_id="$(usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical "$canonical_rule")"; then
    return 1
  fi

  # Re-fetch immediately before removal to avoid TOCTOU (SEC-03)
  local fresh_id
  if ! fresh_id="$(usbguard_find_rule_id_by_canonical "$canonical_rule")"; then
    usbguard_policy_error "rule disappeared before removal (TOCTOU)"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="rule_disappeared"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$fresh_id" != "$rule_id" ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "rule ID changed between lookups (TOCTOU): $rule_id -> $fresh_id"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="rule_id_changed"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! usbguard_policy_run_write remove-rule "$rule_id"; then
    return 1
  fi

  # Verify removal
  if ! list_active_rules; then
    usbguard_policy_error "failed to verify rule removal"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="verification_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  local line rid rtext
  for line in "${USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES[@]}"; do
    usbguard_parse_rule_line "$line" rid rtext || continue
    if [[ "$rid" == "$rule_id" ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "rule $rule_id still present after remove-rule"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="rule_still_present"
      return 1
    fi
  done

  return 0
}

block_device() {
  local device_id="${1:-}"
  if (( $# != 1 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "block_device requires exactly one device ID"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi
  usbguard_validate_device_id "$device_id" || return 1
  usbguard_policy_require_lock || return 1

  if ! check_daemon_status; then
    if [[ "$USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS" != "running" || "$USBAM_IPC_STATUS" != "connected" ]]; then
      usbguard_policy_error "daemon not running or IPC unavailable for block_device"
      USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="daemon_unavailable"
      return 1
    fi
  fi

  USBAM_LAST_DEVICE_ID="$device_id"
  if ! usbguard_policy_run_write block-device "$device_id"; then
    return 1
  fi

  if ! usbguard_policy_run_readonly list-devices; then
    usbguard_policy_error "failed to verify device state after block-device"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="verification_failed"
    return 1
  fi
  local line found=0 state
  while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
    if [[ "$line" =~ ^[[:space:]]*([0-9]+):[[:space:]]+(allow|block|reject)[[:space:]] ]]; then
      if [[ "${BASH_REMATCH[1]}" == "$device_id" ]]; then
        if (( found != 0 )); then
          usbguard_policy_error "device $device_id appears more than once in USBGuard state"
          USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="ambiguous_device"
          return 1
        fi
        found=1
        state="${BASH_REMATCH[2]}"
      fi
    fi
  done <<< "$USBAM_POLICY_STDOUT"
  if (( found == 0 )); then
    usbguard_policy_error "device $device_id disappeared before blocking could be verified"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="device_not_found"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$state" != "block" ]]; then
    usbguard_policy_error "device $device_id is not blocked after block-device"
    USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="device_not_blocked"
    return 1
  fi

  return 0
}

# Additional helpers for higher layers
get_rule_by_id() {
  local rule_id="${1:-}"
  if ! usbguard_validate_device_id "$rule_id"; then
    return 1
  fi
  if ! list_active_rules; then
    return 1
  fi
  local line rid rtext
  for line in "${USBAM_ACTIVE_RULES[@]}"; do
    usbguard_parse_rule_line "$line" rid rtext || continue
    if [[ "$rid" == "$rule_id" ]]; then
      printf '%s\n' "$rtext"
      return 0
    fi
  done
  usbguard_policy_error "rule ID $rule_id not found"
  USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE="rule_not_found"
  return 1
}


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\lib\validators.sh
Source: C:\Users\5722y\Downloads\USB\usbguard-approval-manager\scripts\lib\validators.sh
Size: 10779 bytes
Modified: 2026-11-04T13:27:53.2261086+02:00
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# validators.sh - TTL JSON schema validation (SRS v4.5 Section 13, UT-06)
# Validates ttl.json and clock_state.json handling

if [[ -z "${USBAM_VALIDATORS_LOADED+x}" ]]; then
  readonly USBAM_VALIDATORS_VERSION="1.0.0"
  readonly USBAM_VALIDATORS_JSON_HELPER="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)/atomic-write.py"
  declare -g VALIDATORS_ERROR=""
  declare -g VALIDATORS_ERROR_CODE=""
  declare -gr USBAM_VALIDATORS_LOADED=1
fi

validators_error() {
  VALIDATORS_ERROR="$1"
  VALIDATORS_ERROR_CODE="${2:-validation_failed}"
  printf 'validators: ERROR: %s\n' "$1" >&2
}

validators_is_uuid_v4() {
  local uuid="$1"
  # UUID v4 pattern: xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx where y is 8,9,a,b
  [[ "$uuid" =~ ^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-4[0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$ ]]
}

validators_is_sha256() {
  local hash="$1"
  [[ "$hash" =~ ^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$ ]]
}

# Compute SHA256 of rule - must match usbguard-policy implementation
validators_hash_rule() {
  local rule="$1"
  local canonical
  # Trim like usbguard_canonicalize_rule
  canonical="${rule#"${rule%%[![:space:]]*}"}"
  canonical="${canonical%"${canonical##*[![:space:]]}"}"
  if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    printf '%s' "$canonical" | sha256sum | awk '{print $1}'
  else
    python3 -c "import hashlib,sys; print(hashlib.sha256(sys.argv[1].encode('utf-8')).hexdigest())" "$canonical"
  fi
}

validate_ttl_json() {
  local file_path="$1"
  VALIDATORS_ERROR=""
  VALIDATORS_ERROR_CODE=""

  if (( $# != 1 )); then
    validators_error "validate_ttl_json requires exactly one file path" "invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ ! -f "$file_path" ]]; then
    # Missing file is treated as empty valid state if no records? But per spec, missing with live records -> recovery_required
    # For validation, missing file is considered valid empty if caller handles it
    # We return 0 with empty, but log
    if [[ ! -e "$file_path" ]]; then
      return 0
    fi
    validators_error "ttl.json is not a regular file" "not_regular_file"
    return 1
  fi

  if [[ -L "$file_path" ]]; then
    validators_error "ttl.json is a symlink - refusing" "symlink_not_allowed"
    return 1
  fi

  local json_error
  if ! json_error="$(python3 "$USBAM_VALIDATORS_JSON_HELPER" \
    --file "$file_path" --validate-only 2>&1)"; then
    validators_error "ttl.json is not valid JSON: $json_error" "invalid_json"
    return 1
  fi

  if ! jq -e '
    if (.schema_version | type) == "number" then
      .schema_version == 1 and .schema_version == (.schema_version | floor)
    else
      false
    end
  ' "$file_path" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    validators_error "schema_version must be the integer 1" "invalid_schema_version"
    return 1
  fi

  # Check records is array
  if ! jq -e '.records | type == "array"' "$file_path" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    validators_error "records must be an array" "invalid_records_type"
    return 1
  fi

  # Check for duplicate keys (jq doesn't catch by default, but we can use python helper if needed)
  # Use atomic-write.py validation which already rejects duplicate keys via python

  local record_count
  record_count="$(jq '.records | length' "$file_path")"
  if [[ ! "$record_count" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    validators_error "unable to determine records count" "invalid_records"
    return 1
  fi

  # Validate each record
  local i
  for ((i=0; i<record_count; i++)); do
    if ! jq -e --argjson i "$i" '
      .records[$i] |
      if type != "object" then
        false
      else
        (.managed_id | type) == "string" and
        (.rule | type) == "string" and
        (.rule | explode | all(. >= 32 and . != 127)) and
        (.rule_sha256 | type) == "string" and
        (.status | type) == "string" and
        (.correlation_id | type) == "string" and
        (if (.expires_at | type) == "number" then
          .expires_at == (.expires_at | floor) and
          .expires_at > 0 and
          .expires_at <= 253402300799
        else
          false
        end)
      end
    ' "$file_path" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      validators_error "record $i has an invalid JSON type or expiration range" "invalid_field_type"
      return 1
    fi

    # Check required fields - exactly 6 fields per spec
    local field_count
    field_count="$(jq -r ".records[$i] | keys | length" "$file_path")"
    if (( field_count != 6 )); then
      validators_error "record $i must have exactly 6 fields, got $field_count" "invalid_field_count"
      return 1
    fi

    # Check no extra fields beyond allowed set
    local has_extra
    has_extra="$(jq -r ".records[$i] | keys | map(select(. != \"managed_id\" and . != \"rule\" and . != \"rule_sha256\" and . != \"expires_at\" and . != \"status\" and . != \"correlation_id\")) | length" "$file_path")"
    if [[ "$has_extra" != "0" ]]; then
      validators_error "record $i contains extra fields not in schema" "extra_fields"
      return 1
    fi

    # managed_id must be UUID v4
    local managed_id
    managed_id="$(jq -r ".records[$i].managed_id // empty" "$file_path")"
    if ! validators_is_uuid_v4 "$managed_id"; then
      validators_error "record $i managed_id is not valid UUID v4: $managed_id" "invalid_managed_id"
      return 1
    fi

    # correlation_id UUID v4
    local correlation_id
    correlation_id="$(jq -r ".records[$i].correlation_id // empty" "$file_path")"
    if ! validators_is_uuid_v4 "$correlation_id"; then
      validators_error "record $i correlation_id is not valid UUID v4: $correlation_id" "invalid_correlation_id"
      return 1
    fi

    # rule must be non-empty string, no control chars, must start with allow/block/reject
    local rule
    rule="$(jq -r ".records[$i].rule // empty" "$file_path")"
    if [[ -z "$rule" ]]; then
      validators_error "record $i rule is empty" "invalid_rule"
      return 1
    fi
    local rule_cleaned
    rule_cleaned="$(printf '%s' "$rule" | sed 's/[[:cntrl:]]//g')"
    
    # Check if rule originally contained control characters
    if [[ "$rule" != "$rule_cleaned" ]]; then
      validators_error "record $i rule contains control characters - cleaned for consistency" "invalid_rule_ctrl_chars"
      # Continue with cleaned version but log the issue
    fi
    if [[ ! "$rule" =~ ^(allow|block|reject)[[:space:]] ]]; then
      validators_error "record $i rule must start with allow, block or reject" "invalid_rule"
      return 1
    fi
    if [[ "$rule" == *"00-system.rules"* ]]; then
      validators_error "record $i rule references protected 00-system.rules" "protected_policy"
      return 1
    fi

    # rule_sha256 must be 64 hex and match hash of rule
    local rule_sha256 expected_hash
    rule_sha256="$(jq -r ".records[$i].rule_sha256 // empty" "$file_path")"
    if ! validators_is_sha256 "$rule_sha256"; then
      validators_error "record $i rule_sha256 is not valid SHA256: $rule_sha256" "invalid_hash"
      return 1
    fi
    expected_hash="$(validators_hash_rule "$rule")"
    # Compare case-insensitive
    if [[ "${rule_sha256,,}" != "${expected_hash,,}" ]]; then
      validators_error "record $i rule_sha256 mismatch: expected $expected_hash, got $rule_sha256" "hash_mismatch"
      return 1
    fi

    # expires_at must be a positive, representable integer
    local expires_at
    expires_at="$(jq -r ".records[$i].expires_at // empty" "$file_path")"
    if [[ ! "$expires_at" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] ||
      (( expires_at <= 0 || expires_at > 253402300799 )); then
      validators_error "record $i expires_at must be positive integer, got $expires_at" "invalid_expires_at"
      return 1
    fi

    # status must be one of allowed
    local status
    status="$(jq -r ".records[$i].status // empty" "$file_path")"
    case "$status" in
      pending|active|inconsistent|recovery_required|expired)
        ;;
      *)
        validators_error "record $i status invalid: $status" "invalid_status"
        return 1
        ;;
    esac
  done

  # Check duplicate managed_id
  local duplicate_ids
  duplicate_ids="$(jq -r '.records | map(.managed_id) | group_by(.) | map(select(length>1)) | length' "$file_path")"
  if [[ "$duplicate_ids" != "0" ]]; then
    validators_error "duplicate managed_id found" "duplicate_managed_id"
    return 1
  fi

  # Check duplicate correlation_id? Not required to be unique per spec, but check if needed
  # For now allow duplicates but log? Spec says each record has correlation_id uuid, should be unique per operation
  # We won't enforce uniqueness for correlation_id as it may be reused in retries? But per spec new IDs on import, so we enforce uniqueness as safety
  local duplicate_corr
  duplicate_corr="$(jq -r '.records | map(.correlation_id) | group_by(.) | map(select(length>1)) | length' "$file_path")"
  if [[ "$duplicate_corr" != "0" ]]; then
    # Not fatal per spec, but warn - for strictness we fail
    validators_error "duplicate correlation_id found" "duplicate_correlation_id"
    return 1
  fi

  return 0
}

validate_ttl_record_transition() {
  local from_status="$1"
  local to_status="$2"

  if (( $# != 2 )); then
    validators_error "validate_ttl_record_transition requires from and to status" "invalid_arguments"
    return 1
  fi

  case "$from_status:$to_status" in
    pending:active|pending:inconsistent|pending:recovery_required)
      return 0
      ;;
    active:expired|active:inconsistent|active:recovery_required)
      return 0
      ;;
    inconsistent:recovery_required|inconsistent:active|inconsistent:expired)
      # inconsistent -> active/expired only after verified recovery
      return 0
      ;;
    recovery_required:active|recovery_required:expired|recovery_required:inconsistent)
      # Only after explicit documented admin action
      return 0
      ;;
    expired:expired)
      # expired is final, but self-transition allowed for idempotency check
      return 0
      ;;
    *)
      validators_error "transition $from_status -> $to_status not allowed" "invalid_transition"
      return 1
      ;;
  esac
}

has_non_expired_records() {
  local file_path="$1"
  if [[ ! -f "$file_path" ]]; then
    printf 'no\n'
    return 0
  fi
  if ! validate_ttl_json "$file_path" 2>/dev/null; then
    printf 'yes\n'  # Corrupt file with potential records -> treat as having records for safety
    return 0
  fi
  local count
  count="$(jq '[.records[] | select(.status != "expired")] | length' "$file_path" 2>/dev/null || echo 1)"
  if (( count > 0 )); then
    printf 'yes\n'
  else
    printf 'no\n'
  fi
}

# Helper to generate UUID v4
generate_uuid_v4() {
  if [[ -r /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid ]]; then
    cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid
  else
    python3 -c "import uuid; print(uuid.uuid4())"
  fi
}

# Helper to get current epoch
validators_get_epoch() {
  date +%s
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Validate and restore one Phase 5 archive, or idempotently recover a journal.

set -uo pipefail
umask 077

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
LIB_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/lib"
PYTHON3="${PYTHON3:-python3}"
CONFIG_PATH="${USBAM_CONFIG_PATH:-/etc/usbguard-approval-manager/approval-manager.conf}"
STATE_DIR="${USBAM_STATE_DIR:-/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager}"
TTL_FILE="${USBAM_TTL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/ttl.json}"
RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE="${USBAM_RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/restore_journal.json}"
LOCK_FILE="${USBAM_LOCK_FILE:-/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager.lock}"
USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH="${USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH:-/etc/usbguard/usbguard-daemon.conf}"
BACKUP_FILE=""
RECOVER=0

usage() {
  printf 'Usage: %s --backup ARCHIVE.tar.gz [--help]\n       %s --recover [--help]\n' "$0" "$0"
}

while (( $# )); do
  case "$1" in
    --backup)
      if (( $# < 2 )) || [[ -z "$2" || -n "$BACKUP_FILE" ]]; then
        usage >&2
        exit 7
      fi
      BACKUP_FILE="$2"
      shift 2
      ;;
    --recover)
      if (( RECOVER != 0 )); then
        usage >&2
        exit 7
      fi
      RECOVER=1
      shift
      ;;
    --help)
      if (( $# != 1 )); then
        usage >&2
        exit 7
      fi
      usage
      exit 0
      ;;
    *)
      usage >&2
      exit 7
      ;;
  esac
done
if { (( RECOVER == 1 )) && [[ -n "$BACKUP_FILE" ]]; } ||
  { (( RECOVER == 0 )) && [[ -z "$BACKUP_FILE" ]]; }; then
  usage >&2
  exit 7
fi
if (( EUID != 0 )); then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: root privileges are required\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi

source "$LIB_DIR/stages.sh"
source "$LIB_DIR/lifecycle.sh"
source "$LIB_DIR/logger.sh"
STATE_DIR="${USBAM_STATE_DIR:-/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager}"
TTL_FILE="${USBAM_TTL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/ttl.json}"
RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE="${USBAM_RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/restore_journal.json}"
ROLLBACK_ROOT="$STATE_DIR/restore-rollback"

case "$CONFIG_PATH:$TTL_FILE:$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" in
  *00-system.rules*)
    printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: restore paths must not target protected system policy\n' >&2
    exit 7
    ;;
esac
if [[ -L "$CONFIG_PATH" || ( -e "$CONFIG_PATH" && ! -f "$CONFIG_PATH" ) ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: application configuration path is unsafe\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi
if [[ -f "$CONFIG_PATH" ]] && ! load_config "$CONFIG_PATH"; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: current application configuration is invalid\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi
for protected_state_file in "$CONFIG_PATH" "$TTL_FILE"; do
  if [[ -e "$protected_state_file" || -L "$protected_state_file" ]]; then
    if [[ -L "$protected_state_file" || ! -f "$protected_state_file" ]] ||
      [[ "$(stat -c '%u:%a' -- "$protected_state_file")" != "0:600" ]]; then
      printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: config and TTL files must be root-owned mode 0600\n' >&2
      exit 9
    fi
  fi
done

if ! lock_acquire 5; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: unable to acquire the state lock\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi
if [[ -L "$STATE_DIR" ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: state directory must not be a symbolic link\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi
if [[ ! -d "$STATE_DIR" ]]; then
  mkdir -m 700 -p -- "$STATE_DIR" || {
    printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: unable to create state directory\n' >&2
    exit 9
  }
fi
chmod 700 -- "$STATE_DIR" || exit 9

if ! check_daemon_status && [[ "$USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS" != "stopped" ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: unable to prove that USBGuard is stopped\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi
if [[ "$USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS" != "stopped" ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: daemon must be stopped before restore or recovery\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi
if ! get_persistent_policy_paths; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: persistent policy paths are unavailable\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi
RULE_FILE="${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]:-}"
RULE_FOLDER="${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]:-}"
if [[ -z "$RULE_FILE" ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: safe restore requires a configured RuleFile\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi

work_parent="$(mktemp -d "$STATE_DIR/.restore-work.XXXXXX")" || {
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: unable to create private staging directory\n' >&2
  exit 1
}
chmod 700 "$work_parent" || {
  rmdir "$work_parent" 2>/dev/null || true
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: unable to secure staging directory\n' >&2
  exit 1
}
cleanup() {
  rm -rf -- "$work_parent"
  if (( LOCK_HELD )); then
    lock_release
  fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT

targets_file="$work_parent/targets.json"
if ! jq -cn --arg config "$CONFIG_PATH" --arg ttl "$TTL_FILE" --arg policy "$RULE_FILE" \
  '{config:$config,ttl:$ttl,policy:$policy}' > "$targets_file"; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: unable to build the restore allowlist\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi
chmod 600 "$targets_file" || exit 1

if (( RECOVER )); then
  if ! "$PYTHON3" "$USBAM_LIFECYCLE_HELPER" restore-recover \
    --journal "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" \
    --rollback-root "$ROLLBACK_ROOT" \
    --targets "$targets_file"; then
    exit 9
  fi
  printf 'restore-rules: previous state recovered and verified\n'
  exit 0
fi

if [[ -e "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" || -L "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" ]]; then
  lifecycle_error "restore journal already exists; use --recover"
  exit 9
fi
if [[ -L "$BACKUP_FILE" || ! -f "$BACKUP_FILE" || ! -r "$BACKUP_FILE" ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: backup archive is missing, unsafe, or unreadable\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi
if ! is_g01_verified; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: G-01 verification is required before policy restore\n' >&2
  exit 7
fi
if ! "$PYTHON3" "$USBAM_LIFECYCLE_HELPER" backup-extract \
  --backup "$BACKUP_FILE" --staging "$work_parent/extracted"; then
  exit 1
fi

backup_config="$work_parent/extracted/config/approval-manager.conf"
backup_ttl="$work_parent/extracted/state/ttl.json"
backup_export="$work_parent/extracted/policy/export.json"
staged_rule_file="$work_parent/extracted/policy/rules.conf"
if ! load_config "$backup_config"; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: archived application configuration is invalid\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi
if ! validate_ttl_json "$backup_ttl"; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: archived ttl.json is invalid\n' >&2
  exit 1
fi
if [[ -e "$TTL_FILE" || -L "$TTL_FILE" ]]; then
  if [[ -L "$TTL_FILE" || ! -f "$TTL_FILE" || ! -r "$TTL_FILE" ]] ||
    ! validate_ttl_json "$TTL_FILE"; then
    printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: current ttl.json is invalid or unsafe\n' >&2
    exit 9
  fi
fi

if [[ -e "$RULE_FILE" || -L "$RULE_FILE" ]]; then
  if [[ -L "$RULE_FILE" || ! -f "$RULE_FILE" || ! -r "$RULE_FILE" ]]; then
    printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: current RuleFile is unsafe or unreadable\n' >&2
    exit 9
  fi
  if [[ "$(stat -c '%u' -- "$RULE_FILE")" != "0" ]] ||
    (( (8#$(stat -c '%a' -- "$RULE_FILE") & 0022) != 0 )); then
    printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: current RuleFile ownership or permissions are unsafe\n' >&2
    exit 9
  fi
  while IFS= read -r line || [[ -n "$line" ]]; do
    line="${line#"${line%%[![:space:]]*}"}"
    line="${line%"${line##*[![:space:]]}"}"
    [[ -z "$line" || "$line" == \#* ]] && continue
    if [[ "$line" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]] || ! usbguard_validate_rule "$line" ||
      [[ "$line" != allow\ * ]]; then
      printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: current RuleFile has policy outside the approved allowlist\n' >&2
      exit 9
    fi
  done < "$RULE_FILE"
fi

if [[ -n "$RULE_FOLDER" ]]; then
  if [[ "$(stat -c '%u' -- "$RULE_FOLDER")" != "0" ]] ||
    (( (8#$(stat -c '%a' -- "$RULE_FOLDER") & 0022) != 0 )); then
    printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: RuleFolder ownership or permissions are unsafe\n' >&2
    exit 9
  fi
  folder_entries="$work_parent/rule-folder.entries"
  if ! find "$RULE_FOLDER" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -print0 > "$folder_entries"; then
    printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: unable to inspect RuleFolder\n' >&2
    exit 9
  fi
  while IFS= read -r -d '' entry; do
    if [[ -L "$entry" ]]; then
      printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: RuleFolder contains a symbolic link\n' >&2
      exit 9
    fi
    if [[ "${entry##*/}" != "00-system.rules" ]]; then
      printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: safe restore cannot replace a non-system RuleFolder entry\n' >&2
      exit 9
    fi
    [[ -f "$entry" ]] || {
      printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: protected system rule entry is not a regular file\n' >&2
      exit 9
    }
  done < "$folder_entries"
fi

restore_snapshot() {
  local target snapshot
  local -a targets=("$CONFIG_PATH" "$TTL_FILE" "$RULE_FILE" "$USBAM_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH")
  for target in "${targets[@]}"; do
    if [[ -L "$target" ]]; then
      return 1
    elif [[ -e "$target" ]]; then
      [[ -f "$target" ]] || return 1
      snapshot="$(sha256sum -- "$target" | awk '{print $1}')" || return 1
      printf '%s:%s\n' "$target" "$snapshot"
    else
      printf '%s:missing\n' "$target"
    fi
  done
  if [[ -n "$RULE_FOLDER" ]]; then
    snapshot="$(find "$RULE_FOLDER" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 \
      -printf '%f:%y:%s:%T@\n' | LC_ALL=C sort | sha256sum | awk '{print $1}')" || return 1
    printf 'RuleFolder:%s\n' "$snapshot"
  fi
}
before_snapshot="$(restore_snapshot)" || {
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: unable to establish a safe pre-restore snapshot\n' >&2
  exit 9
}

: > "$staged_rule_file"
while IFS= read -r rule; do
  [[ -n "$rule" ]] || continue
  if ! usbguard_validate_rule "$rule" || [[ "$rule" != allow\ * ]]; then
    printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: archive contains an invalid permanent rule\n' >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "$rule" >> "$staged_rule_file" || exit 1
done < <(jq -r '.permanent_rules[]' "$backup_export")
chmod 600 "$staged_rule_file" || exit 1

printf 'Restore diff:\n'
printf '  config: replace with validated archived configuration\n'
printf '  RuleFile: replace approved permanent allow rules (%s rules)\n' \
  "$(wc -l < "$staged_rule_file" | tr -d '[:space:]')"
active_ttl_count="$(jq '[.records[] | select(.status == "active")] | length' "$backup_ttl")"
printf '  TTL: %s archived active record(s) become recovery_required; no temporary rule is applied\n' \
  "$active_ttl_count"
if [[ ! -t 0 ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: restore requires interactive confirmation\n' >&2
  exit 7
fi
printf 'Apply this restore? [y/N] '
IFS= read -r answer || exit 7
if [[ "$answer" != "y" && "$answer" != "Y" ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: cancelled; no changes made\n'
  exit 0
fi
if ! check_daemon_status && [[ "$USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS" != "stopped" ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: unable to re-verify stopped daemon after confirmation\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi
if [[ "$USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS" != "stopped" ]] || ! is_g01_verified; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: daemon state or G-01 verification changed after confirmation\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi
if ! get_persistent_policy_paths ||
  [[ "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]:-}" != "$RULE_FILE" ||
    "${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]:-}" != "$RULE_FOLDER" ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: configured policy paths changed after confirmation\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi
after_snapshot="$(restore_snapshot)" || {
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: unable to recheck restore targets\n' >&2
  exit 9
}
if [[ "$before_snapshot" != "$after_snapshot" ]]; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: restore targets changed while awaiting confirmation\n' >&2
  exit 9
fi

if ! "$PYTHON3" "$USBAM_LIFECYCLE_HELPER" restore-apply \
  --journal "$RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE" \
  --rollback-root "$ROLLBACK_ROOT" \
  --targets "$targets_file" \
  --staging "$work_parent/extracted"; then
  exit 9
fi
correlation_id="$( "$PYTHON3" -c 'import uuid; print(uuid.uuid4())' )" || exit 9
if ! log_audit "restore_rules" "applied" "$correlation_id" "validated archive restored"; then
  printf 'restore-rules: ERROR: restore applied but Audit failed\n' >&2
  exit 14
fi
printf 'restore-rules: restore completed; restored TTL records require explicit recovery\n'


================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# scripts/restore.sh
# Phase 5 - Restore with journal, validators, atomic restores, clock trust, G-01 gate
# SRS v4.5: Restores from backup, creates restore_journal.json before any change
# to block operations until recovery, validates backup, restores RuleFile and
# ttl.json atomically, deletes journal at end, with clock trust check.

set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
LIB_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/lib"
if [[ ! -d "$LIB_DIR" ]]; then
  LIB_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/../scripts/lib"
fi
if [[ ! -f "$LIB_DIR/config-reader.sh" ]]; then
  for cand in "/opt/usbguard-approval-manager/scripts/lib" "./scripts/lib" "$SCRIPT_DIR/lib"; do
    if [[ -f "$cand/config-reader.sh" ]]; then
      LIB_DIR="$cand"
      break
    fi
  done
fi

# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/config-reader.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/lock.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/logger.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/result.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/validators.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/usbguard-policy.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/time-guards.sh"
# shellcheck source=/dev/null
source "$LIB_DIR/journal.sh"

CONFIG_PATH="${USBAM_CONFIG_PATH:-/etc/usbguard-approval-manager/approval-manager.conf}"
STATE_DIR="${USBAM_STATE_DIR:-/var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager}"
TTL_FILE="$STATE_DIR/ttl.json"
CLOCK_STATE_FILE="$STATE_DIR/clock_state.json"
RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE="${USBAM_RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE:-$STATE_DIR/restore_journal.json}"
BACKUP_BASE_DIR="${USBAM_BACKUP_BASE_DIR:-/var/backups/usbguard-approval-manager}"
STATE_LOCK_FILE="${USBAM_STATE_LOCK_FILE:-/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager-state.lock}"
POLICY_LOCK_FILE="${USBAM_POLICY_LOCK_FILE:-/run/lock/usbguard-approval-manager-policy.lock}"
ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER="$LIB_DIR/atomic-write.py"
AUDIT_WRITER="$LIB_DIR/audit_write.py"

RESTORE_ERROR=""
RESTORE_BACKUP_PATH=""
RESTORE_DRY_RUN=0
RESTORE_FORCE=0

restore_error() {
  RESTORE_ERROR="$1"
  printf 'restore: ERROR: %s\n' "$1" >&2
}

restore_fsync_dir() {
  local dir="$1"
  python3 -c 'import os,sys; fd=os.open(sys.argv[1], os.O_DIRECTORY); os.fsync(fd); os.close(fd)' "$dir" 2>/dev/null || true
}

restore_atomic_copy() {
  local src="$1"
  local dst="$2"
  local dst_dir tmp_dst

  if [[ -L "$src" ]]; then
    restore_error "source is symlink: $src"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -f "$src" ]]; then
    restore_error "source not regular file: $src"
    return 1
  fi
  dst_dir="$(dirname -- "$dst")"
  if [[ -L "$dst_dir" ]]; then
    restore_error "dest dir is symlink: $dst_dir"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "$dst_dir" ]]; then
    if ! mkdir -p "$dst_dir"; then
      restore_error "mkdir dest dir failed $dst_dir"
      return 1
    fi
    chmod 700 "$dst_dir" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi
  tmp_dst="$dst_dir/.$(basename -- "$dst").tmp.$$.$RANDOM"
  if ! cp --no-dereference -p -- "$src" "$tmp_dst"; then
    rm -f -- "$tmp_dst"
    restore_error "cp failed $src -> $tmp_dst"
    return 1
  fi
  chmod 600 "$tmp_dst" || true
  # fsync file
  python3 -c 'import os,sys; fd=os.open(sys.argv[1], os.O_RDONLY); os.fsync(fd); os.close(fd)' "$tmp_dst" 2>/dev/null || true
  if ! mv -f -- "$tmp_dst" "$dst"; then
    rm -f -- "$tmp_dst"
    restore_error "mv $tmp_dst -> $dst failed"
    return 1
  fi
  chmod 600 "$dst" 2>/dev/null || true
  restore_fsync_dir "$dst_dir"
  return 0
}

restore_atomic_copy_folder() {
  local src_folder="$1"
  local dst_folder="$2"
  local dst_parent tmp_new old_backup

  if [[ -L "$src_folder" ]]; then
    restore_error "src folder is symlink: $src_folder"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "$src_folder" ]]; then
    restore_error "src folder not directory: $src_folder"
    return 1
  fi

  dst_parent="$(dirname -- "$dst_folder")"
  if [[ ! -d "$dst_parent" ]]; then
    if ! mkdir -p "$dst_parent"; then
      restore_error "mkdir parent $dst_parent failed"
      return 1
    fi
    chmod 700 "$dst_parent" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi

  tmp_new="$dst_parent/.$(basename -- "$dst_folder").new.$$.$RANDOM"
  old_backup="$dst_folder.old.$(date +%s).$$"

  if ! mkdir -p "$tmp_new"; then
    restore_error "mkdir tmp new $tmp_new failed"
    return 1
  fi
  chmod 700 "$tmp_new" || true

  # copy files securely, skip symlinks
  local entry
  for entry in "$src_folder"/*; do
    [[ -e "$entry" ]] || continue
    if [[ -L "$entry" ]]; then
      restore_error "skipping symlink in src backup folder: $entry"
      continue
    fi
    if [[ -f "$entry" ]]; then
      local base
      base="$(basename -- "$entry")"
      if ! cp --no-dereference -p -- "$entry" "$tmp_new/$base"; then
        rm -rf -- "$tmp_new"
        restore_error "copy $entry -> $tmp_new/$base failed"
        return 1
      fi
      chmod 600 "$tmp_new/$base" 2>/dev/null || true
      python3 -c 'import os,sys; fd=os.open(sys.argv[1], os.O_RDONLY); os.fsync(fd); os.close(fd)' "$tmp_new/$base" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
  done
  restore_fsync_dir "$tmp_new"

  # backup old folder if exists
  if [[ -d "$dst_folder" ]]; then
    if ! mv -- "$dst_folder" "$old_backup"; then
      rm -rf -- "$tmp_new"
      restore_error "mv old folder $dst_folder -> $old_backup failed"
      return 1
    fi
    restore_fsync_dir "$dst_parent"
  fi

  if ! mv -- "$tmp_new" "$dst_folder"; then
    # try to restore old backup
    if [[ -d "$old_backup" ]]; then
      mv -- "$old_backup" "$dst_folder" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
    rm -rf -- "$tmp_new"
    restore_error "mv new $tmp_new -> $dst_folder failed"
    return 1
  fi
  restore_fsync_dir "$dst_parent"
  # fsync restored folder
  restore_fsync_dir "$dst_folder"
  # remove old backup after successful restore
  rm -rf -- "$old_backup" 2>/dev/null || true
  restore_fsync_dir "$dst_parent"
  return 0
}

restore_release_locks() {
  journal_release_both_locks 2>/dev/null || true
}

restore_release_state_only() {
  journal_release_state_lock 2>/dev/null || true
}

validate_backup_dir() {
  local bdir="$1"
  if [[ -z "$bdir" ]]; then
    restore_error "backup path empty"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ "$bdir" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]; then
    restore_error "backup path contains control chars"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -L "$bdir" ]]; then
    restore_error "backup path is symlink: $bdir"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "$bdir" ]]; then
    restore_error "backup dir not found: $bdir"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -f "$bdir/manifest.json" ]]; then
    restore_error "manifest.json missing in backup dir: $bdir"
    return 1
  fi
  if [[ -L "$bdir/manifest.json" ]]; then
    restore_error "manifest.json is symlink"
    return 1
  fi
  if ! jq -e . "$bdir/manifest.json" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    restore_error "manifest.json not valid JSON"
    return 1
  fi
  local schema
  schema="$(jq -r '.schema_version // empty' "$bdir/manifest.json")"
  if [[ "$schema" != "1" ]]; then
    restore_error "manifest schema_version must be 1, got $schema"
    return 1
  fi
  # check correlation_id uuid
  local cid
  cid="$(jq -r '.correlation_id // empty' "$bdir/manifest.json")"
  if [[ -n "$cid" ]] && ! journal_is_uuid_v4 "$cid"; then
    restore_error "manifest correlation_id not UUID v4"
    return 1
  fi
  # if ttl.json exists in backup, validate it
  if [[ -f "$bdir/ttl.json" ]]; then
    if [[ -L "$bdir/ttl.json" ]]; then
      restore_error "backup ttl.json is symlink"
      return 1
    fi
    if ! validate_ttl_json "$bdir/ttl.json"; then
      restore_error "backup ttl.json invalid: $VALIDATORS_ERROR"
      return 1
    fi
  fi
  # clock_state.json if exists, basic JSON check
  if [[ -f "$bdir/clock_state.json" ]]; then
    if ! jq -e . "$bdir/clock_state.json" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      restore_error "backup clock_state.json invalid JSON"
      return 1
    fi
  fi
  return 0
}

restore_check_clock_trust() {
  local backup_ttl="$1"
  # if backup has no non-expired records, clock trust not needed for restore?
  # But per task we must check clock trust
  if [[ ! -f "$backup_ttl" ]]; then
    return 0
  fi
  local has_records
  has_records="$(has_non_expired_records "$backup_ttl")"
  if [[ "$has_records" != "yes" ]]; then
    # no active records, no need for trusted clock, but still try to detect if possible
    return 0
  fi
  # has non-expired -> need trusted clock
  # use detect_clock_jump with current state file
  local clock_state_path="$CLOCK_STATE_FILE"
  if ! detect_clock_jump "$clock_state_path" "yes"; then
    local trust_status="$CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS"
    local detail_code="$CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE"
    restore_error "clock not trusted for restore (has non-expired backup records): $trust_status $detail_code"
    return 9
  fi
  return 0
}

usage() {
  cat <<EOF
Usage: $0 --backup <path> [--dry-run] [--force] [--help]

Restore USBGuard Approval Manager from backup

Creates $RESTORE_JOURNAL_FILE before any change to block operations
until recovery, validates backup, restores RuleFile and ttl.json atomically.

Options:
  --backup <path>   Backup directory (full path or name under $BACKUP_BASE_DIR)
  --dry-run         Validate backup and show what would be restored, no changes
  --force           Allow restore even if journal exists or clock untrusted (use with caution)
  --help            Show this help

Exit codes:
  0 success
  1 operational failure
  7 validation / G-01 not verified
  9 recovery required / clock untrusted
  14 audit write failed

Environment:
  USBAM_CONFIG_PATH, USBAM_STATE_DIR, USBAM_BACKUP_BASE_DIR,
  USBAM_STATE_LOCK_FILE, USBAM_POLICY_LOCK_FILE

Security:
  - Requires G-01 verification (SEC-19)
  - Split-locking: state lock then policy lock
  - Journal blocks all operations until removed (EXIT 9)
  - Never calls block-device automatically
EOF
}

main() {
  local backup_arg=""
  while (( $# )); do
    case "$1" in
      --backup)
        backup_arg="${2:-}"
        shift 2
        ;;
      --dry-run)
        RESTORE_DRY_RUN=1
        shift
        ;;
      --force)
        RESTORE_FORCE=1
        shift
        ;;
      --help)
        usage
        exit 0
        ;;
      *)
        printf 'ERROR: unknown argument %s\n' "$1" >&2
        usage >&2
        exit 7
        ;;
    esac
  done

  if [[ -z "$backup_arg" ]]; then
    printf 'ERROR: --backup is required\n' >&2
    usage >&2
    exit 7
  fi
  if [[ "$backup_arg" =~ [[:cntrl:]] ]]; then
    printf 'ERROR: backup path contains control characters\n' >&2
    exit 7
  fi

  result_start

  # Load config - data-only, no source/eval
  if [[ -f "$CONFIG_PATH" ]]; then
    if ! load_config "$CONFIG_PATH"; then
      restore_error "config invalid"
      result_validation_failed "config invalid"
      result_print_json
      exit 7
    fi
  fi

  # G-01 mandatory gate
  if ! is_g01_verified; then
    restore_error "G-01 not verified - restore requires G-01 verification (SEC-19). Set USBAM_G01_VERIFIED=1 or create /var/lib/usbguard-approval-manager/g01-verified"
    result_validation_failed "G-01 not verified for restore"
    result_print_json
    exit 7
  fi

  # Resolve backup path: if not absolute, try under BACKUP_BASE_DIR
  local resolved_backup=""
  if [[ "$backup_arg" == /* ]]; then
    resolved_backup="$backup_arg"
  else
    if [[ -d "$BACKUP_BASE_DIR/$backup_arg" ]]; then
      resolved_backup="$BACKUP_BASE_DIR/$backup_arg"
    else
      # try to find latest matching prefix? For simplicity require absolute or name under base
      resolved_backup="$BACKUP_BASE_DIR/$backup_arg"
    fi
  fi
  RESTORE_BACKUP_PATH="$resolved_backup"

  if [[ -L "$resolved_backup" ]]; then
    restore_error "backup path is symlink: $resolved_backup"
    result_validation_failed "backup path is symlink"
    result_print_json
    exit 7
  fi

  if ! validate_backup_dir "$resolved_backup"; then
    result_validation_failed "backup validation failed: $RESTORE_ERROR"
    result_print_json
    exit 7
  fi

  log_info "restore: backup validated at $resolved_backup" 2>/dev/null || printf 'INFO: backup validated %s\n' "$resolved_backup"

  # Clock trust check before journal creation (read-only check)
  local backup_ttl_path="$resolved_backup/ttl.json"
  if [[ -f "$backup_ttl_path" ]]; then
    if ! restore_check_clock_trust "$backup_ttl_path"; then
      local rc=$?
      if (( rc == 9 )) && (( RESTORE_FORCE == 0 )); then
        result_recovery_required "clock not trusted for restore: $CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS $CLOCK_TRUST_DETAIL_CODE"
        result_print_json
        exit 9
      elif (( RESTORE_FORCE )); then
        log_error "restore: clock untrusted but --force overrides: $CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS" 2>/dev/null || true
        printf 'WARN: clock untrusted but --force used: %s\n' "$CLOCK_TRUST_STATUS" >&2
      fi
    fi
  fi

  if (( RESTORE_DRY_RUN )); then
    printf 'DRY-RUN: would restore from %s\n' "$resolved_backup"
    if [[ -f "$resolved_backup/manifest.json" ]]; then
      cat "$resolved_backup/manifest.json"
    fi
    # show what files would be restored
    if [[ -f "$resolved_backup/ttl.json" ]]; then
      local cnt
      cnt="$(jq '.records | length' "$resolved_backup/ttl.json" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)"
      printf 'DRY-RUN: ttl.json with %s records would be restored to %s\n' "$cnt" "$TTL_FILE"
    fi
    local current_rule_file="" current_rule_folder=""
    if get_persistent_policy_paths 2>/dev/null; then
      current_rule_file="${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]:-}"
      current_rule_folder="${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]:-}"
      printf 'DRY-RUN: current RuleFile=%s RuleFolder=%s\n' "${current_rule_file:-none}" "${current_rule_folder:-none}"
    fi
    result_success "dry-run validated backup $resolved_backup"
    result_print_json
    exit 0
  fi

  # Check journal exists before acquiring locks - if exists, block unless --force
  if journal_exists; then
    if (( RESTORE_FORCE == 0 )); then
      restore_error "restore journal already exists at $JOURNAL_FILE - previous restore may have crashed, recovery required"
      result_recovery_required "restore journal exists - recovery required"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    else
      log_error "restore: journal exists but --force will overwrite" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
  fi

  # Acquire state lock first (split locking order)
  trap 'restore_release_locks' EXIT INT TERM

  if ! journal_acquire_state_lock 5; then
    restore_error "unable to acquire state lock: $JOURNAL_ERROR"
    result_operation_failed "state lock acquisition failed"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi

  # Re-check journal under state lock
  if journal_exists && (( RESTORE_FORCE == 0 )); then
    restore_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    restore_error "restore journal exists under lock - recovery required"
    result_recovery_required "journal exists under lock"
    result_print_json
    exit 9
  fi

  # Create journal BEFORE any change - this blocks other operations (stages.sh checks EXIT 9)
  if ! journal_create "$resolved_backup" "restore"; then
    restore_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    restore_error "failed to create restore journal: $JOURNAL_ERROR"
    result_operation_failed "journal creation failed"
    result_print_json
    exit 1
  fi

  local correlation_id
  correlation_id="$(journal_last_correlation_id)"
  if [[ -z "$correlation_id" ]]; then
    correlation_id="$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/uuid 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)"
  fi
  RESULT_CORRELATION_ID="$correlation_id"

  log_info "restore: journal created at $JOURNAL_FILE correlation=$correlation_id" 2>/dev/null || true

  # Now acquire policy lock while still holding state lock (split-locking both)
  if ! journal_acquire_policy_lock 5; then
    # journal remains to block operations - recovery required
    restore_release_state_only
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    restore_error "unable to acquire policy lock after journal creation - journal remains, recovery required: $JOURNAL_ERROR"
    result_recovery_required "policy lock failed after journal creation - journal preserved"
    result_print_json
    exit 9
  fi

  log_info "restore: both locks acquired" 2>/dev/null || true

  # Get current policy paths under locks
  if ! get_persistent_policy_paths; then
    # keep journal - recovery required
    restore_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    restore_error "policy source unavailable under locks: $USBAM_POLICY_ERROR_CODE"
    result_recovery_required "policy source unavailable under lock - journal preserved"
    result_print_json
    exit 9
  fi

  local current_rule_file="" current_rule_folder=""
  current_rule_file="${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFile]:-}"
  current_rule_folder="${USBAM_PERSISTENT_POLICY_PATHS[RuleFolder]:-}"

  # Re-validate backup under locks (TOCTOU protection) and check daemon?
  if ! validate_backup_dir "$resolved_backup"; then
    restore_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    result_validation_failed "backup invalid under lock: $RESTORE_ERROR - journal preserved for recovery"
    result_print_json
    exit 7
  fi

  # Check daemon status - must be running for policy verification but not fatal for file restore?
  if ! check_daemon_status; then
    if [[ "$USBAM_DAEMON_STATUS" != "running" || "$USBAM_IPC_STATUS" != "connected" ]]; then
      log_error "restore: daemon not running or IPC unavailable - proceeding with file restore but policy verification will be limited" 2>/dev/null || true
      # not fatal, but warn
    fi
  fi

  # --- Restore RuleFile ---
  local backup_manifest_rule_name
  backup_manifest_rule_name="$(jq -r '.files.rule_file.backup_name // empty' "$resolved_backup/manifest.json" 2>/dev/null || echo "")"
  local backup_rulefile_src=""
  if [[ -n "$backup_manifest_rule_name" && -f "$resolved_backup/$backup_manifest_rule_name" ]]; then
    backup_rulefile_src="$resolved_backup/$backup_manifest_rule_name"
  else
    # fallback: find first file that is not manifest, ttl, clock_state, and not dir RuleFolder
    local candidate
    for candidate in "$resolved_backup"/*; do
      [[ -f "$candidate" ]] || continue
      local b
      b="$(basename -- "$candidate")"
      case "$b" in
        manifest.json|ttl.json|clock_state.json) continue ;;
        *) backup_rulefile_src="$candidate"; break ;;
      esac
    done
  fi

  if [[ -n "$backup_rulefile_src" && -n "$current_rule_file" ]]; then
    if ! restore_atomic_copy "$backup_rulefile_src" "$current_rule_file"; then
      restore_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      restore_error "RuleFile restore failed - journal preserved"
      result_recovery_required "RuleFile restore failed - journal preserved at $JOURNAL_FILE"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
    log_info "restore: RuleFile restored $backup_rulefile_src -> $current_rule_file" 2>/dev/null || true
  else
    if [[ -n "$current_rule_file" ]]; then
      log_info "restore: no RuleFile in backup, skipping" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
  fi

  # --- Restore RuleFolder ---
  local backup_rulefolder_src="$resolved_backup/RuleFolder"
  if [[ -d "$backup_rulefolder_src" && -n "$current_rule_folder" ]]; then
    if ! restore_atomic_copy_folder "$backup_rulefolder_src" "$current_rule_folder"; then
      restore_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      restore_error "RuleFolder restore failed - journal preserved"
      result_recovery_required "RuleFolder restore failed - journal preserved"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
    log_info "restore: RuleFolder restored $backup_rulefolder_src -> $current_rule_folder" 2>/dev/null || true
  elif [[ -d "$backup_rulefolder_src" ]]; then
    # current system may not have RuleFolder configured but backup does - warn
    log_info "restore: backup contains RuleFolder but current config has no RuleFolder - skipping" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi

  # --- Restore ttl.json atomically via atomic-write.py ---
  if [[ -f "$resolved_backup/ttl.json" ]]; then
    local ttl_content
    if ! ttl_content="$(cat "$resolved_backup/ttl.json")"; then
      restore_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      restore_error "failed to read backup ttl.json"
      result_recovery_required "ttl.json read failed - journal preserved"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
    # Ensure state dir exists
    if ! mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR" 2>/dev/null; then
      restore_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      restore_error "state dir creation failed"
      result_recovery_required "state dir unavailable - journal preserved"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
    chmod 700 "$STATE_DIR" 2>/dev/null || true

    if [[ ! -r "$ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER" ]]; then
      restore_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      restore_error "atomic-write helper missing"
      result_recovery_required "atomic-write helper missing - journal preserved"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi

    if ! python3 "$ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER" --file "$TTL_FILE" --data "$ttl_content"; then
      restore_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      restore_error "atomic write ttl.json failed"
      result_recovery_required "ttl.json atomic write failed - journal preserved"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
    chmod 600 "$TTL_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true
    restore_fsync_dir "$STATE_DIR"

    if ! validate_ttl_json "$TTL_FILE"; then
      restore_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      restore_error "restored ttl.json invalid: $VALIDATORS_ERROR"
      result_recovery_required "restored ttl.json invalid - journal preserved"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
    log_info "restore: ttl.json restored" 2>/dev/null || true
  else
    log_info "restore: backup has no ttl.json, skipping" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi

  # --- Restore clock_state.json atomically if exists ---
  if [[ -f "$resolved_backup/clock_state.json" ]]; then
    local clock_content
    if ! clock_content="$(cat "$resolved_backup/clock_state.json")"; then
      restore_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      restore_error "failed to read backup clock_state.json"
      result_recovery_required "clock_state.json read failed - journal preserved"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
    if ! python3 "$ATOMIC_WRITE_HELPER" --file "$CLOCK_STATE_FILE" --data "$clock_content"; then
      restore_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      restore_error "atomic write clock_state.json failed"
      result_recovery_required "clock_state.json write failed - journal preserved"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
    chmod 600 "$CLOCK_STATE_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true
    restore_fsync_dir "$STATE_DIR"
    log_info "restore: clock_state.json restored" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi

  # Validate final state
  if [[ -f "$TTL_FILE" ]]; then
    if ! validate_ttl_json "$TTL_FILE"; then
      restore_release_locks
      trap - EXIT INT TERM
      restore_error "final ttl.json invalid after restore: $VALIDATORS_ERROR"
      result_recovery_required "final validation failed - journal preserved"
      result_print_json
      exit 9
    fi
  fi

  # Success path - delete journal before releasing locks (journal deletion under locks ensures no race)
  if ! journal_delete "$JOURNAL_FILE"; then
    restore_release_locks
    trap - EXIT INT TERM
    restore_error "failed to delete journal after successful restore - manual deletion required but restore considered successful"
    # We have restored files, but journal deletion failed - this is still recovery_required because journal blocks operations
    result_recovery_required "restore succeeded but journal deletion failed - manual recovery needed"
    result_print_json
    exit 9
  fi

  log_info "restore: journal deleted after successful restore" 2>/dev/null || true

  # Release both locks
  restore_release_locks
  trap - EXIT INT TERM

  # Audit
  local audit_failed=0
  if [[ -r "$AUDIT_WRITER" ]]; then
    if ! python3 "$AUDIT_WRITER" \
      --action "restore" \
      --policy-result "applied" \
      --correlation-id "$correlation_id" \
      --details "restore from $resolved_backup completed"; then
      log_error "restore: audit write failed" 2>/dev/null || true
      audit_failed=1
    fi
  else
    if declare -F log_audit >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      if ! log_audit "restore" "applied" "$correlation_id" "restore from $resolved_backup completed"; then
        audit_failed=1
      fi
    fi
  fi

  if declare -F log_info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    log_info "restore completed from $resolved_backup correlation_id=$correlation_id" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi

  if (( audit_failed )); then
    result_policy_applied_audit_failed "restore completed from $resolved_backup but audit failed, journal removed"
    result_print_json
    exit 14
  fi

  result_success "restore completed from $resolved_backup"
  result_print_json
  exit 0
}

main "$@"
